## Context

Cohezion accumulates rich execution experience (phi_score, 12D FLUME trajectories, coherence
metrics) via JourneyTracker but never uses it to update model weights. Four training pipelines
exist in `flume/` but all generate bash scripts and stop — none execute training. Meanwhile
cloud API costs accumulate on Claude Code / Gemini CLI every session.

The goal is a closed **Cohezion Autonomous Learning Loop (CALL)**: Cohezion's own runtime
experiences become the training signal, AgentJet provides the RL loop, Unsloth provides the
fast fine-tuning backend, and improved local models route away from cloud APIs for familiar
tasks. The Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 with 128GB unified memory is uniquely capable for this — MoE
models like nemotron-3-super (120B total / 12B active) fit entirely in unified memory.

**Critical constraints driving the design:**
- OOM prevention: 128GB is shared CPU+GPU; training + inference models loaded simultaneously
  = crash. Must unload inference models before any training job.
- Context window management: KV cache grows quadratically with context. A 128K context uses
  16x more memory than 32K. Ollama settings + context compression must be first-class.
- AMD training gap: Unsloth Studio AMD training is "coming soon" — Phase 1 uses existing
  llama.cpp/llamafactory path. Unsloth slots in as a drop-in when AMD support ships.

---

## Architecture

```
COHEZION RUNTIME (online)
  CompoundExecutor → JourneyTracker → phi_score + skill_name
  JourneyFinetuneConverter → data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl
       │
       ▼
AGENTJET RL LAYER  (src/cohezion/agentjet/)
  CohezionWorkflow  — wraps CompoundExecutor as AgentJet rollout function
  PhiScoreJudger    — phi_score → scalar reward (phi×2-1 maps [0.5,1]→[0,1])
  JourneyTaskReader — reads existing JSONL (no new data pipeline)
  AgentJetTrainer   — orchestrates RL loop; manages model unload before training
  ContextOptimizer  — per-model Ollama settings (num_ctx, flash_attn, rope_scaling)
       │
       ▼
TRAINING BACKEND (phased)
  Phase 1 (now):    llamafactory + llama.cpp via LocalFinetuner (already exists)
  Phase 2 (soon):   Unsloth Studio QLoRA when AMD training ships
  Both phases:      Unsloth Data Recipes (available NOW) for vault→dataset pipeline
       │
       ▼
OLLAMA LOCAL (deployment)
  GGUF export → ollama create cohezion-{domain}-v{n}
  SmartRouter updated to prefer domain-specialized fine-tuned models
  OllamaContextManager ensures models loaded with correct context settings
```

### context_optimizer.py — ContextOptimizer + OllamaContextManager
Critical component for OOM prevention and context window efficiency.

```python
@dataclass
class ModelContextProfile:
    """Per-model Ollama context settings."""
    model_name: str
    num_ctx: int          # Context window (KV cache driver)
    num_gpu: int          # GPU layers (-1 = all)
    flash_attention: bool # Reduces KV cache ~50%
    rope_scaling: str     # "linear"|"ntk"|"yarn" for extended ctx
    num_parallel: int     # Concurrent requests (default 1 during training)
    keep_alive: str       # "0" during training (unload after use)

CONTEXT_PROFILES: dict[str, ModelContextProfile] = {
    "qwen3.5:0.8b":  ModelContextProfile("qwen3.5:0.8b",  num_ctx=32768, flash_attention=True, ...),
    "qwen3.5:9b":    ModelContextProfile("qwen3.5:9b",    num_ctx=16384, flash_attention=True, ...),
    "nemotron-3-nano:30b": ModelContextProfile(...,       num_ctx=8192,  flash_attention=True, ...),
    # Training profiles have reduced ctx to save KV cache memory during training
}

class OllamaContextManager:
    """Apply context profiles to Ollama models via API.
    Enforces keep_alive=0 before training to free memory.
    Monitors memory pressure (threshold from TierConfig).
    """
    def apply_profile(self, model: str, profile: ModelContextProfile) -> None: ...
    def unload_all_for_training(self) -> None:
        """Unload ALL inference models before starting training job."""
        # Critical OOM prevention: POST /api/generate keep_alive=0 for each loaded model
        ...
    def reload_inference_models(self) -> None:
        """Restore hot/warm tier after training completes."""
        ...
    def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
        """Check available unified memory before training."""
        ...
    def check_oom_risk(self, model_size_gb: float, training_overhead_factor: float = 3.0) -> bool:
        """Return True if loading model for training risks OOM.
        training_overhead_factor=3.0: weights + gradients + optimizer states.
        """
        ...
```

```python
    """Orchestrates the full CALL RL cycle.

    Safety protocol:
    1. Check available memory
    2. Unload ALL inference models (OllamaContextManager.unload_all_for_training)
    3. Run AgentJet RL loop (or llamafactory as Phase 1 fallback)

        dry_run: bool = False,
```

## Context Window Optimization Strategy

Key Ollama environment variables to set/expose as Cohezion config:

```bash
# In .env or cohezion config:
OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION=1       # 50% KV cache reduction — enable always
OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE=q8_0     # Quantized KV cache (further memory reduction)
OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=1          # During training: 1 parallel request max
OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=2     # During training: max 2 models (reduce from 4)
```

Per-model context window sizing rules (to prevent KV cache OOM):

| Scenario | num_ctx | Rationale |
|----------|---------|-----------|
| Routing models (0.8B-2B) | 32768 | Fast, small KV cache |
| Execution models (9B-30B) | 16384 | Balance quality vs memory |
| Deep reasoning (70B+) | 8192 | Prevent OOM at large sizes |
| During QLoRA training | 2048 | Training doesn't need long context |
| Fine-tuned domain models | 8192 | Adequate for specialized tasks |

The `ContextHarness` in `cohezion/reliability/context_harness.py` already does prompt pruning
targeting phi3:mini. Extend it to use per-model `ModelContextProfile` instead of hardcoded target.

---

## OOM Prevention Protocol (Non-Negotiable)

Before ANY training job, `AgentJetTrainer._safety_check()` must:

1. Call `OllamaContextManager.get_available_memory_gb()`
2. Estimate training memory: `model_params_gb × 3.0` (weights + gradients + optimizer)
3. If `available < required × 1.2` (20% headroom): raise `OOMRiskError`, abort
4. Call `OllamaContextManager.unload_all_for_training()`:
   - `POST http://localhost:11434/api/generate` with `keep_alive: 0` for each model
   - Wait for confirmation (poll `/api/ps` until empty)
5. After training: call `reload_inference_models()` in finally block

`DegradationDetector` already monitors `memory_pressure_threshold: 0.80` — wire it to
trigger model eviction before training starts.

## Platform Resource Manager (Cross-Session)

**The problem**: Multiple Claude Code sessions, Gemini CLI sessions, background scripts, and
training jobs all compete for 128GB unified memory with no coordination. Each session's
`model_pool_manager.py` is a singleton that sees only its own allocations. A training job
can OOM the machine while a separate session is serving inference.

**The solution**: A lightweight persistent resource daemon that acts as the platform's
memory arbiter — any session must check in before loading models or starting training.

### Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           RESOURCE DAEMON (persistent)              │
│  Polls Ollama /api/ps every 30s                     │
│  Tracks: loaded_models, reserved_gb, training_locks │
│  Backend: SurrealDB (already running at ws://8000)  │
│  API: HTTP :8765 (lightweight, always-on)           │
└───────────┬────────────────────────┬────────────────┘
            │ reads                  │ reads/writes
  ┌─────────▼──────┐       ┌────────▼────────────┐
  │ Claude Code    │       │ Training Job         │
  │ Session A      │       │ (AgentJetTrainer)    │
  │ ResourceClient │       │ ResourceClient       │
  └────────────────┘       └─────────────────────┘
            │
  ┌─────────▼──────┐
  │ Claude Code    │
  │ Session B      │
  │ ResourceClient │
  └────────────────┘
```

### New Component: src/cohezion/platform/resource_manager.py

```python
class ResourceDaemon:
    """Persistent platform resource arbiter.

    Runs as background process between sessions.
    Polls Ollama /api/ps for ground-truth model state.
    Manages training locks to prevent concurrent training OOM.

    Start: uv run python -m cohezion.platform.resource_manager
    Or via systemd: cohezion-resources.service
    """
    POLL_INTERVAL_S = 30         # Ollama /api/ps poll frequency
    PORT = 8765                  # Local HTTP API port
    TRAINING_LOCK_TTL_S = 7200   # Max training job reservation = 2 hours

    async def poll_ollama_state(self) -> OllamaState:
        """Query /api/ps for actual loaded models + memory usage."""
        ...

    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
        """Total unified memory - Ollama usage - OS overhead - 10GB safety buffer."""
        ...

    async def request_training_lock(
        self, session_id: str, model: str, required_gb: float
    ) -> TrainingLock | None:
        """Exclusive lock for training. Returns None if insufficient memory.
        Locks are stored in SurrealDB with TTL for crash recovery.
        """
        ...

    async def release_training_lock(self, lock_id: str) -> None: ...

    async def register_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """Track active sessions for cleanup on death."""
        ...

    async def cleanup_dead_sessions(self) -> None:
        """Release locks from sessions that died without cleanup."""
        ...


class ResourceClient:
    """Lightweight client for any session to consult the ResourceDaemon.

    Falls back gracefully if daemon not running (single-session mode).
    All sessions MUST use this before loading large models or training.
    """
    DAEMON_URL = "http://localhost:8765"

    async def check_memory(self) -> PlatformMemoryState:
        """Get current memory state across all sessions."""
        ...

    async def acquire_training_lock(
        self, model: str, required_gb: float, timeout_s: float = 30.0
    ) -> TrainingLock | None:
        """Request exclusive training access. Blocks until available or timeout."""
        ...

    async def can_load_model(self, model_name: str, size_gb: float) -> bool:
        """Check if loading a model is safe given all sessions' current usage."""
        ...

    def is_daemon_running(self) -> bool:
        """Silent check — False = fall back to local-only mode."""
        ...


@dataclass
class PlatformMemoryState:
    total_gb: float              # 128 for Ryzen AI MAX+ 395
    ollama_used_gb: float        # From /api/ps (all sessions combined)
    system_reserved_gb: float    # OS + non-Ollama processes
    training_reserved_gb: float  # Active training lock reservations
    available_gb: float          # total - ollama_used - system_reserved - training_reserved
    safety_buffer_gb: float = 10.0  # Never let available drop below this
    loaded_models: list[str]     # All models currently loaded across ALL sessions


@dataclass
class TrainingLock:
    lock_id: str
    session_id: str
    model: str
    reserved_gb: float
    acquired_at: float
    expires_at: float            # TTL for crash recovery
```

### Session Usage Pattern

Every new Claude Code / Gemini CLI session that uses Cohezion:

```python
# On session start (in CompoundExecutor or via hook)
client = ResourceClient()
await client.register_session(session_id=os.environ["COHEZION_SESSION_ID"])

# Before any model load
if not await client.can_load_model("qwen3-coder-next", size_gb=45.0):
    logger.warning("Insufficient platform memory, falling back to nemotron-3-nano:30b")

# Before training (in AgentJetTrainer)
lock = await client.acquire_training_lock("qwen3.5:9b", required_gb=27.0)
if lock is None:
    raise ResourceUnavailableError("Training blocked: another session holds training lock")
try:
    await self._run_training(...)
finally:
    await client.release_training_lock(lock.lock_id)
```

### Usage Pattern Learning

The daemon tracks which models each session uses and how long, building a usage histogram
stored in SurrealDB. This feeds back into `model_pool_config.py` tier assignments:

```python
# Weekly: daemon promotes/demotes models based on actual cross-session usage
# "nemotron-3-nano is used by 80% of sessions for >30min → promote to WARM"
# "qwen3-coder-next last used 14 days ago → demote to COLD"
class TierOptimizer:
    def recommend_tier_changes(self, usage_history: list[SessionRecord]) -> list[TierChange]:
        """Data-driven tier assignment based on actual cross-session usage."""
        ...
```

### Integration into AgentJetTrainer

`AgentJetTrainer._safety_check()` becomes:

```python
async def _safety_check(self, model: str, required_gb: float) -> None:
    client = ResourceClient()
    if client.is_daemon_running():
        lock = await client.acquire_training_lock(model, required_gb, timeout_s=30.0)
        if lock is None:
            raise ResourceUnavailableError(
                f"Cannot train {model}: platform has insufficient memory "
                f"or another session holds the training lock"
            )
        self._training_lock = lock  # Released in finally block of train()
    else:
        # Fallback: local-only check (single-session safety)
        state = await OllamaContextManager().get_available_memory_gb()
        if state < required_gb * 1.2:
            raise OOMRiskError(f"OOM risk: need {required_gb}GB, have {state}GB")
```

## Gemini Embedding 2 — Orchestration Anchor

Gemini Embedding 2 (March 10, 2026) is Google's first natively multimodal embedding model:
maps text, images, video, audio, and documents into ONE unified embedding space.

This upgrades Cohezion's orchestration from keyword matching → semantic concept matching.

### Why It Matters

`SemanticCache` already has a plug-and-play `EmbeddingModel` interface. The current
`FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel` uses a 256D SHA-256 hash projection — not truly semantic. Gemini
Embedding 2 slots directly into the same interface as a higher-quality tier.

### Three Uses (in priority order)

**1. Vault Indexing (offline, one-time cost)**
Embed every vault note, decision, pattern, and PRIME skill using Gemini Embedding 2.
Store vectors locally in SurrealDB. Never pay again for the same content.
This makes `vault_find_relevant_context()` orders of magnitude more accurate.

**2. FLUME VAE Distillation (calibration, not runtime)**
Use Gemini Embedding 2 vectors as teacher signal to train the FLUME VAE.
The VAE learns to approximate Gemini's embedding quality locally.
After calibration: near-cloud quality at zero API cost per runtime query.
This is knowledge distillation for embeddings — same principle as DistilBERT vs BERT.

**3. Cross-Modal Orchestration (when cloud available)**
Task descriptions, code snippets, trajectory data, and skill definitions all embedded
into the same space. SmartRouter semantic matching across modalities, not just text.

### New Component: src/cohezion/agentjet/embeddings.py

```python
class GeminiEmbeddingModel(EmbeddingModel):
    """Cloud Gemini Embedding 2 — uses Gemini API, caches locally.

    Never embeds the same content twice. Stores in SurrealDB with content hash key.
    Falls back to FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel when cloud unavailable.
    """
    def __init__(self, cache_backend: SurrealClient, fallback: EmbeddingModel): ...
    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
        key = sha256(text)
        if cached := await self.cache_backend.get(key): return cached
        result = await self._call_gemini_api(text)
        await self.cache_backend.store(key, result)
        return result

class EmbeddingDistiller:
    """Distills Gemini Embedding 2 quality into local FLUME VAE.

    Generates Gemini embeddings for vault corpus, then fine-tunes FLUME VAE
    to minimize distance to Gemini vectors. Result: local embeddings that
    approximate cloud quality without runtime API calls.
    """
    def distill(self, vault_corpus: list[str], epochs: int = 10) -> Path: ...
    # Outputs calibrated FLUME VAE checkpoint

class EmbeddingOrchestrator:
    """Selects embedding model based on context and availability.

    Routing table:
    - Vault indexing (offline):    GeminiEmbeddingModel (cloud, cached)
    - Runtime inference (online):  FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel (local, calibrated)
    - Training reward signals:     GeminiEmbeddingModel (offline, for distillation)
    - Fast routing (hot path):     DistilledEmbeddingModel (hash fallback, <1ms)
    """
    def get_model(self, context: str) -> EmbeddingModel: ...
```

### Integration Points

- `SemanticCache.__init__`: Accept `EmbeddingOrchestrator` as `embedding_model` param
  (already supports custom `embedding_model` injection — zero interface change)
- `TrajectorySearchEngine.find_similar_trajectories()`: Replace hash embeddings with
  calibrated FLUME VAE (post-distillation) for better trajectory matching
- `vault_find_relevant_context()`: Use GeminiEmbeddingModel for vault indexing pass
- `SkillSelector.select_skills()`: Use calibrated embeddings for semantic skill matching

### Cost Control

Gemini Embedding 2 is API-based (cloud). Usage rules:
- ONLY call for novel content (not seen before, per SurrealDB content-hash cache)
- NEVER call during runtime execution (FLUME VAE handles runtime)
- Batch vault indexing runs offline (configurable schedule, not per-request)
- Circuit breaker: if Gemini API unavailable, fall back to FLUME VAE silently

---

| Existing | Used By | How |
|----------|---------|-----|
| `flume/journey_finetune_pipeline.py:JourneyFinetuneConverter` | `JourneyTaskReader` | Reads its JSONL output |
| `flume/local_finetune_pipeline.py:LocalFinetuner` | `AgentJetTrainer` | Phase 1 training backend |
| `compound/journey_tracker.py:phi_score` | `PhiScoreJudger` | Already computed, just read |
| `compound/executor.py:CompoundExecutor` | `CohezionWorkflow` | Wrapped as AgentJet rollout |
| `reliability/context_harness.py:ContextHarness` | `OllamaContextManager` | Extended for per-model profiles |
| `swarm/model_pool_config.py:TierConfig` | `OllamaContextManager` | `memory_pressure_threshold` |
| `swarm/dynamic_model_router.py` | `AgentJetTrainer` | Concurrency limit enforcement |

| `persistence/surreal_client.py` | `ResourceDaemon`, `ResourceClient` | Training lock storage + TTL |
| `swarm/model_pool_manager.py` | `ResourceDaemon` | Per-session model state (feeds daemon) |
| `swarm/model_pool_config.py:TierConfig` | `TierOptimizer` | Reads current tiers, writes recommendations |

```
     1→"""AgentJet RL training orchestrator for the CALL loop."""
     2→from __future__ import annotations
     3→
     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→import time
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Literal
    10→
    11→from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager
    12→from cohezion.agentjet.judger import PhiScoreJudger
    13→from cohezion.agentjet.task_reader import JourneyTaskReader
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from cohezion.agentjet.workflow import CohezionWorkflow
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class TrainingResult:
    23→    """Result of a single AgentJet CALL training cycle."""
    24→
    25→    success: bool
    26→    model_name: str           # Final Ollama model name after training
    27→    base_model: str           # Model that was fine-tuned
    28→    skill_domain: str | None  # Which skill domain was trained
    29→    epochs_completed: int
    30→    samples_used: int
    31→    avg_reward: float         # Average reward from PhiScoreJudger
    32→    training_duration_s: float
    33→    output_path: Path | None  # Path to GGUF or config script
    34→    error: str | None = None  # Error message if success=False
    35→    dry_run: bool = False
    36→
    37→
    38→class AgentJetTrainer:
    39→    """Orchestrates the full CALL RL cycle with OOM-safe training.
    40→
    41→    Safety protocol (enforced in train()):
    42→    1. Check available memory via ResourceClient or OllamaContextManager.
    43→    2. Unload ALL inference models.
    44→    3. Run training backend (llamafactory Phase 1, or agentjet Phase 3).
    45→    4. Export GGUF → ollama create cohezion-{domain}-v{n}.
    46→    5. Reload inference models.
    47→    6. Update SmartRouter with new model name.
    48→    All steps run inside try/finally so reload always happens.
    49→
    50→    Parameters
    51→    ----------
    52→    workflow : CohezionWorkflow, optional
    53→        Workflow instance for generating rollouts. Created lazily if not provided.
    54→    judger : PhiScoreJudger, optional
    55→        Reward judger. Defaults to PhiScoreJudger().
    56→    reader : JourneyTaskReader, optional
    57→        Task loader. Defaults to JourneyTaskReader().
    58→    context_manager : OllamaContextManager, optional
    59→        Manages Ollama model lifecycle. Defaults to OllamaContextManager().
    60→    backend : Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"]
    61→        Training backend to use. "llamafactory" uses LocalFinetuner (Phase 1).
    62→        "agentjet" targets verl/PPO (Phase 3, not yet implemented).
    63→    """
    64→
    65→    def __init__(
    66→        self,
    67→        workflow: CohezionWorkflow | None = None,
    68→        judger: PhiScoreJudger | None = None,
    69→        reader: JourneyTaskReader | None = None,
    70→        context_manager: OllamaContextManager | None = None,
    71→        backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = "llamafactory",
    72→    ) -> None:
    73→        self._workflow = workflow  # Lazily resolved; not required for training
    74→        self.judger = judger if judger is not None else PhiScoreJudger()
    75→        self.reader = reader if reader is not None else JourneyTaskReader()
    76→        self.context_manager = (
    77→            context_manager if context_manager is not None else OllamaContextManager()
    78→        )
    79→        self.backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = backend
    80→        self._training_lock: object | None = None  # TrainingLock from ResourceClient
    81→
    82→    async def train(
    83→        self,
    84→        target_model: str = "qwen3.5:9b",
    85→        skill_domain: str | None = None,
    86→        epochs: int = 3,
    87→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    88→        dry_run: bool = False,
    89→    ) -> TrainingResult:
    90→        """Run the full CALL cycle: load data → safety check → train → reload.
    91→
    92→        Parameters
    93→        ----------
    94→        target_model : str
    95→            Base Ollama model to fine-tune.
    96→        skill_domain : str, optional
    97→            Filter training data to this skill domain. None means all domains.
    98→        epochs : int
    99→            Number of training epochs to pass to the backend.
   100→        min_phi : float
   101→            Minimum phi_score threshold for including a task in the training set.
   102→        dry_run : bool
   103→            If True, skip actual training and model unload/reload. Useful for
   104→            validating the pipeline without touching Ollama.
   105→
   106→        Returns
   107→        -------
   108→        TrainingResult
   109→            Describes the outcome of the training run.
   110→        """
   111→        start_time = time.monotonic()
   112→
   113→        # 1. Load training data
   114→        tasks = self.reader.read(skill_filter=skill_domain, min_phi=min_phi)
   115→        if not tasks:
   116→            logger.warning(
   117→                "AgentJetTrainer.train: no tasks matched filters "
   118→                "(skill_domain=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   119→                skill_domain,
   120→                min_phi,
   121→            )
   122→            return TrainingResult(
   123→                success=False,
   124→                model_name="",
   125→                base_model=target_model,
   126→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   127→                epochs_completed=0,
   128→                samples_used=0,
   129→                avg_reward=0.0,
   130→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   131→                output_path=None,
   132→                error="No training data matched filters",
   133→                dry_run=dry_run,
   134→            )
   135→
   136→        # 2. OOM safety check — acquires lock or raises OOMRiskError
   137→        await self._safety_check(target_model)
   138→
   139→        output_path: Path | None = None
   140→        try:
   141→            # 3. Unload all inference models before training
   142→            if not dry_run:
   143→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: unloading inference models for training")
   144→                await self.context_manager.unload_all_for_training()
   145→
   146→            # 4. Compute rewards for dataset quality metrics
   147→            rewards = self.judger.batch_judge(tasks)
   148→            avg_reward = sum(rewards) / len(rewards) if rewards else 0.0
   149→            logger.info(
   150→                "AgentJetTrainer: %d tasks, avg_reward=%.3f",
   151→                len(tasks),
   152→                avg_reward,
   153→            )
   154→
   155→            # 5. Run training backend
   156→            if not dry_run:
   157→                output_path = await self._run_training(
   158→                    target_model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs
   159→                )
   160→
   161→            # 6. Build final model name
   162→            domain = skill_domain or "general"
   163→            version = int(time.time()) % 10000
   164→            final_model_name = f"cohezion-{domain}-v{version}"
   165→
   166→            logger.info(
   167→                "AgentJetTrainer: training complete — model=%s output=%s",
   168→                final_model_name,
   169→                output_path,
   170→            )
   171→            return TrainingResult(
   172→                success=True,
   173→                model_name=final_model_name,
   174→                base_model=target_model,
   175→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   176→                epochs_completed=epochs,
   177→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   178→                avg_reward=avg_reward,
   179→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   180→                output_path=output_path,
   181→                dry_run=dry_run,
   182→            )
   183→
   184→        except Exception as exc:
   185→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer.train failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   186→            return TrainingResult(
   187→                success=False,
   188→                model_name="",
   189→                base_model=target_model,
   190→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   191→                epochs_completed=0,
   192→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   193→                avg_reward=0.0,
   194→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   195→                output_path=None,
   196→                error=str(exc),
   197→                dry_run=dry_run,
   198→            )
   199→
   200→        finally:
   201→            # Always reload inference models — even if training failed
   202→            if not dry_run:
   203→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: reloading inference models")
   204→                try:
   205→                    await self.context_manager.reload_inference_models()
   206→                except Exception as reload_exc:
   207→                    logger.warning(
   208→                        "Inference model reload failed (non-fatal): %s", reload_exc
   209→                    )
   210→
   211→            # Release training lock regardless of outcome
   212→            if self._training_lock is not None:
   213→                try:
   214→                    from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import ResourceClient
   215→
   216→                    client = ResourceClient()
   217→                    lock_id: str = self._training_lock.lock_id  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   218→                    await client.release_training_lock(lock_id)
   219→                    logger.debug("AgentJetTrainer: released training lock %s", lock_id)
   220→                except Exception as lock_exc:
   221→                    logger.warning(
   222→                        "Training lock release failed (non-fatal): %s", lock_exc
   223→                    )
   224→                finally:
   225→                    self._training_lock = None
   226→
   227→    async def _safety_check(self, model: str) -> None:
   228→        """OOM check before training begins.
   229→
   230→        Tries the platform ResourceClient daemon first (cross-session coordination).
   231→        Falls back to a local Ollama memory estimate when the daemon is unavailable.
   232→
   233→        Raises
   234→        ------
   235→        OOMRiskError
   236→            When local memory check indicates insufficient headroom.
   237→        ResourceUnavailableError
   238→            When the platform daemon explicitly denies the training lock.
   239→        """
   240→        profile = self.context_manager.get_profile(model)
   241→        required_gb = profile.size_gb * 3.0  # Training needs ~3x model size
   242→
   243→        # Attempt platform daemon coordination first
   244→        try:
   245→            from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import (
   246→                ResourceClient,
   247→                ResourceUnavailableError,
   248→            )
   249→
   250→            client = ResourceClient()
   251→            if client.is_daemon_running():
   252→                lock = await client.acquire_training_lock(
   253→                    model, required_gb, timeout_s=30.0
   254→                )
   255→                if lock is None:
   256→                    raise ResourceUnavailableError(
   257→                        f"Platform daemon denied training lock for {model} "
   258→                        f"(need {required_gb:.1f} GiB)"
   259→                    )
   260→                self._training_lock = lock
   261→                logger.info(
   262→                    "AgentJetTrainer: acquired training lock %s (%.1f GiB reserved)",
   263→                    lock.lock_id,
   264→                    required_gb,
   265→                )
   266→                return
   267→        except ImportError:
   268→            logger.debug(
   269→                "cohezion.platform.resource_manager unavailable; using local OOM check"
   270→            )
   271→
   272→        # Fallback: local available memory estimate
   273→        available = await self.context_manager.get_available_memory_gb()
   274→        headroom_required = required_gb * 1.2
   275→        if available < headroom_required:
   276→            from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager  # noqa: F401
   277→
   278→            # Import OOMRiskError from resource_manager if available, else define locally
   279→            try:
   280→                from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import OOMRiskError
   281→            except ImportError:
   282→
   283→                class OOMRiskError(RuntimeError):  # type: ignore[no-redef]
   284→                    pass
   285→
   286→            raise OOMRiskError(
   287→                f"OOM risk: need {headroom_required:.1f} GiB (with 20% headroom), "
   288→                f"have {available:.1f} GiB available"
   289→            )
   290→
   291→        logger.info(
   292→            "AgentJetTrainer: local OOM check passed (need %.1f GiB, have %.1f GiB)",
   293→            headroom_required,
   294→            available,
   295→        )
   296→
   297→    async def _run_training(
   298→        self,
   299→        model: str,
   300→        tasks: list[dict],
   301→        skill_domain: str | None,
   302→        epochs: int,
   303→    ) -> Path | None:
   304→        """Dispatch to the configured training backend.
   305→
   306→        Parameters
   307→        ----------
   308→        model : str
   309→            Base model identifier.
   310→        tasks : list[dict]
   311→            Normalised task dicts from JourneyTaskReader.
   312→        skill_domain : str, optional
   313→            Skill domain being trained (used for output naming).
   314→        epochs : int
   315→            Number of training epochs.
   316→
   317→        Returns
   318→        -------
   319→        Path | None
   320→            Path to generated training config/script, or None on failure.
   321→        """
   322→        if self.backend == "llamafactory":
   323→            return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   324→        if self.backend == "agentjet":
   325→            return await self._run_agentjet_rl(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   326→        logger.warning("Unknown backend %r; falling back to llamafactory", self.backend)
   327→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   328→
   329→    async def _run_llamafactory(
   330→        self,
   331→        model: str,
   332→        tasks: list[dict],
   333→        skill_domain: str | None,
   334→        epochs: int,
   335→    ) -> Path | None:
   336→        """Phase 1: generate a llamafactory training config via LocalFinetuner.
   337→
   338→        Writes tasks to the standard JSONL location, then calls
   339→        LocalFinetuner.run_qlora_training() which produces a bash script / config.
   340→
   341→        Returns the path to the generated config file, or None on error.
   342→        """
   343→        try:
   344→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   345→
   346→            output_name = f"cohezion_{skill_domain or 'general'}_v{int(time.time()) % 10000}"
   347→
   348→            # Map full Ollama model names to LocalFinetuner's known base keys
   349→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   350→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   351→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   352→                "phi4": "phi4",
   353→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   354→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   355→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   356→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   357→            }
   358→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   359→
   360→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name=output_name)
   361→
   362→            # Ensure training data directory and JSONL exist for LocalFinetuner
   363→            import json
   364→
   365→            data_dir = Path("data/training")
   366→            data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   367→            journey_path = data_dir / "finetune_journeys.jsonl"
   368→
   369→            if not journey_path.exists():
   370→                logger.info(
   371→                    "AgentJetTrainer: writing %d tasks to %s for LocalFinetuner",
   372→                    len(tasks),
   373→                    journey_path,
   374→                )
   375→                with journey_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   376→                    for task in tasks:
   377→                        fh.write(json.dumps(task) + "\n")
   378→
   379→            # Run in executor — LocalFinetuner is synchronous
   380→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   381→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   382→                None,
   383→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   384→            )
   385→            logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: llamafactory config generated: %s", config_path)
   386→            return config_path
   387→
   388→        except Exception as exc:
   389→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer._run_llamafactory failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   390→            return None
   391→
   392→    async def _run_agentjet_rl(
   393→        self,
   394→        model: str,
   395→        tasks: list[dict],
   396→        skill_domain: str | None,
   397→        epochs: int,
   398→    ) -> Path | None:
   399→        """Phase 3: AgentJet verl/PPO RL training.
   400→
   401→        Not yet implemented (AMD ROCm support for verl is pending).
   402→        Falls back to llamafactory until Phase 3 ships.
   403→        """
   404→        logger.warning(
   405→            "AgentJet RL backend (verl/PPO) is not yet implemented for AMD. "
   406→            "Falling back to llamafactory."
   407→        )
   408→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The trainer uses a "safety first" fallback pattern: it tries to coordinate with the platform ResourceDaemon via HTTP first (cross-session lock), then falls back to a local Ollama memory poll. This layered approach is critical for multi-session OOM prevention — the daemon gives global visibility that individual sessions can't have.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```
     1→"""Platform resource arbiter for cross-session memory coordination."""
     2→from __future__ import annotations
     3→
     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→import time
     7→import uuid
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→import aiohttp
    12→from aiohttp import web
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→# Errors
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→
    22→class ResourceUnavailableError(RuntimeError):
    23→    """Raised when a requested resource cannot be acquired."""
    24→
    25→
    26→class OOMRiskError(ResourceUnavailableError):
    27→    """Raised when allocating memory would risk OOM on the host."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→# Data models
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass
    36→class PlatformMemoryState:
    37→    """Snapshot of host memory allocation across all sessions.
    38→
    39→    Values are in GiB. Reflects the Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 with 128 GiB unified RAM.
    40→    """
    41→
    42→    total_gb: float = 128.0  # Ryzen AI MAX+ 395
    43→    ollama_used_gb: float = 0.0
    44→    system_reserved_gb: float = 8.0  # OS overhead
    45→    training_reserved_gb: float = 0.0
    46→    safety_buffer_gb: float = 10.0
    47→    loaded_models: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    48→
    49→    @property
    50→    def available_gb(self) -> float:
    51→        """GiB available for new allocations."""
    52→        return (
    53→            self.total_gb
    54→            - self.ollama_used_gb
    55→            - self.system_reserved_gb
    56→            - self.training_reserved_gb
    57→            - self.safety_buffer_gb
    58→        )
    59→
    60→
    61→@dataclass
    62→class TrainingLock:
    63→    """Exclusive reservation for a training workload."""
    64→
    65→    lock_id: str
    66→    session_id: str
    67→    model: str
    68→    reserved_gb: float
    69→    acquired_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    70→    expires_at: float = 0.0  # Set by caller to acquired_at + TTL
    71→
    72→    def is_expired(self) -> bool:
    73→        return self.expires_at > 0 and time.time() > self.expires_at
    74→
    75→
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→# ResourceClient — used by callers to talk to the daemon (or fall back locally)
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→
    80→
    81→class ResourceClient:
    82→    """Thin HTTP client that delegates resource decisions to ResourceDaemon.
    83→
    84→    Falls back to a local Ollama poll when the daemon is not running so that
    85→    callers remain functional in development without the daemon process.
    86→    """
    87→
    88→    DAEMON_URL = "http://localhost:8765"
    89→    _OLLAMA_PS_URL = "http://localhost:11434/api/ps"
    90→
    91→    def is_daemon_running(self) -> bool:
    92→        """Return True if the daemon health endpoint responds."""
    93→        import socket
    94→
    95→        try:
    96→            conn = socket.create_connection(("localhost", 8765), timeout=1)
    97→            conn.close()
    98→            return True
    99→        except OSError:
   100→            return False
   101→
   102→    async def check_memory(self) -> PlatformMemoryState:
   103→        """Return current memory state from daemon or local Ollama poll."""
   104→        if self.is_daemon_running():
   105→            try:
   106→                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   107→                    async with session.get(
   108→                        f"{self.DAEMON_URL}/memory", timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=5)
   109→                    ) as resp:
   110→                        data = await resp.json()
   111→                        return PlatformMemoryState(
   112→                            total_gb=data.get("total_gb", 128.0),
   113→                            ollama_used_gb=data.get("ollama_used_gb", 0.0),
   114→                            system_reserved_gb=data.get("system_reserved_gb", 8.0),
   115→                            training_reserved_gb=data.get("training_reserved_gb", 0.0),
   116→                            safety_buffer_gb=data.get("safety_buffer_gb", 10.0),
   117→                            loaded_models=data.get("loaded_models", []),
   118→                        )
   119→            except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   120→                logger.warning("Daemon unreachable, falling back to local poll: %s", exc)
   121→
   122→        return await self._poll_ollama_locally()
   123→
   124→    async def _poll_ollama_locally(self) -> PlatformMemoryState:
   125→        """Poll Ollama /api/ps directly and build a best-effort memory state."""
   126→        state = PlatformMemoryState()
   127→        try:
   128→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   129→                async with session.get(
   130→                    self._OLLAMA_PS_URL, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=5)
   131→                ) as resp:
   132→                    data = await resp.json()
   133→                    models = data.get("models", [])
   134→                    state.loaded_models = [m.get("name", "") for m in models]
   135→                    state.ollama_used_gb = sum(
   136→                        m.get("size", 0) / (1024**3) for m in models
   137→                    )
   138→        except (aiohttp.ClientError, Exception) as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   139→            logger.debug("Could not reach Ollama: %s", exc)
   140→        return state
   141→
   142→    async def acquire_training_lock(
   143→        self,
   144→        model: str,
   145→        required_gb: float,
   146→        timeout_s: float = 30.0,
   147→    ) -> TrainingLock | None:
   148→        """Request an exclusive training lock, returning None if unavailable."""
   149→        if self.is_daemon_running():
   150→            try:
   151→                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   152→                    payload = {
   153→                        "model": model,
   154→                        "required_gb": required_gb,
   155→                        "session_id": _current_session_id(),
   156→                    }
   157→                    async with session.post(
   158→                        f"{self.DAEMON_URL}/locks",
   159→                        json=payload,
   160→                        timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=timeout_s),
   161→                    ) as resp:
   162→                        if resp.status == 200:
   163→                            data = await resp.json()
   164→                            lock = TrainingLock(
   165→                                lock_id=data["lock_id"],
   166→                                session_id=data["session_id"],
   167→                                model=data["model"],
   168→                                reserved_gb=data["reserved_gb"],
   169→                                acquired_at=data["acquired_at"],
   170→                                expires_at=data["expires_at"],
   171→                            )
   172→                            return lock
   173→                        return None
   174→            except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   175→                logger.warning("Daemon lock request failed, using local check: %s", exc)
   176→
   177→        # Fallback: local memory check
   178→        state = await self._poll_ollama_locally()
   179→        if state.available_gb < required_gb * 1.2:
   180→            raise OOMRiskError(
   181→                f"Insufficient memory: need {required_gb * 1.2:.1f} GiB, "
   182→                f"have {state.available_gb:.1f} GiB"
   183→            )
   184→        now = time.time()
   185→        return TrainingLock(
   186→            lock_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
   187→            session_id=_current_session_id(),
   188→            model=model,
   189→            reserved_gb=required_gb,
   190→            acquired_at=now,
   191→            expires_at=now + ResourceDaemon.TRAINING_LOCK_TTL_S,
   192→        )
   193→
   194→    async def release_training_lock(self, lock_id: str) -> None:
   195→        """Release a previously acquired training lock."""
   196→        if not self.is_daemon_running():
   197→            logger.debug("Daemon not running; local lock %s released (no-op)", lock_id)
   198→            return
   199→        try:
   200→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   201→                async with session.delete(
   202→                    f"{self.DAEMON_URL}/locks/{lock_id}",
   203→                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=5),
   204→                ) as resp:
   205→                    if resp.status not in (200, 204):
   206→                        logger.warning("Lock release returned HTTP %s", resp.status)
   207→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   208→            logger.warning("Could not release lock %s: %s", lock_id, exc)
   209→
   210→    async def can_load_model(self, model_name: str, size_gb: float) -> bool:
   211→        """Return True if there is enough memory to load the model safely."""
   212→        state = await self.check_memory()
   213→        return state.available_gb > size_gb * 1.2
   214→
   215→    async def register_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
   216→        """Notify the daemon that a new session is starting."""
   217→        if not self.is_daemon_running():
   218→            return
   219→        try:
   220→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   221→                await session.post(
   222→                    f"{self.DAEMON_URL}/sessions",
   223→                    json={"session_id": session_id},
   224→                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=5),
   225→                )
   226→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   227→            logger.debug("Could not register session: %s", exc)
   228→
   229→
   230→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   231→# ResourceDaemon — long-running process that arbitrates locks across sessions
   232→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→
   234→
   235→class ResourceDaemon:
   236→    """HTTP server that provides cross-session resource arbitration.
   237→
   238→    Run via: python -m cohezion.platform.resource_manager
   239→    """
   240→
   241→    PORT = 8765
   242→    POLL_INTERVAL_S = 30
   243→    TRAINING_LOCK_TTL_S = 7200  # 2 hours
   244→
   245→    _OLLAMA_PS_URL = "http://localhost:11434/api/ps"
   246→
   247→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   248→        self._memory_state = PlatformMemoryState()
   249→        self._locks: dict[str, TrainingLock] = {}
   250→        self._sessions: set[str] = set()
   251→        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
   252→
   253→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   254→    # Ollama polling
   255→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→
   257→    async def poll_ollama_state(self) -> PlatformMemoryState:
   258→        """Fetch /api/ps from Ollama and return a PlatformMemoryState."""
   259→        state = PlatformMemoryState()
   260→        try:
   261→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   262→                async with session.get(
   263→                    self._OLLAMA_PS_URL, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10)
   264→                ) as resp:
   265→                    data = await resp.json()
   266→                    models = data.get("models", [])
   267→                    state.loaded_models = [m.get("name", "") for m in models]
   268→                    state.ollama_used_gb = sum(
   269→                        m.get("size", 0) / (1024**3) for m in models
   270→                    )
   271→                    # training_reserved_gb is computed from active locks
   272→                    async with self._lock:
   273→                        state.training_reserved_gb = sum(
   274→                            lk.reserved_gb for lk in self._locks.values()
   275→                            if not lk.is_expired()
   276→                        )
   277→        except (aiohttp.ClientError, Exception) as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   278→            logger.debug("Ollama poll failed: %s", exc)
   279→        return state
   280→
   281→    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
   282→        """Return currently available GiB."""
   283→        state = await self.poll_ollama_state()
   284→        return state.available_gb
   285→
   286→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   287→    # Lock management
   288→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   289→
   290→    async def request_training_lock(
   291→        self,
   292→        session_id: str,
   293→        model: str,
   294→        required_gb: float,
   295→    ) -> TrainingLock | None:
   296→        """Grant a training lock if sufficient memory is available."""
   297→        async with self._lock:
   298→            await self.cleanup_dead_sessions()
   299→            available = self._memory_state.available_gb
   300→            if available < required_gb * 1.2:
   301→                logger.warning(
   302→                    "Lock denied for %s/%s: need %.1f GiB, available %.1f GiB",
   303→                    session_id, model, required_gb * 1.2, available,
   304→                )
   305→                return None
   306→
   307→            now = time.time()
   308→            lock = TrainingLock(
   309→                lock_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
   310→                session_id=session_id,
   311→                model=model,
   312→                reserved_gb=required_gb,
   313→                acquired_at=now,
   314→                expires_at=now + self.TRAINING_LOCK_TTL_S,
   315→            )
   316→            self._locks[lock.lock_id] = lock
   317→            self._memory_state.training_reserved_gb += required_gb
   318→            logger.info("Granted lock %s for %s (%.1f GiB)", lock.lock_id, model, required_gb)
   319→            return lock
   320→
   321→    async def release_training_lock(self, lock_id: str) -> None:
   322→        """Remove a lock and free the reserved memory."""
   323→        async with self._lock:
   324→            lock = self._locks.pop(lock_id, None)
   325→            if lock:
   326→                self._memory_state.training_reserved_gb = max(
   327→                    0.0, self._memory_state.training_reserved_gb - lock.reserved_gb
   328→                )
   329→                logger.info("Released lock %s", lock_id)
   330→
   331→    async def cleanup_dead_sessions(self) -> None:
   332→        """Remove expired locks (called while holding self._lock)."""
   333→        expired = [lid for lid, lk in self._locks.items() if lk.is_expired()]
   334→        for lid in expired:
   335→            lock = self._locks.pop(lid)
   336→            self._memory_state.training_reserved_gb = max(
   337→                0.0, self._memory_state.training_reserved_gb - lock.reserved_gb
   338→            )
   339→            logger.info("Expired lock %s for session %s", lid, lock.session_id)
   340→
   341→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   342→    # aiohttp request handlers
   343→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   344→
   345→    async def _handle_health(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
   346→        return web.json_response({"status": "ok"})
   347→
   348→    async def _handle_get_memory(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
   349→        state = await self.poll_ollama_state()
   350→        async with self._lock:
   351→            self._memory_state = state
   352→        return web.json_response({
   353→            "total_gb": state.total_gb,
   354→            "ollama_used_gb": state.ollama_used_gb,
   355→            "system_reserved_gb": state.system_reserved_gb,
   356→            "training_reserved_gb": state.training_reserved_gb,
   357→            "safety_buffer_gb": state.safety_buffer_gb,
   358→            "loaded_models": state.loaded_models,
   359→            "available_gb": state.available_gb,
   360→        })
   361→
   362→    async def _handle_post_lock(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
   363→        body: dict[str, Any] = await request.json()
   364→        session_id = body.get("session_id", "unknown")
   365→        model = body.get("model", "")
   366→        required_gb = float(body.get("required_gb", 0))
   367→
   368→        lock = await self.request_training_lock(session_id, model, required_gb)
   369→        if lock is None:
   370→            return web.json_response({"error": "insufficient_memory"}, status=503)
   371→        return web.json_response({
   372→            "lock_id": lock.lock_id,
   373→            "session_id": lock.session_id,
   374→            "model": lock.model,
   375→            "reserved_gb": lock.reserved_gb,
   376→            "acquired_at": lock.acquired_at,
   377→            "expires_at": lock.expires_at,
   378→        })
   379→
   380→    async def _handle_delete_lock(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
   381→        lock_id = request.match_info["lock_id"]
   382→        await self.release_training_lock(lock_id)
   383→        return web.Response(status=204)
   384→
   385→    async def _handle_post_session(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
   386→        body: dict[str, Any] = await request.json()
   387→        session_id = body.get("session_id", "")
   388→        if session_id:
   389→            self._sessions.add(session_id)
   390→            logger.info("Registered session %s", session_id)
   391→        return web.json_response({"ok": True})
   392→
   393→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   394→    # Startup
   395→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   396→
   397→    async def _poll_loop(self) -> None:
   398→        while True:
   399→            await asyncio.sleep(self.POLL_INTERVAL_S)
   400→            try:
   401→                state = await self.poll_ollama_state()
   402→                async with self._lock:
   403→                    self._memory_state = state
   404→                    await self.cleanup_dead_sessions()
   405→            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   406→                logger.warning("Poll loop error: %s", exc)
   407→
   408→    async def start(self) -> None:
   409→        """Run the aiohttp server and background poll loop."""
   410→        app = web.Application()
   411→        app.router.add_get("/health", self._handle_health)
   412→        app.router.add_get("/memory", self._handle_get_memory)
   413→        app.router.add_post("/locks", self._handle_post_lock)
   414→        app.router.add_delete("/locks/{lock_id}", self._handle_delete_lock)
   415→        app.router.add_post("/sessions", self._handle_post_session)
   416→
   417→        runner = web.AppRunner(app)
   418→        await runner.setup()
   419→        site = web.TCPSite(runner, "localhost", self.PORT)
   420→        await site.start()
   421→        logger.info("ResourceDaemon listening on port %d", self.PORT)
   422→
   423→        # Initial poll
   424→        state = await self.poll_ollama_state()
   425→        async with self._lock:
   426→            self._memory_state = state
   427→
   428→        await self._poll_loop()
   429→
   430→
   431→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   432→# Helpers
   433→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   434→
   435→
   436→def _current_session_id() -> str:
   437→    """Return a stable session identifier for the current process."""
   438→    import os
   439→
   440→    return os.environ.get("COHEZION_SESSION_ID", f"pid-{os.getpid()}")
   441→
   442→
   443→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   444→# Module entry point
   445→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   446→
   447→
   448→if __name__ == "__main__":
   449→    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
   450→    daemon = ResourceDaemon()
   451→    asyncio.run(daemon.start())
   452→
```

```
     1→"""Per-model context profiles and OOM-safe Ollama lifecycle management."""
     2→from __future__ import annotations
     3→
     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→import aiohttp
     9→
    10→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    11→
    12→OLLAMA_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:11434"
    13→TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB = 128.0
    14→SAFETY_BUFFER_GB = 10.0
    15→OS_OVERHEAD_GB = 8.0
    16→
    17→_DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS = ["phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", "nomic-embed-text:latest"]
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class ModelContextProfile:
    22→    """Per-model Ollama context settings to prevent KV-cache OOM."""
    23→
    24→    model_name: str
    25→    num_ctx: int  # Context window (KV cache driver)
    26→    num_gpu: int = -1  # GPU layers (-1 = all)
    27→    flash_attention: bool = True  # Reduces KV cache ~50%
    28→    rope_scaling: str = "linear"  # "linear"|"ntk"|"yarn"
    29→    num_parallel: int = 1  # Concurrent requests
    30→    keep_alive: str = "5m"  # How long to keep loaded ("0" = unload immediately)
    31→    size_gb: float = 0.0  # Approximate model size in GB (for OOM checks)
    32→
    33→
    34→def _mk(model_name: str, num_ctx: int, size_gb: float, flash_attention: bool = True, **kw: object) -> ModelContextProfile:
    35→    return ModelContextProfile(model_name=model_name, num_ctx=num_ctx, size_gb=size_gb, flash_attention=flash_attention, **kw)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    36→
    37→
    38→def _training(base: ModelContextProfile) -> ModelContextProfile:
    39→    """Return a copy of a profile with training overrides (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
    40→    return ModelContextProfile(
    41→        model_name=base.model_name,
    42→        num_ctx=2048,
    43→        num_gpu=base.num_gpu,
    44→        flash_attention=base.flash_attention,
    45→        rope_scaling=base.rope_scaling,
    46→        num_parallel=base.num_parallel,
    47→        keep_alive="0",
    48→        size_gb=base.size_gb,
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Per-model context profiles
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→_BASE_PROFILES: list[ModelContextProfile] = [
    56→    # Routing models (0.8B–2B): 32768 ctx
    57→    _mk("qwen3.5:0.8b", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=1.0, flash_attention=True),
    58→    _mk("phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    59→    _mk("nomic-embed-text:latest", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=0.5, flash_attention=False),
    60→    # Execution models (9B–30B): 16384 ctx
    61→    _mk("qwen3.5:9b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    62→    _mk("nemotron-3-nano:30b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    63→    _mk("glm-4.7-flash:latest", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=8.0, flash_attention=True),
    64→    _mk("qwen3-coder:30b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    65→    _mk("deepcoder:14b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=14.0, flash_attention=True),
    66→    _mk("qwen3-coder-next", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=50.0, flash_attention=True),
    67→    _mk("minimax-m2.7", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=25.0, flash_attention=True),
    68→    # Deep reasoning (70B+): 8192 ctx
    69→    _mk("nemotron-3-super:120b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=30.0, flash_attention=True),  # MoE, 12B active
    70→    _mk("glm-5", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),  # 40B active params
    71→    _mk("openai/gpt-oss-20b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=22.0, flash_attention=True),
    72→    # Utility / fine-tuned domain
    73→    _mk("phi3:mini", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    74→    # Fallback
    75→    _mk("default", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    76→]
    77→
    78→# Training variants that override the base profile with reduced context and no keep_alive
    79→_TRAINING_VARIANTS = ["qwen3.5:9b", "nemotron-3-nano:30b", "phi3:mini"]
    80→
    81→CONTEXT_PROFILES: dict[str, ModelContextProfile] = {p.model_name: p for p in _BASE_PROFILES}
    82→
    83→for _model_name in _TRAINING_VARIANTS:
    84→    _key = f"{_model_name}:training"
    85→    CONTEXT_PROFILES[_key] = _training(CONTEXT_PROFILES[_model_name])
    86→
    87→
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→# OllamaContextManager
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→
    92→
    93→class OllamaContextManager:
    94→    """Apply context profiles to Ollama models; enforce OOM-safe training lifecycle."""
    95→
    96→    def __init__(self, base_url: str = OLLAMA_BASE_URL) -> None:
    97→        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
    98→        self._cached_available_gb: float = TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB
    99→
   100→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→    # Profile lookup
   102→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→    def get_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   105→        """Return profile for model; falls back to 'default' if not found."""
   106→        return CONTEXT_PROFILES.get(model_name, CONTEXT_PROFILES["default"])
   107→
   108→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→    # Ollama API helpers
   110→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→    async def apply_profile(self, model: str, profile: ModelContextProfile) -> None:
   113→        """Log the intended context options for a model (advisory; Ollama applies these at generate time)."""
   114→        logger.info(
   115→            "Context profile for %s: num_ctx=%d flash_attention=%s keep_alive=%s",
   116→            model,
   117→            profile.num_ctx,
   118→            profile.flash_attention,
   119→            profile.keep_alive,
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    async def get_loaded_models(self) -> list[str]:
   123→        """Query /api/ps for currently loaded models. Returns empty list on error."""
   124→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   125→        try:
   126→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   127→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   128→                    if resp.status != 200:
   129→                        logger.warning("GET /api/ps returned HTTP %d", resp.status)
   130→                        return []
   131→                    data = await resp.json()
   132→                    models: list[str] = [m["name"] for m in data.get("models", [])]
   133→                    return models
   134→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   135→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps")
   136→            return []
   137→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   138→            logger.warning("ClientError querying Ollama /api/ps: %s", exc)
   139→            return []
   140→
   141→    async def unload_model(self, model: str) -> None:
   142→        """Unload a specific model via POST /api/generate with keep_alive=0."""
   143→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   144→        payload = {"model": model, "keep_alive": 0}
   145→        try:
   146→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   147→                async with asyncio.wait_for(
   148→                    session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=30.0
   149→                ) as resp:
   150→                    if resp.status not in (200, 404):
   151→                        logger.warning("Unload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   152→                    else:
   153→                        logger.info("Unloaded model: %s", model)
   154→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   155→            logger.warning("Timeout unloading model %s", model)
   156→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   157→            logger.warning("ClientError unloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   158→
   159→    async def unload_all_for_training(self) -> None:
   160→        """CRITICAL OOM prevention: unload ALL models before training begins.
   161→
   162→        Steps:
   163→        1. GET /api/ps to find all loaded models.
   164→        2. POST /api/generate keep_alive=0 for each.
   165→        3. Poll /api/ps until empty (max 60 s timeout).
   166→        """
   167→        loaded = await self.get_loaded_models()
   168→        if not loaded:
   169→            logger.info("No models loaded; nothing to unload before training.")
   170→            return
   171→
   172→        logger.info("Unloading %d model(s) before training: %s", len(loaded), loaded)
   173→        await asyncio.gather(*(self.unload_model(m) for m in loaded))
   174→
   175→        # Poll until /api/ps is empty or timeout
   176→        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 60.0
   177→        while asyncio.get_event_loop().time() < deadline:
   178→            remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   179→            if not remaining:
   180→                logger.info("All models unloaded; safe to begin training.")
   181→                return
   182→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   183→
   184→        remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   185→        if remaining:
   186→            logger.warning("Training unload timeout; %d model(s) still loaded: %s", len(remaining), remaining)
   187→
   188→    async def reload_inference_models(self, hot_models: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
   189→        """Restore hot-tier models after training completes.
   190→
   191→        Sends a lightweight /api/generate prompt to each model so Ollama pre-loads it.
   192→        """
   193→        targets = hot_models if hot_models is not None else _DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS
   194→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   195→
   196→        async def _warm(model: str) -> None:
   197→            payload = {"model": model, "prompt": "", "keep_alive": "5m", "stream": False}
   198→            try:
   199→                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   200→                    async with asyncio.wait_for(
   201→                        session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=60.0
   202→                    ) as resp:
   203→                        if resp.status == 200:
   204→                            logger.info("Reloaded inference model: %s", model)
   205→                        else:
   206→                            logger.warning("Reload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   207→            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   208→                logger.warning("Timeout reloading model %s", model)
   209→            except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   210→                logger.warning("ClientError reloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   211→
   212→        await asyncio.gather(*(_warm(m) for m in targets))
   213→
   214→    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
   215→        """Estimate available unified memory in GB.
   216→
   217→        Formula: 128 - sum(loaded model sizes from /api/ps) - OS_OVERHEAD_GB.
   218→        Clamps to >= 0.0. Updates internal cache used by check_oom_risk().
   219→        """
   220→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   221→        loaded_gb = 0.0
   222→        try:
   223→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   224→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   225→                    if resp.status == 200:
   226→                        data = await resp.json()
   227→                        for m in data.get("models", []):
   228→                            size_bytes: int = m.get("size", 0)
   229→                            loaded_gb += size_bytes / (1024**3)
   230→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   231→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps for memory estimate")
   232→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   233→            logger.warning("ClientError querying /api/ps for memory: %s", exc)
   234→
   235→        available = max(0.0, TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - loaded_gb - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB)
   236→        self._cached_available_gb = available
   237→        return available
   238→
   239→    def check_oom_risk(
   240→        self,
   241→        model_size_gb: float,
   242→        training_overhead_factor: float = 3.0,
   243→    ) -> bool:
   244→        """Return True if loading model for training risks OOM.
   245→
   246→        Uses cached available GB (call get_available_memory_gb() first for accuracy).
   247→        OOM risk when: available_gb < model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   248→        """
   249→        required = model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   250→        return self._cached_available_gb < required
   251→
   252→    @property
   253→    def cached_available_gb(self) -> float:
   254→        """Last known available GB. Updated by get_available_memory_gb()."""
   255→        return self._cached_available_gb
   256→
   257→
   258→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   259→# ContextOptimizer (simple helper)
   260→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   261→
   262→
   263→class ContextOptimizer:
   264→    """Helper to select optimal context settings for a given task type."""
   265→
   266→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   267→        self._manager = OllamaContextManager()
   268→
   269→    def get_training_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   270→        """Return reduced-context profile for training (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
   271→        training_key = f"{model_name}:training"
   272→        if training_key in CONTEXT_PROFILES:
   273→            return CONTEXT_PROFILES[training_key]
   274→        # Build an ad-hoc training profile from whatever base we find
   275→        base = self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   276→        return _training(base)
   277→
   278→    def get_inference_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   279→        """Return standard inference profile for a model."""
   280→        return self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   281→
   282→    def estimate_kv_cache_gb(self, num_ctx: int, model_size_gb: float) -> float:
   283→        """Estimate KV cache memory in GB.
   284→
   285→        Approximation: num_ctx * model_size_gb / 128_000.
   286→        KV cache grows roughly linearly with context length for a given model size.
   287→        """
   288→        return (num_ctx * model_size_gb) / 128_000
   289→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The `ResourceClient` implements a "daemon-or-degrade" pattern — if the long-running arbiter isn't reachable, it falls back to polling Ollama directly. This is a resilient design: development works without the daemon; production gets cross-session coordination. The training lock even creates a local in-memory lock when offline, maintaining the interface contract without requiring a server.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Which option?

---

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The CALL (Cohezion Autonomous Learning Loop) implementation follows a layered "graceful degradation" architecture throughout: GeminiEmbeddingModel falls back to FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel, ResourceClient falls back to local Ollama polling, and AgentJetTrainer falls back from verl/PPO to llamafactory. This means the system is fully functional at every stage of deployment maturity — you don't need the daemon running, Gemini available, or Unsloth AMD training shipped to use the core loop.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`
