---

# Code Review: Cohezion Autonomous Learning Loop (CALL)

## Strengths

The implementation shows strong fundamentals across most of the surface area.

**Architecture clarity.** The module decomposition is coherent: `workflow.py` wraps compound execution, `judger.py` owns reward math, `task_reader.py` handles schema normalisation, `context_optimizer.py` controls Ollama lifecycle, `trainer.py` orchestrates the full cycle. Responsibilities are well separated and each file is readable independently.

**OOM safety protocol.** The two-layer safety check in `AgentJetTrainer._safety_check` (daemon-first, local poll fallback) and the `try/finally` reload guarantee in `AgentJetTrainer.train` correctly ensure inference models are restored even on training failure. The logic at `trainer.py:199-220` is exactly right.

**Reward function.** `PhiScoreJudger._compute_reward` implements the HIHO-aware piecewise function correctly: boundary values are clamped, the three regions map to the documented ranges, and the module-level docstring matches the implementation. The test suite (`test_judger.py`) covers all boundary values including the exact continuity at `phi=0.4` and `phi=0.7`.

**Daemon lock lifecycle.** `ResourceDaemon.request_training_lock` and `release_training_lock` correctly hold `self._lock` during mutation. The `cleanup_dead_sessions` comment noting it is called while holding the lock documents the invariant correctly, and the caller in `_poll_loop` does the same, which is safe because `asyncio.Lock` is not reentrant — the poll loop calls `cleanup_dead_sessions` while already holding `self._lock` at line 402–404, but `cleanup_dead_sessions` does not attempt to re-acquire it, so there is no deadlock.

**Fallback coverage.** Every external dependency (`ExperienceCollector`, `LocalFinetuner`, Gemini API, SurrealDB cache, ResourceDaemon) has a graceful degradation path that is explicitly tested.

**Test quality.** The 98 tests all pass, cover both happy and error paths, use `tmp_path` for filesystem fixtures, mock at source, and adhere to the project anti-pattern rules. The `test_trainer.py` tests verify the `finally` reload guarantee directly.

---

## Issues

### Critical (Must Fix)

**C1: `async with asyncio.wait_for(...)` is a runtime TypeError.**

Files: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/agentjet/context_optimizer.py` lines 141, 161, 237.

`asyncio.wait_for()` returns a plain coroutine, not an async context manager. Using it as the target of `async with` raises `TypeError: 'coroutine' object does not support the asynchronous context manager protocol` at the moment the call is made. This was confirmed empirically against aiohttp 3.13.3 with a live test.

This means `get_loaded_models()`, `unload_model()`, and `get_available_memory_gb()` all raise at runtime. Since `unload_all_for_training()` calls `get_loaded_models()` and then `unload_model()`, the entire OOM-prevention critical path fails when Ollama is actually running. The `dry_run=True` tests pass because those methods are mocked; the live path is untested.

Fix: replace `async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=X) as resp` with `async with session.get(url, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=X)) as resp`. `resource_manager.py` already uses `aiohttp.ClientTimeout` correctly throughout — context_optimizer.py should follow the same pattern.

The three affected sites:

```
# context_optimizer.py line 141 — wrong
async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:

# correct
async with session.get(url, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10.0)) as resp:
```

This applies identically to lines 161 and 237.

---

**C2: OOMRiskError raised before the `try` block is silently swallowed by the API handler.**

Files: `trainer.py:138`, `api/__init__.py:1592`.

`_safety_check` is called at line 138, before the `try` block at line 141. If `_safety_check` raises `OOMRiskError` or `ResourceUnavailableError`, neither the inference-model reload nor the lock release in the `finally` block executes. There is also no training lock set at that point, so the lock release is moot — but the reload is critical.

More importantly, the API handler at `api/__init__.py:1592` catches all `Exception` and returns a 200 OK response with `success=False`. This means an OOM denial, which should be signalled as a 503 or 409, is silently returned as a successful HTTP 200. A caller retrying on a 200 will hammer training indefinitely.

The test `test_oom_check_raises_when_memory_insufficient` patches `_safety_check` directly and asserts `pytest.raises(RuntimeError)`, which verifies the exception propagates from `train()` — but the `train()` call in that test is not wrapped in the API handler, so the API silently-swallowing problem is untested.

Fix for trainer: move the reload guard into a broader `try/finally` that also wraps `_safety_check`, or document clearly that reload is intentionally skipped when safety check fails (the docstring currently claims "All steps run inside try/finally so reload always happens" — which is false).

Fix for API: catch `OOMRiskError` and `ResourceUnavailableError` before the broad `except Exception` and return HTTP 503.

---

**C3: Hardcoded developer paths in the systemd unit file.**

File: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/cohezion-resources.service` lines 8–9.

```
WorkingDirectory=/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion
ExecStart=/home/mike-anderson/.local/bin/uv run python -m cohezion.platform.resource_manager
```

This service file cannot be installed on any machine other than the developer's own. The `%i` in `User=%i` is a systemd instance specifier that only expands when the unit is installed as `cohezion-resources@<username>.service`. But `WorkingDirectory` and `ExecStart` are hardcoded absolute paths, making the file non-portable. The project's `.claude/rules/testing.md` explicitly prohibits hardcoded developer paths.

Fix: use `%h` (home directory) or make `WorkingDirectory` an environment variable populated by the installer, or template the path similarly to how `User=%i` is templated.

---

### Important (Should Fix)

**I1: `_MODEL_KEY_MAP` is duplicated verbatim in `trainer.py` and `unsloth_bridge.py`.**

Files: `trainer.py:340-348`, `unsloth_bridge.py:251-260`.

The map is identical in both locations. DRY violation. If a new Ollama model is added to the context profiles (e.g., `qwen3-coder:30b`), it must be added in two places and will silently fall back to `qwen3.5` in the other.

Fix: extract `_MODEL_KEY_MAP` to a module-level constant in `context_optimizer.py` (it logically belongs with the profiles) and import it from both callers.

---

**I2: Relative paths `data/training/...` break when the process is not run from the project root.**

Files: `task_reader.py:38`, `trainer.py:356`, `unsloth_bridge.py:99`, `unsloth_bridge.py:117`.

`Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")` resolves relative to `os.getcwd()`. When the API is started from a different directory (e.g., the systemd unit's `WorkingDirectory` changes), or in a test that doesn't explicitly set `tmp_path`, this silently creates or reads from the wrong location. This is the same class of issue as the anti-pattern described in `.claude/rules/testing.md`.

Fix: anchor paths to the project root using `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[N]` and a `DATA_DIR` constant, consistent with how the rest of the codebase handles paths.

---

**I3: `asyncio.get_event_loop()` is deprecated in Python 3.10+ when called from coroutines.**

Files: `trainer.py:371`, `workflow.py:100`, `context_optimizer.py:188-189`, `unsloth_bridge.py:264`, `embeddings.py:87`, `embeddings.py:178`.

The correct call inside a running async context is `asyncio.get_running_loop()`. `get_event_loop()` emits a `DeprecationWarning` in Python 3.10+ and will eventually stop returning the running loop when called from a coroutine. The project targets Python 3.13+, so this is active today.

Fix: replace all `asyncio.get_event_loop()` calls inside coroutines with `asyncio.get_running_loop()`.

---

**I4: SurrealDB credentials hardcoded as `"root"/"root"` in production embedding code.**

File: `embeddings.py:235`, `embeddings.py:262`.

```python
auth=aiohttp.BasicAuth("root", "root"),
```

These are the default development credentials. Production or shared deployments may use different credentials. This should be read from environment variables (`SURREAL_USER`, `SURREAL_PASS`), consistent with how `GeminiEmbeddingModel` reads `GEMINI_API_KEY` from the environment.

---

**I5: Training lock is stored as instance state (`self._training_lock`), creating a re-entrancy hazard.**

File: `trainer.py:82`, `trainer.py:253`.

`AgentJetTrainer` stores the active lock as `self._training_lock`. If `train()` is called concurrently on the same instance (which the API handler at `api/__init__.py:1572` allows, since a new `AgentJetTrainer()` is created per request), the state is owned by the instance and is safe. However, if the trainer is instantiated as a singleton and two requests race, the second call clobbers `self._training_lock` before the first's `finally` block releases it, leaving the first lock unreleased and permanently reserving memory.

The API currently creates a new `AgentJetTrainer()` per request (line 1572), which avoids the race but adds overhead and prevents concurrent-training detection. The correct fix is either to keep per-request instantiation (acceptable) and document the singleton hazard, or to hold the lock as a local variable and pass it to the `finally` block, eliminating the instance state entirely.

---

**I6: `unload_all_for_training` does not propagate failure when models remain loaded after timeout.**

File: `context_optimizer.py:196-201`.

After the 60-second unload poll loop times out, the code logs a warning but returns normally. The caller (`AgentJetTrainer.train`) proceeds to training even though models are still in unified memory. On a 128 GiB machine training a 30B model, this is the exact OOM scenario the safety protocol exists to prevent. The warning is insufficient; this should raise or set a flag that causes the caller to abort.

---

**I7: Training data write skips update when file already exists.**

File: `trainer.py:360-368`.

```python
if not journey_path.exists():
    ...write tasks to file...
```

If `finetune_journeys.jsonl` already exists from a previous run, the new `tasks` (freshly filtered and scored) are not written. `LocalFinetuner` then trains on stale data, silently. The guard should be removed, or the file should always be overwritten with the current batch.

---

### Minor (Nice to Have)

**M1: `FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel._get_encoder` missing return type annotation.**

File: `embeddings.py:78`. `def _get_encoder(self): # type: ignore[return]` — the `type: ignore` suppresses a mypy error rather than fixing the missing annotation. The return type should be typed with the actual encoder type or `Any`.

**M2: `GeminiEmbeddingModel.encode_sync` spawns a `ThreadPoolExecutor` for every call when inside a running loop.**

File: `embeddings.py:182-185`. Creating and tearing down a thread pool on every synchronous call is expensive. If sync callers are frequent, a module-level shared executor is preferable.

**M3: `GEMINI_API_URL` in `embeddings.py:123-125` refers to `text-embedding-004`, not "Gemini Embedding 2".**

The class docstring and the `DIMENSION = 768` constant reference "Gemini Embedding 2", but the API URL points to `text-embedding-004`. These are the same model, but the mismatch will confuse maintainers. Pick one name and use it consistently.

**M4: `start_resource_daemon.sh` uses `exec` which prevents the `set -e` check on the `curl` exit code.**

File: `scripts/start_resource_daemon.sh:12`. `exec` replaces the shell process, so the script's signal handlers and exit traps are gone. This is cosmetic for a simple launcher but means `set -euo pipefail` provides no protection after that line.

**M5: `TierOptimizer.recommend_tier_changes` docstring says "Only models already present in `current_tiers` are evaluated" but `usage_histogram` models not in `current_tiers` are silently ignored without logging.**

File: `tier_optimizer.py:56-60`. The behavior is correct but a debug-level log for untracked models would ease operational debugging when a model is missing from the tier map.

---

## Recommendations

1. Fix C1 immediately before any other work. The `context_optimizer.py` bug silently breaks the critical OOM-prevention path in all non-mocked execution. The fix is mechanical: replace three `asyncio.wait_for` calls with `aiohttp.ClientTimeout`.

2. Address C2 by deciding whether `_safety_check` raising before the `try` block is intentional (model reload should not happen if safety check never passed) and update the docstring to match. Then add explicit `OOMRiskError` / `ResourceUnavailableError` handling in the API handler to return 503 rather than 200.

3. Fix C3 before the systemd unit is shared or documented as installable, since it cannot currently work on any other machine.

4. I1 (duplicated `_MODEL_KEY_MAP`) and I2 (relative paths) are straightforward refactors that should be batched together since they both involve the `data/training/` path and model name resolution.

5. Add a test that exercises `get_loaded_models()` and `unload_model()` without patching the method under test — specifically, mock `aiohttp.ClientSession` directly so the actual aiohttp integration code runs. The current `test_context_optimizer.py` patches `get_loaded_models` on the object, so it never exercises the aiohttp call path that carries the C1 bug.

---

## Assessment

**Ready to merge? No.**

The `async with asyncio.wait_for(...)` bug in `context_optimizer.py` (C1) causes a `TypeError` at runtime on every live Ollama call, which means `unload_all_for_training()` — the central OOM-prevention mechanism — silently fails whenever models are actually loaded. All three affected code paths are reachable through the API's `/agentjet/train` endpoint. The remaining Critical issues (C2, C3) and Important issues (I1–I7) should be addressed before merge, but C1 alone is a blocking defect.

> AGENT

Reviewing the findings:

- **C1** verified — `asyncio.wait_for()` is a coroutine, not an async context manager. `resource_manager.py` already uses `aiohttp.ClientTimeout` correctly. This is a real bug affecting the entire OOM-prevention path.
- **C2** verified — `_safety_check` fires before the `try` block at `trainer.py:141`. If the daemon path sets `self._training_lock` then raises, the lock is never released. API returns 200 on OOM denial.
- **C3** verified — hardcoded `/home/mike-anderson` in systemd unit.
- **I1–I7** all check out against the source.
- **I5** (singleton hazard): the API creates a new `AgentJetTrainer()` per request, so no actual race condition today — but converting the lock to a local variable is the right design.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
C1 is a subtle Python async pitfall: `asyncio.wait_for()` wraps a coroutine to add timeout behavior, but a coroutine is not an async context manager. `aiohttp`'s `ClientSession.get()` returns a context manager directly, so the timeout belongs in the request call via `aiohttp.ClientTimeout`, not wrapping it with `wait_for`.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

     1→"""AgentJet RL training orchestrator for the CALL loop."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import asyncio
     6→import logging
     7→import time
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Literal
    11→
    12→from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager
    13→from cohezion.agentjet.judger import PhiScoreJudger
    14→from cohezion.agentjet.task_reader import JourneyTaskReader
    15→
    16→
    17→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    18→    from cohezion.agentjet.workflow import CohezionWorkflow
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass
    24→class TrainingResult:
    25→    """Result of a single AgentJet CALL training cycle."""
    26→
    27→    success: bool
    28→    model_name: str  # Final Ollama model name after training
    29→    base_model: str  # Model that was fine-tuned
    30→    skill_domain: str | None  # Which skill domain was trained
    31→    epochs_completed: int
    32→    samples_used: int
    33→    avg_reward: float  # Average reward from PhiScoreJudger
    34→    training_duration_s: float
    35→    output_path: Path | None  # Path to GGUF or config script
    36→    error: str | None = None  # Error message if success=False
    37→    dry_run: bool = False
    38→
    39→
    40→class AgentJetTrainer:
    41→    """Orchestrates the full CALL RL cycle with OOM-safe training.
    42→
    43→    Safety protocol (enforced in train()):
    44→    1. Check available memory via ResourceClient or OllamaContextManager.
    45→    2. Unload ALL inference models.
    46→    3. Run training backend (llamafactory Phase 1, or agentjet Phase 3).
    47→    4. Export GGUF → ollama create cohezion-{domain}-v{n}.
    48→    5. Reload inference models.
    49→    6. Update SmartRouter with new model name.
    50→    All steps run inside try/finally so reload always happens.
    51→
    52→    Parameters
    53→    ----------
    54→    workflow : CohezionWorkflow, optional
    55→        Workflow instance for generating rollouts. Created lazily if not provided.
    56→    judger : PhiScoreJudger, optional
    57→        Reward judger. Defaults to PhiScoreJudger().
    58→    reader : JourneyTaskReader, optional
    59→        Task loader. Defaults to JourneyTaskReader().
    60→    context_manager : OllamaContextManager, optional
    61→        Manages Ollama model lifecycle. Defaults to OllamaContextManager().
    62→    backend : Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"]
    63→        Training backend to use. "llamafactory" uses LocalFinetuner (Phase 1).
    64→        "agentjet" targets verl/PPO (Phase 3, not yet implemented).
    65→    """
    66→
    67→    def __init__(
    68→        self,
    69→        workflow: CohezionWorkflow | None = None,
    70→        judger: PhiScoreJudger | None = None,
    71→        reader: JourneyTaskReader | None = None,
    72→        context_manager: OllamaContextManager | None = None,
    73→        backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = "llamafactory",
    74→    ) -> None:
    75→        self._workflow = workflow  # Lazily resolved; not required for training
    76→        self.judger = judger if judger is not None else PhiScoreJudger()
    77→        self.reader = reader if reader is not None else JourneyTaskReader()
    78→        self.context_manager = (
    79→            context_manager if context_manager is not None else OllamaContextManager()
    80→        )
    81→        self.backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = backend
    82→        self._training_lock: object | None = None  # TrainingLock from ResourceClient
    83→
    84→    async def train(
    85→        self,
    86→        target_model: str = "qwen3.5:9b",
    87→        skill_domain: str | None = None,
    88→        epochs: int = 3,
    89→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    90→        dry_run: bool = False,
    91→    ) -> TrainingResult:
    92→        """Run the full CALL cycle: load data → safety check → train → reload.
    93→
    94→        Parameters
    95→        ----------
    96→        target_model : str
    97→            Base Ollama model to fine-tune.
    98→        skill_domain : str, optional
    99→            Filter training data to this skill domain. None means all domains.
   100→        epochs : int
   101→            Number of training epochs to pass to the backend.
   102→        min_phi : float
   103→            Minimum phi_score threshold for including a task in the training set.
   104→        dry_run : bool
   105→            If True, skip actual training and model unload/reload. Useful for
   106→            validating the pipeline without touching Ollama.
   107→
   108→        Returns
   109→        -------
   110→        TrainingResult
   111→            Describes the outcome of the training run.
   112→        """
   113→        start_time = time.monotonic()
   114→
   115→        # 1. Load training data
   116→        tasks = self.reader.read(skill_filter=skill_domain, min_phi=min_phi)
   117→        if not tasks:
   118→            logger.warning(
   119→                "AgentJetTrainer.train: no tasks matched filters (skill_domain=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   120→                skill_domain,
   121→                min_phi,
   122→            )
   123→            return TrainingResult(
   124→                success=False,
   125→                model_name="",
   126→                base_model=target_model,
   127→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   128→                epochs_completed=0,
   129→                samples_used=0,
   130→                avg_reward=0.0,
   131→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   132→                output_path=None,
   133→                error="No training data matched filters",
   134→                dry_run=dry_run,
   135→            )
   136→
   137→        # 2. OOM safety check — acquires lock or raises OOMRiskError
   138→        await self._safety_check(target_model)
   139→
   140→        output_path: Path | None = None
   141→        try:
   142→            # 3. Unload all inference models before training
   143→            if not dry_run:
   144→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: unloading inference models for training")
   145→                await self.context_manager.unload_all_for_training()
   146→
   147→            # 4. Compute rewards for dataset quality metrics
   148→            rewards = self.judger.batch_judge(tasks)
   149→            avg_reward = sum(rewards) / len(rewards) if rewards else 0.0
   150→            logger.info(
   151→                "AgentJetTrainer: %d tasks, avg_reward=%.3f",
   152→                len(tasks),
   153→                avg_reward,
   154→            )
   155→
   156→            # 5. Run training backend
   157→            if not dry_run:
   158→                output_path = await self._run_training(target_model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   159→
   160→            # 6. Build final model name
   161→            domain = skill_domain or "general"
   162→            version = int(time.time()) % 10000
   163→            final_model_name = f"cohezion-{domain}-v{version}"
   164→
   165→            logger.info(
   166→                "AgentJetTrainer: training complete — model=%s output=%s",
   167→                final_model_name,
   168→                output_path,
   169→            )
   170→            return TrainingResult(
   171→                success=True,
   172→                model_name=final_model_name,
   173→                base_model=target_model,
   174→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   175→                epochs_completed=epochs,
   176→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   177→                avg_reward=avg_reward,
   178→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   179→                output_path=output_path,
   180→                dry_run=dry_run,
   181→            )
   182→
   183→        except Exception as exc:
   184→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer.train failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   185→            return TrainingResult(
   186→                success=False,
   187→                model_name="",
   188→                base_model=target_model,
   189→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   190→                epochs_completed=0,
   191→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   192→                avg_reward=0.0,
   193→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   194→                output_path=None,
   195→                error=str(exc),
   196→                dry_run=dry_run,
   197→            )
   198→
   199→        finally:
   200→            # Always reload inference models — even if training failed
   201→            if not dry_run:
   202→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: reloading inference models")
   203→                try:
   204→                    await self.context_manager.reload_inference_models()
   205→                except Exception as reload_exc:
   206→                    logger.warning("Inference model reload failed (non-fatal): %s", reload_exc)
   207→
   208→            # Release training lock regardless of outcome
   209→            if self._training_lock is not None:
   210→                try:
   211→                    from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import ResourceClient
   212→
   213→                    client = ResourceClient()
   214→                    lock_id: str = self._training_lock.lock_id  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   215→                    await client.release_training_lock(lock_id)
   216→                    logger.debug("AgentJetTrainer: released training lock %s", lock_id)
   217→                except Exception as lock_exc:
   218→                    logger.warning("Training lock release failed (non-fatal): %s", lock_exc)
   219→                finally:
   220→                    self._training_lock = None
   221→
   222→    async def _safety_check(self, model: str) -> None:
   223→        """OOM check before training begins.
   224→
   225→        Tries the platform ResourceClient daemon first (cross-session coordination).
   226→        Falls back to a local Ollama memory estimate when the daemon is unavailable.
   227→
   228→        Raises
   229→        ------
   230→        OOMRiskError
   231→            When local memory check indicates insufficient headroom.
   232→        ResourceUnavailableError
   233→            When the platform daemon explicitly denies the training lock.
   234→        """
   235→        profile = self.context_manager.get_profile(model)
   236→        required_gb = profile.size_gb * 3.0  # Training needs ~3x model size
   237→
   238→        # Attempt platform daemon coordination first
   239→        try:
   240→            from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import (
   241→                ResourceClient,
   242→                ResourceUnavailableError,
   243→            )
   244→
   245→            client = ResourceClient()
   246→            if client.is_daemon_running():
   247→                lock = await client.acquire_training_lock(model, required_gb, timeout_s=30.0)
   248→                if lock is None:
   249→                    raise ResourceUnavailableError(
   250→                        f"Platform daemon denied training lock for {model} "
   251→                        f"(need {required_gb:.1f} GiB)"
   252→                    )
   253→                self._training_lock = lock
   254→                logger.info(
   255→                    "AgentJetTrainer: acquired training lock %s (%.1f GiB reserved)",
   256→                    lock.lock_id,
   257→                    required_gb,
   258→                )
   259→                return
   260→        except ImportError:
   261→            logger.debug("cohezion.platform.resource_manager unavailable; using local OOM check")
   262→
   263→        # Fallback: local available memory estimate
   264→        available = await self.context_manager.get_available_memory_gb()
   265→        headroom_required = required_gb * 1.2
   266→        if available < headroom_required:
   267→            from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager  # noqa: F401
   268→
   269→            # Import OOMRiskError from resource_manager if available, else define locally
   270→            try:
   271→                from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import OOMRiskError
   272→            except ImportError:
   273→
   274→                class OOMRiskError(RuntimeError):  # type: ignore[no-redef]
   275→                    pass
   276→
   277→            raise OOMRiskError(
   278→                f"OOM risk: need {headroom_required:.1f} GiB (with 20% headroom), "
   279→                f"have {available:.1f} GiB available"
   280→            )
   281→
   282→        logger.info(
   283→            "AgentJetTrainer: local OOM check passed (need %.1f GiB, have %.1f GiB)",
   284→            headroom_required,
   285→            available,
   286→        )
   287→
   288→    async def _run_training(
   289→        self,
   290→        model: str,
   291→        tasks: list[dict],
   292→        skill_domain: str | None,
   293→        epochs: int,
   294→    ) -> Path | None:
   295→        """Dispatch to the configured training backend.
   296→
   297→        Parameters
   298→        ----------
   299→        model : str
   300→            Base model identifier.
   301→        tasks : list[dict]
   302→            Normalised task dicts from JourneyTaskReader.
   303→        skill_domain : str, optional
   304→            Skill domain being trained (used for output naming).
   305→        epochs : int
   306→            Number of training epochs.
   307→
   308→        Returns
   309→        -------
   310→        Path | None
   311→            Path to generated training config/script, or None on failure.
   312→        """
   313→        if self.backend == "llamafactory":
   314→            return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   315→        if self.backend == "agentjet":
   316→            return await self._run_agentjet_rl(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   317→        logger.warning("Unknown backend %r; falling back to llamafactory", self.backend)
   318→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   319→
   320→    async def _run_llamafactory(
   321→        self,
   322→        model: str,
   323→        tasks: list[dict],
   324→        skill_domain: str | None,
   325→        epochs: int,
   326→    ) -> Path | None:
   327→        """Phase 1: generate a llamafactory training config via LocalFinetuner.
   328→
   329→        Writes tasks to the standard JSONL location, then calls
   330→        LocalFinetuner.run_qlora_training() which produces a bash script / config.
   331→
   332→        Returns the path to the generated config file, or None on error.
   333→        """
   334→        try:
   335→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   336→
   337→            output_name = f"cohezion_{skill_domain or 'general'}_v{int(time.time()) % 10000}"
   338→
   339→            # Map full Ollama model names to LocalFinetuner's known base keys
   340→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   341→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   342→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   343→                "phi4": "phi4",
   344→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   345→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   346→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   347→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   348→            }
   349→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   350→
   351→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name=output_name)
   352→
   353→            # Ensure training data directory and JSONL exist for LocalFinetuner
   354→            import json
   355→
   356→            data_dir = Path("data/training")
   357→            data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   358→            journey_path = data_dir / "finetune_journeys.jsonl"
   359→
   360→            if not journey_path.exists():
   361→                logger.info(
   362→                    "AgentJetTrainer: writing %d tasks to %s for LocalFinetuner",
   363→                    len(tasks),
   364→                    journey_path,
   365→                )
   366→                with journey_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   367→                    for task in tasks:
   368→                        fh.write(json.dumps(task) + "\n")
   369→
   370→            # Run in executor — LocalFinetuner is synchronous
   371→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   372→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   373→                None,
   374→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   375→            )
   376→            logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: llamafactory config generated: %s", config_path)
   377→            return config_path
   378→
   379→        except Exception as exc:
   380→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer._run_llamafactory failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   381→            return None
   382→
   383→    async def _run_agentjet_rl(
   384→        self,
   385→        model: str,
   386→        tasks: list[dict],
   387→        skill_domain: str | None,
   388→        epochs: int,
   389→    ) -> Path | None:
   390→        """Phase 3: AgentJet verl/PPO RL training.
   391→
   392→        Not yet implemented (AMD ROCm support for verl is pending).
   393→        Falls back to llamafactory until Phase 3 ships.
   394→        """
   395→        logger.warning(
   396→            "AgentJet RL backend (verl/PPO) is not yet implemented for AMD. "
   397→            "Falling back to llamafactory."
   398→        )
   399→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   400→

     1→"""Per-model context profiles and OOM-safe Ollama lifecycle management."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import asyncio
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→
     9→import aiohttp
    10→
    11→
    12→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13→
    14→OLLAMA_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:11434"
    15→TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB = 128.0
    16→SAFETY_BUFFER_GB = 10.0
    17→OS_OVERHEAD_GB = 8.0
    18→
    19→_DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS = ["phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", "nomic-embed-text:latest"]
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class ModelContextProfile:
    24→    """Per-model Ollama context settings to prevent KV-cache OOM."""
    25→
    26→    model_name: str
    27→    num_ctx: int  # Context window (KV cache driver)
    28→    num_gpu: int = -1  # GPU layers (-1 = all)
    29→    flash_attention: bool = True  # Reduces KV cache ~50%
    30→    rope_scaling: str = "linear"  # "linear"|"ntk"|"yarn"
    31→    num_parallel: int = 1  # Concurrent requests
    32→    keep_alive: str = "5m"  # How long to keep loaded ("0" = unload immediately)
    33→    size_gb: float = 0.0  # Approximate model size in GB (for OOM checks)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _mk(
    37→    model_name: str, num_ctx: int, size_gb: float, flash_attention: bool = True, **kw: object
    38→) -> ModelContextProfile:
    39→    return ModelContextProfile(
    40→        model_name=model_name,
    41→        num_ctx=num_ctx,
    42→        size_gb=size_gb,
    43→        flash_attention=flash_attention,
    44→        **kw,
    45→    )  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    46→
    47→
    48→def _training(base: ModelContextProfile) -> ModelContextProfile:
    49→    """Return a copy of a profile with training overrides (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
    50→    return ModelContextProfile(
    51→        model_name=base.model_name,
    52→        num_ctx=2048,
    53→        num_gpu=base.num_gpu,
    54→        flash_attention=base.flash_attention,
    55→        rope_scaling=base.rope_scaling,
    56→        num_parallel=base.num_parallel,
    57→        keep_alive="0",
    58→        size_gb=base.size_gb,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→# Per-model context profiles
    64→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→_BASE_PROFILES: list[ModelContextProfile] = [
    66→    # Routing models (0.8B–2B): 32768 ctx
    67→    _mk("qwen3.5:0.8b", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=1.0, flash_attention=True),
    68→    _mk("phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    69→    _mk("nomic-embed-text:latest", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=0.5, flash_attention=False),
    70→    # Execution models (9B–30B): 16384 ctx
    71→    _mk("qwen3.5:9b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    72→    _mk("nemotron-3-nano:30b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    73→    _mk("glm-4.7-flash:latest", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=8.0, flash_attention=True),
    74→    _mk("qwen3-coder:30b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    75→    _mk("deepcoder:14b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=14.0, flash_attention=True),
    76→    _mk("qwen3-coder-next", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=50.0, flash_attention=True),
    77→    _mk("minimax-m2.7", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=25.0, flash_attention=True),
    78→    # Deep reasoning (70B+): 8192 ctx
    79→    _mk(
    80→        "nemotron-3-super:120b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=30.0, flash_attention=True
    81→    ),  # MoE, 12B active
    82→    _mk("glm-5", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),  # 40B active params
    83→    _mk("openai/gpt-oss-20b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=22.0, flash_attention=True),
    84→    # Utility / fine-tuned domain
    85→    _mk("phi3:mini", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    86→    # Fallback
    87→    _mk("default", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    88→]
    89→
    90→# Training variants that override the base profile with reduced context and no keep_alive
    91→_TRAINING_VARIANTS = ["qwen3.5:9b", "nemotron-3-nano:30b", "phi3:mini"]
    92→
    93→CONTEXT_PROFILES: dict[str, ModelContextProfile] = {p.model_name: p for p in _BASE_PROFILES}
    94→
    95→for _model_name in _TRAINING_VARIANTS:
    96→    _key = f"{_model_name}:training"
    97→    CONTEXT_PROFILES[_key] = _training(CONTEXT_PROFILES[_model_name])
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# OllamaContextManager
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→
   105→class OllamaContextManager:
   106→    """Apply context profiles to Ollama models; enforce OOM-safe training lifecycle."""
   107→
   108→    def __init__(self, base_url: str = OLLAMA_BASE_URL) -> None:
   109→        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
   110→        self._cached_available_gb: float = (
   111→            TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB
   112→        )
   113→
   114→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→    # Profile lookup
   116→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→    def get_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   119→        """Return profile for model; falls back to 'default' if not found."""
   120→        return CONTEXT_PROFILES.get(model_name, CONTEXT_PROFILES["default"])
   121→
   122→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→    # Ollama API helpers
   124→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→
   126→    async def apply_profile(self, model: str, profile: ModelContextProfile) -> None:
   127→        """Log the intended context options for a model (advisory; Ollama applies these at generate time)."""
   128→        logger.info(
   129→            "Context profile for %s: num_ctx=%d flash_attention=%s keep_alive=%s",
   130→            model,
   131→            profile.num_ctx,
   132→            profile.flash_attention,
   133→            profile.keep_alive,
   134→        )
   135→
   136→    async def get_loaded_models(self) -> list[str]:
   137→        """Query /api/ps for currently loaded models. Returns empty list on error."""
   138→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   139→        try:
   140→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   141→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   142→                    if resp.status != 200:
   143→                        logger.warning("GET /api/ps returned HTTP %d", resp.status)
   144→                        return []
   145→                    data = await resp.json()
   146→                    models: list[str] = [m["name"] for m in data.get("models", [])]
   147→                    return models
   148→        except TimeoutError:
   149→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps")
   150→            return []
   151→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   152→            logger.warning("ClientError querying Ollama /api/ps: %s", exc)
   153→            return []
   154→
   155→    async def unload_model(self, model: str) -> None:
   156→        """Unload a specific model via POST /api/generate with keep_alive=0."""
   157→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   158→        payload = {"model": model, "keep_alive": 0}
   159→        try:
   160→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   161→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=30.0) as resp:
   162→                    if resp.status not in (200, 404):
   163→                        logger.warning("Unload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   164→                    else:
   165→                        logger.info("Unloaded model: %s", model)
   166→        except TimeoutError:
   167→            logger.warning("Timeout unloading model %s", model)
   168→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   169→            logger.warning("ClientError unloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   170→
   171→    async def unload_all_for_training(self) -> None:
   172→        """CRITICAL OOM prevention: unload ALL models before training begins.
   173→
   174→        Steps:
   175→        1. GET /api/ps to find all loaded models.
   176→        2. POST /api/generate keep_alive=0 for each.
   177→        3. Poll /api/ps until empty (max 60 s timeout).
   178→        """
   179→        loaded = await self.get_loaded_models()
   180→        if not loaded:
   181→            logger.info("No models loaded; nothing to unload before training.")
   182→            return
   183→
   184→        logger.info("Unloading %d model(s) before training: %s", len(loaded), loaded)
   185→        await asyncio.gather(*(self.unload_model(m) for m in loaded))
   186→
   187→        # Poll until /api/ps is empty or timeout
   188→        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 60.0
   189→        while asyncio.get_event_loop().time() < deadline:
   190→            remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   191→            if not remaining:
   192→                logger.info("All models unloaded; safe to begin training.")
   193→                return
   194→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   195→
   196→        remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   197→        if remaining:
   198→            logger.warning(
   199→                "Training unload timeout; %d model(s) still loaded: %s", len(remaining), remaining
   200→            )
   201→
   202→    async def reload_inference_models(self, hot_models: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
   203→        """Restore hot-tier models after training completes.
   204→
   205→        Sends a lightweight /api/generate prompt to each model so Ollama pre-loads it.
   206→        """
   207→        targets = hot_models if hot_models is not None else _DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS
   208→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   209→
   210→        async def _warm(model: str) -> None:
   211→            payload = {"model": model, "prompt": "", "keep_alive": "5m", "stream": False}
   212→            try:
   213→                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, asyncio.wait_for(
   214→                    session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=60.0
   215→                ) as resp:
   216→                    if resp.status == 200:
   217→                        logger.info("Reloaded inference model: %s", model)
   218→                    else:
   219→                        logger.warning("Reload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   220→            except TimeoutError:
   221→                logger.warning("Timeout reloading model %s", model)
   222→            except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   223→                logger.warning("ClientError reloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   224→
   225→        await asyncio.gather(*(_warm(m) for m in targets))
   226→
   227→    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
   228→        """Estimate available unified memory in GB.
   229→
   230→        Formula: 128 - sum(loaded model sizes from /api/ps) - OS_OVERHEAD_GB.
   231→        Clamps to >= 0.0. Updates internal cache used by check_oom_risk().
   232→        """
   233→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   234→        loaded_gb = 0.0
   235→        try:
   236→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   237→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   238→                    if resp.status == 200:
   239→                        data = await resp.json()
   240→                        for m in data.get("models", []):
   241→                            size_bytes: int = m.get("size", 0)
   242→                            loaded_gb += size_bytes / (1024**3)
   243→        except TimeoutError:
   244→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps for memory estimate")
   245→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   246→            logger.warning("ClientError querying /api/ps for memory: %s", exc)
   247→
   248→        available = max(0.0, TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - loaded_gb - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB)
   249→        self._cached_available_gb = available
   250→        return available
   251→
   252→    def check_oom_risk(
   253→        self,
   254→        model_size_gb: float,
   255→        training_overhead_factor: float = 3.0,
   256→    ) -> bool:
   257→        """Return True if loading model for training risks OOM.
   258→
   259→        Uses cached available GB (call get_available_memory_gb() first for accuracy).
   260→        OOM risk when: available_gb < model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   261→        """
   262→        required = model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   263→        return self._cached_available_gb < required
   264→
   265→    @property
   266→    def cached_available_gb(self) -> float:
   267→        """Last known available GB. Updated by get_available_memory_gb()."""
   268→        return self._cached_available_gb
   269→
   270→
   271→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   272→# ContextOptimizer (simple helper)
   273→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   274→
   275→
   276→class ContextOptimizer:
   277→    """Helper to select optimal context settings for a given task type."""
   278→
   279→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   280→        self._manager = OllamaContextManager()
   281→
   282→    def get_training_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   283→        """Return reduced-context profile for training (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
   284→        training_key = f"{model_name}:training"
   285→        if training_key in CONTEXT_PROFILES:
   286→            return CONTEXT_PROFILES[training_key]
   287→        # Build an ad-hoc training profile from whatever base we find
   288→        base = self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   289→        return _training(base)
   290→
   291→    def get_inference_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   292→        """Return standard inference profile for a model."""
   293→        return self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   294→
   295→    def estimate_kv_cache_gb(self, num_ctx: int, model_size_gb: float) -> float:
   296→        """Estimate KV cache memory in GB.
   297→
   298→        Approximation: num_ctx * model_size_gb / 128_000.
   299→        KV cache grows roughly linearly with context length for a given model size.
   300→        """
   301→        return (num_ctx * model_size_gb) / 128_000
   302→

     1→"""Unsloth Studio integration for Phase 2 QLoRA training.
     2→
     3→Available NOW:  Data Recipes API (convert vault → dataset).
     4→Available SOON: QLoRA training (when AMD ROCm support ships in Unsloth).
     5→

     7→Phase 2 (planned): QLoRA via Unsloth Studio once AMD support is confirmed.

     9→
    10→from __future__ import annotations
    11→
    12→import json
    13→import logging
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→import aiohttp
    18→
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    22→UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL = "https://studio.unsloth.ai"

    24→# Data Recipes endpoint (available now — no AMD dependency)
    25→_RECIPES_ENDPOINT = "/api/v1/recipes"

    27→
    28→class UnslothBridge:
    29→    """Interface to Unsloth Studio for dataset preparation and QLoRA training.
    30→
    31→    Phases
    32→    ------
    33→    Phase 1 (active): LocalFinetuner fallback — llama.cpp / llamafactory config.
    34→    Phase 2 (pending): QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio when AMD support ships.
    35→    Data Recipes (active): ``create_dataset_from_vault`` is available now and
    36→    calls the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API to convert vault notes to JSONL.
    37→
    38→    Parameters
    39→    ----------
    40→    api_key : str, optional
    41→        Unsloth Studio API key. If None, Data Recipes API calls will be
    42→        attempted without auth (may fail for private endpoints).
    43→    studio_url : str
    44→        Base URL for the Unsloth Studio API.
    45→    """
    46→
    47→    def __init__(
    48→        self,
    49→        api_key: str | None = None,
    50→        studio_url: str = UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL,
    51→    ) -> None:
    52→        self._api_key = api_key
    53→        self._studio_url = studio_url.rstrip("/")
    54→
    55→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→    # Capability probes
    57→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→
    59→    def is_training_available(self) -> bool:
    60→        """Return True if Unsloth QLoRA training is supported on current hardware.
    61→
    62→        Currently returns False because Unsloth Studio QLoRA training requires
    63→        CUDA (NVIDIA) and AMD ROCm support has not shipped yet.  When AMD support
    64→        arrives this method should be updated to probe the runtime.
    65→        """
    66→        return False
    67→
    68→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→    # Data Recipes (available now)
    70→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    71→
    72→    async def create_dataset_from_vault(
    73→        self,
    74→        vault_query: str,
    75→        output_path: Path | None = None,
    76→    ) -> Path:
    77→        """Convert vault notes to a JSONL training dataset via Unsloth Data Recipes.
    78→
    79→        Attempts to call the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API.  If the API is
    80→        unavailable (no key, network error, or endpoint not reachable) the
    81→        method falls back to reading the local finetune_journeys.jsonl file
    82→        produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
    83→
    84→        Parameters
    85→        ----------
    86→        vault_query : str
    87→            Search query used to select relevant vault notes (forwarded to the
    88→            Data Recipes API as the ``query`` field).
    89→        output_path : Path, optional
    90→            Where to write the output JSONL.  Defaults to
    91→            ``data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl``.
    92→
    93→        Returns
    94→        -------
    95→        Path
    96→            Absolute path to the generated JSONL dataset file.
    97→        """
    98→        out = (
    99→            output_path if output_path is not None else Path("data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl")
   100→        )
   101→        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   102→
   103→        # Try Unsloth Data Recipes API first
   104→        api_data = await self._call_data_recipes_api(vault_query)
   105→        if api_data is not None:
   106→            logger.info(
   107→                "UnslothBridge: received %d records from Data Recipes API",
   108→                len(api_data),
   109→            )
   110→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   111→                for record in api_data:
   112→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   113→            logger.info("UnslothBridge: dataset written to %s", out)
   114→            return out
   115→
   116→        # Fallback: copy local finetune_journeys.jsonl
   117→        local_source = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
   118→        if local_source.exists():
   119→            logger.info(
   120→                "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unavailable; copying %s → %s",
   121→                local_source,
   122→                out,
   123→            )
   124→            records = _read_jsonl(local_source)
   125→            # Filter records loosely by vault_query in instruction/output text
   126→            if vault_query:
   127→                query_lower = vault_query.lower()
   128→                filtered = [
   129→                    r
   130→                    for r in records
   131→                    if query_lower in str(r.get("instruction", "")).lower()
   132→                    or query_lower in str(r.get("output", "")).lower()
   133→                ]
   134→                # If filter is too aggressive, keep all records
   135→                if not filtered:
   136→                    filtered = records
   137→            else:
   138→                filtered = records
   139→
   140→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   141→                for record in filtered:
   142→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   143→            logger.info(
   144→                "UnslothBridge: fallback dataset written (%d records) to %s",
   145→                len(filtered),
   146→                out,
   147→            )
   148→            return out
   149→
   150→        # Last resort: empty dataset with a warning
   151→        logger.warning(
   152→            "UnslothBridge: no data source available (API down, no local JSONL). "
   153→            "Writing empty dataset to %s",
   154→            out,
   155→        )
   156→        out.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
   157→        return out
   158→
   159→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   160→    # QLoRA training (Phase 2 — not yet available on AMD)
   161→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   162→
   163→    async def train_qlora(
   164→        self,
   165→        dataset: Path,
   166→        model: str,
   167→        config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   168→    ) -> Path | None:
   169→        """Attempt QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio.
   170→
   171→        Returns None when AMD training is not yet supported (current state).
   172→        Phase 2 will implement full QLoRA when Unsloth ships AMD ROCm support.
   173→
   174→        Parameters
   175→        ----------
   176→        dataset : Path
   177→            Path to the JSONL training dataset (e.g., from create_dataset_from_vault).
   178→        model : str
   179→            Base model identifier (e.g., "qwen3.5:9b").
   180→        config : dict, optional
   181→            Additional training configuration forwarded to the Unsloth API.
   182→
   183→        Returns
   184→        -------
   185→        Path | None
   186→            Path to the trained model checkpoint, or None if training is
   187→            unavailable on the current hardware.
   188→        """
   189→        if not self.is_training_available():
   190→            logger.info(
   191→                "UnslothBridge.train_qlora: AMD QLoRA not available yet (Phase 2). "
   192→                "Falling back to LocalFinetuner for %s.",
   193→                model,
   194→            )
   195→            return await self._llamafactory_fallback(dataset, model, config)
   196→
   197→        # Phase 2 placeholder — implement when AMD Unsloth support ships
   198→        raise NotImplementedError(
   199→            "Unsloth QLoRA training on AMD is not yet implemented. "
   200→            "Set is_training_available() → True once AMD support ships."
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   204→    # Private helpers
   205→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   206→
   207→    async def _call_data_recipes_api(self, vault_query: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:
   208→        """Call Unsloth Studio Data Recipes endpoint.
   209→
   210→        Returns the parsed list of records on success, or None on any failure.
   211→        """
   212→        url = f"{self._studio_url}{_RECIPES_ENDPOINT}"
   213→        headers: dict[str, str] = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   214→        if self._api_key:
   215→            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self._api_key}"
   216→
   217→        payload = {"query": vault_query, "format": "jsonl"}
   218→
   219→        try:
   220→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, session.post(
   221→                url,
   222→                json=payload,
   223→                headers=headers,
   224→                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=30.0),
   225→            ) as resp:
   226→                if resp.status == 200:
   227→                    data = await resp.json()
   228→                    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = data.get("records", [])
   229→                    return records
   230→                logger.debug(
   231→                    "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API returned HTTP %d — using fallback",
   232→                    resp.status,
   233→                )
   234→                return None
   235→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   236→            logger.debug("UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unreachable: %s", exc)
   237→            return None
   238→
   239→    async def _llamafactory_fallback(
   240→        self,
   241→        dataset: Path,
   242→        model: str,
   243→        config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   244→    ) -> Path | None:
   245→        """Generate a llamafactory training config as a Phase 2 fallback."""
   246→        import asyncio
   247→
   248→        try:
   249→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   250→
   251→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   252→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   253→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   254→                "phi4": "phi4",
   255→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   256→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   257→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   258→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   259→            }
   260→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   261→            epochs = int((config or {}).get("epochs", 3))
   262→
   263→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name="cohezion_unsloth_phase2")
   264→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   265→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   266→                None,
   267→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   268→            )
   269→            logger.info("UnslothBridge: llamafactory fallback config written to %s", config_path)
   270→            return config_path
   271→        except Exception as exc:
   272→            logger.error("UnslothBridge._llamafactory_fallback failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   273→            return None
   274→
   275→
   276→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   277→# Module-level helpers
   278→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   279→
   280→
   281→def _read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   282→    """Read a JSONL file and return a list of parsed dicts. Skips invalid lines."""
   283→    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   284→    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   285→        for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   286→            line = line.strip()
   287→            if not line:
   288→                continue
   289→            try:
   290→                records.append(json.loads(line))
   291→            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   292→                logger.warning(
   293→                    "_read_jsonl: skipping invalid JSON at line %d in %s: %s",
   294→                    lineno,
   295→                    path,
   296→                    exc,
   297→                )
   298→    return records
   299→

     1→"""Gemini Embedding 2 integration with local FLUME VAE fallback.
     2→
     3→Models: GeminiEmbeddingModel (cloud + SurrealDB cache), FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel (local 256D).
     4→EmbeddingDistiller: knowledge distillation stub (Phase 2).
     5→EmbeddingOrchestrator: context-aware routing.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import asyncio
    11→import hashlib
    12→import logging
    13→import os
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass
    15→from enum import Enum, auto
    16→from pathlib import Path
    17→from typing import Protocol, runtime_checkable
    18→
    19→import numpy as np
    20→
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Data types
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class EmbeddingResult:
    32→    """Result of an embedding operation."""
    33→
    34→    vector: np.ndarray  # The embedding vector
    35→    model: str  # Which model produced it
    36→    cached: bool = False  # Whether this came from cache
    37→    dimension: int = 0  # Vector dimension (set from vector.shape[0])
    38→
    39→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    40→        self.dimension = len(self.vector)
    41→
    42→
    43→class EmbeddingContext(Enum):
    44→    """Routing context for embedding requests."""
    45→
    46→    VAULT_INDEXING = auto()  # Offline, one-time — use Gemini cloud
    47→    RUNTIME = auto()  # Online hot path — use local FLUME VAE
    48→    TRAINING_REWARD = auto()  # Offline for distillation — use Gemini cloud
    49→    FAST_ROUTING = auto()  # Hot path — use hash fallback
    50→
    51→
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Protocol
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→
    56→
    57→@runtime_checkable
    58→class EmbeddingModel(Protocol):
    59→    """Protocol for embedding models — sync and async encode."""
    60→
    61→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult: ...
    62→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult: ...
    63→
    64→
    65→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→# FLUME VAE local model
    67→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→
    69→
    70→class FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel:
    71→    """Local FLUME VAE embedding (256D). Used for runtime inference."""
    72→
    73→    DIMENSION = 256
    74→
    75→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    76→        self._encoder = None  # Lazy load
    77→
    78→    def _get_encoder(self):  # type: ignore[return]
    79→        if self._encoder is None:
    80→            from cohezion.flume.vae_encoder import get_encoder
    81→
    82→            self._encoder = get_encoder()
    83→        return self._encoder
    84→
    85→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
    86→        """Encode text using FLUME VAE (runs in thread to avoid blocking loop)."""
    87→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
    88→        vector = await loop.run_in_executor(None, self._encode_blocking, text)
    89→        return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="flume-vae-256d")
    90→
    91→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
    92→        """Synchronous encode using FLUME VAE."""
    93→        vector = self._encode_blocking(text)
    94→        return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="flume-vae-256d")
    95→
    96→    def _encode_blocking(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
    97→        try:
    98→            encoder = self._get_encoder()
    99→            return encoder.encode(text)
   100→        except Exception as exc:
   101→            logger.warning("FLUME VAE encode failed, using hash fallback: %s", exc)
   102→            return self._hash_encode(text)
   103→
   104→    @staticmethod
   105→    def _hash_encode(text: str) -> np.ndarray:
   106→        """Deterministic 256D hash fallback."""
   107→        h = hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).digest()
   108→        embedding = np.array([h[i % len(h)] / 255.0 for i in range(256)], dtype=np.float32)
   109→        norm = np.linalg.norm(embedding)
   110→        if norm > 0:
   111→            embedding /= norm
   112→        return embedding
   113→
   114→
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→# Gemini cloud model
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→
   120→class GeminiEmbeddingModel:
   121→    """Gemini Embedding 2 with content-hash SurrealDB cache and FLUME VAE fallback."""
   122→
   123→    GEMINI_API_URL = (
   124→        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/text-embedding-004:embedContent"
   125→    )
   126→    DIMENSION = 768  # Gemini Embedding 2 output dimension
   127→    _CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT = 3
   128→
   129→    def __init__(
   130→        self,
   131→        api_key: str | None = None,
   132→        fallback: EmbeddingModel | None = None,
   133→        surreal_url: str = "ws://localhost:8000",
   134→    ) -> None:
   135→        self._api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")
   136→        self._fallback = fallback or FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel()
   137→        self._surreal_url = surreal_url
   138→        self._fail_count = 0  # Simple circuit-breaker counter
   139→        self._surreal: object | None = None  # Lazy SurrealDB handle
   140→
   141→    # --- Public interface ---------------------------------------------------
   142→
   143→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
   144→        """Encode with cache-first strategy."""
   145→        key = self._content_hash(text)
   146→
   147→        # 1. Cache lookup
   148→        cached_vector = await self._cache_lookup(key)
   149→        if cached_vector is not None:
   150→            return EmbeddingResult(vector=cached_vector, model="gemini-embedding-2", cached=True)
   151→
   152→        # 2. Circuit breaker: skip API if too many recent failures
   153→        if self._fail_count >= self._CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT:
   154→            logger.warning(
   155→                "Gemini circuit open (%d failures), routing to fallback", self._fail_count
   156→            )
   157→            return await self._fallback.encode(text)
   158→
   159→        # 3. Call Gemini API
   160→        try:
   161→            vector = await self._call_gemini_api(text)
   162→            self._fail_count = 0  # Reset on success
   163→            await self._cache_store(key, vector)
   164→            return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="gemini-embedding-2")
   165→        except Exception as exc:
   166→            self._fail_count += 1
   167→            logger.warning(
   168→                "Gemini API error (%d/%d): %s — falling back to FLUME VAE",
   169→                self._fail_count,
   170→                self._CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT,
   171→                exc,
   172→            )
   173→            return await self._fallback.encode(text)
   174→
   175→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
   176→        """Synchronous wrapper for async encode."""
   177→        try:
   178→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   179→            if loop.is_running():
   180→                # Called from within an async context — run in thread
   181→                import concurrent.futures
   182→
   183→                with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
   184→                    future = pool.submit(asyncio.run, self.encode(text))
   185→                    return future.result()
   186→            return loop.run_until_complete(self.encode(text))
   187→        except RuntimeError:
   188→            return asyncio.run(self.encode(text))
   189→
   190→    # --- Internal helpers ---------------------------------------------------
   191→
   192→    @staticmethod
   193→    def _content_hash(text: str) -> str:
   194→        return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
   195→
   196→    async def _call_gemini_api(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
   197→        """Call Gemini Embedding 2 API. Raises on any error."""
   198→        try:
   199→            import aiohttp
   200→        except ImportError as exc:
   201→            raise RuntimeError("aiohttp required for Gemini API calls") from exc
   202→
   203→        if not self._api_key:
   204→            raise ValueError("GEMINI_API_KEY not set")
   205→
   206→        url = f"{self.GEMINI_API_URL}?key={self._api_key}"
   207→        payload = {"model": "models/text-embedding-004", "content": {"parts": [{"text": text}]}}
   208→
   209→        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, session.post(
   210→            url, json=payload, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10)
   211→        ) as resp:
   212→            if resp.status != 200:
   213→                body = await resp.text()
   214→                raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini API {resp.status}: {body[:200]}")
   215→            data = await resp.json()
   216→
   217→        values = data["embedding"]["values"]
   218→        vector = np.array(values, dtype=np.float32)
   219→        norm = np.linalg.norm(vector)
   220→        if norm > 0:
   221→            vector /= norm
   222→        return vector
   223→
   224→    async def _cache_lookup(self, key: str) -> np.ndarray | None:
   225→        """Check SurrealDB for a cached embedding vector."""
   226→        try:
   227→            import aiohttp
   228→
   229→            url = "http://localhost:8000/sql"
   230→            query = f"SELECT vector FROM embedding_cache WHERE content_hash = '{key}' LIMIT 1;"
   231→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, session.post(
   232→                url,
   233→                data=query,
   234→                headers={"Accept": "application/json", "NS": "cohezion", "DB": "embeddings"},
   235→                auth=aiohttp.BasicAuth("root", "root"),
   236→                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=2),
   237→            ) as resp:
   238→                if resp.status != 200:
   239→                    return None
   240→                result = await resp.json()
   241→                rows = result[0].get("result", []) if result else []
   242→                if rows and rows[0].get("vector"):
   243→                    return np.array(rows[0]["vector"], dtype=np.float32)
   244→        except Exception as exc:
   245→            logger.debug("Cache lookup skipped: %s", exc)
   246→        return None
   247→
   248→    async def _cache_store(self, key: str, vector: np.ndarray) -> None:
   249→        """Store embedding vector in SurrealDB cache."""
   250→        try:
   251→            import aiohttp
   252→
   253→            vector_list = vector.tolist()
   254→            query = (
   255→                f"CREATE embedding_cache CONTENT {{content_hash: '{key}', vector: {vector_list}}};"
   256→            )
   257→            url = "http://localhost:8000/sql"
   258→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, session.post(
   259→                url,
   260→                data=query,
   261→                headers={"Accept": "application/json", "NS": "cohezion", "DB": "embeddings"},
   262→                auth=aiohttp.BasicAuth("root", "root"),
   263→                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=2),
   264→            ) as resp:
   265→                if resp.status != 200:
   266→                    logger.debug("Cache store returned %d", resp.status)
   267→        except Exception as exc:
   268→            logger.debug("Cache store skipped (non-critical): %s", exc)
   269→
   270→
   271→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   272→# Distiller
   273→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   274→
   275→
   276→class EmbeddingDistiller:
   277→    """Distills Gemini teacher embeddings into local FLUME VAE student (Phase 2 stub)."""
   278→
   279→    def __init__(
   280→        self,
   281→        teacher: GeminiEmbeddingModel,
   282→        student_vae_path: Path | None = None,
   283→    ) -> None:
   284→        self._teacher = teacher
   285→        self._student_vae_path = student_vae_path
   286→
   287→    async def distill(
   288→        self,
   289→        corpus: list[str],
   290→        epochs: int = 10,
   291→        output_path: Path | None = None,
   292→    ) -> Path:
   293→        """Fine-tune FLUME VAE to minimize distance to Gemini vectors (Phase 2 stub)."""
   294→        logger.warning(
   295→            "EmbeddingDistiller.distill() is a Phase 2 stub. "
   296→            "corpus=%d texts, epochs=%d. Full FLUME VAE training API not yet implemented.",
   297→            len(corpus),
   298→            epochs,
   299→        )
   300→        return output_path or Path("")
   301→
   302→
   303→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   304→# Orchestrator
   305→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   306→
   307→
   308→class EmbeddingOrchestrator:
   309→    """Routes embedding requests to the right model based on context.
   310→
   311→    VAULT_INDEXING / TRAINING_REWARD -> Gemini cloud.
   312→    RUNTIME / FAST_ROUTING           -> FLUME VAE local.
   313→    """
   314→
   315→    def __init__(
   316→        self,
   317→        gemini_model: GeminiEmbeddingModel | None = None,
   318→        flume_model: FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel | None = None,
   319→    ) -> None:
   320→        self._gemini = gemini_model or GeminiEmbeddingModel()
   321→        self._flume = flume_model or FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel()
   322→
   323→    def get_model(self, context: EmbeddingContext) -> EmbeddingModel:
   324→        """Return the appropriate model for the given context."""
   325→        if context in (EmbeddingContext.VAULT_INDEXING, EmbeddingContext.TRAINING_REWARD):
   326→            return self._gemini
   327→        # RUNTIME and FAST_ROUTING both use local FLUME VAE
   328→        return self._flume
   329→
   330→    async def encode(
   331→        self,
   332→        text: str,
   333→        context: EmbeddingContext = EmbeddingContext.RUNTIME,
   334→    ) -> EmbeddingResult:
   335→        """Encode text using the model appropriate for the given context."""
   336→        model = self.get_model(context)
   337→        return await model.encode(text)
