     1→"""JourneyTaskReader: loads training tasks from journey JSONL or ExperienceCollector.
     2→
     3→Primary source: ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` produced by
     4→``JourneyToFinetuneConverter.run()``.
     5→
     6→Fallback source: ``ExperienceCollector.collect_all()`` which pulls from
     7→Parquet shards, SurrealDB, and vault experiment JSON files.
     8→
     9→JSONL record schema (from JourneyToFinetuneConverter._to_training_pairs):
    10→  {
    11→    "instruction": str,
    12→    "output": str,
    13→    "metadata": {
    14→      "skill": str,
    15→      "phi_score": float,
    16→      "mission": str,
    17→      "trajectory_12d": list[float]
    18→    }
    19→  }
    20→
    21→ExperienceCollector records use a flat schema with fields including:
    22→  phi_score, skill_name, mission_id, input_preview, trajectory, ...
    23→
    24→JourneyTaskReader normalises both schemas into a consistent task dict with
    25→top-level ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, and ``output``.
    26→"""
    27→
    28→from __future__ import annotations
    29→
    30→import json
    31→import logging
    32→from pathlib import Path
    33→from typing import Any
    34→
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→_DEFAULT_PATH = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
    39→
    40→
    41→class JourneyTaskReader:
    42→    """Reads journey data as task dicts for AgentJet training.
    43→
    44→    Loads from ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` when available;
    45→    falls back to ``ExperienceCollector`` when the file does not exist or
    46→    cannot be read.
    47→
    48→    Parameters
    49→    ----------
    50→    jsonl_path : Path, optional
    51→        Path to the JSONL file produced by ``JourneyToFinetuneConverter``.
    52→        Defaults to ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl``.
    53→    """
    54→
    55→    DEFAULT_PATH: Path = _DEFAULT_PATH
    56→
    57→    def __init__(self, jsonl_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    58→        self._jsonl_path: Path = jsonl_path if jsonl_path is not None else self.DEFAULT_PATH
    59→
    60→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→    # Public API
    62→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→    def read(
    65→        self,
    66→        skill_filter: str | None = None,
    67→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    68→        max_samples: int = 1000,
    69→    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    70→        """Load tasks, optionally filtered by skill name and minimum phi_score.
    71→
    72→        Parameters
    73→        ----------
    74→        skill_filter : str, optional
    75→            If provided, only tasks whose ``skill_name`` matches this string
    76→            (case-insensitive) are returned.
    77→        min_phi : float
    78→            Minimum phi_score threshold. Tasks below this value are excluded.
    79→        max_samples : int
    80→            Maximum number of tasks to return.
    81→
    82→        Returns
    83→        -------
    84→        list[dict]
    85→            Normalised task dicts, each containing at minimum:
    86→            ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, ``output``.
    87→        """
    88→        records = self._load_records()
    89→
    90→        filtered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    91→        for rec in records:
    92→            phi = float(rec.get("phi_score", 0.0))
    93→            if phi < min_phi:
    94→                continue
    95→            if skill_filter is not None:
    96→                skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "")).lower()
    97→                if skill != skill_filter.lower():
    98→                    continue
    99→            filtered.append(rec)
   100→            if len(filtered) >= max_samples:
   101→                break
   102→
   103→        logger.info(
   104→            "JourneyTaskReader.read: %d/%d records pass filters (skill_filter=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   105→            len(filtered),
   106→            len(records),
   107→            skill_filter,
   108→            min_phi,
   109→        )
   110→        return filtered
   111→
   112→    def group_by_skill(self, min_phi: float = 0.7) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   113→        """Group tasks by skill name, filtered by minimum phi_score.
   114→
   115→        Parameters
   116→        ----------
   117→        min_phi : float
   118→            Only tasks with phi_score >= min_phi are included.
   119→
   120→        Returns
   121→        -------
   122→        dict[str, list[dict]]
   123→            Mapping of skill_name → list of task dicts.
   124→        """
   125→        records = self.read(min_phi=min_phi, max_samples=100_000)
   126→        groups: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
   127→        for rec in records:
   128→            skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "unknown"))
   129→            groups.setdefault(skill, []).append(rec)
   130→
   131→        logger.info(
   132→            "JourneyTaskReader.group_by_skill: %d skills, %d total tasks (min_phi=%.2f)",
   133→            len(groups),
   134→            sum(len(v) for v in groups.values()),
   135→            min_phi,
   136→        )
   137→        return groups
   138→
   139→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   140→    # Private loaders
   141→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→
   143→    def _load_records(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   144→        """Load records from JSONL or fall back to ExperienceCollector."""
   145→        if self._jsonl_path.exists():
   146→            try:
   147→                return self._load_from_jsonl()
   148→            except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
   149→                logger.warning(
   150→                    "Failed to load JSONL from %s (%s), falling back to collector",
   151→                    self._jsonl_path,
   152→                    exc,
   153→                )
   154→        else:
   155→            logger.debug(
   156→                "JSONL file %s not found, using ExperienceCollector fallback",
   157→                self._jsonl_path,
   158→            )
   159→
   160→        return self._load_from_collector()
   161→
   162→    def _load_from_jsonl(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   163→        """Parse JSONL produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
   164→
   165→        Each line is a JSON object with schema::
   166→
   167→            {
   168→              "instruction": str,
   169→              "output": str,
   170→              "metadata": {"skill": str, "phi_score": float, ...}
   171→            }
   172→
   173→        Returns
   174→        -------
   175→        list[dict]
   176→            Normalised records with top-level ``phi_score`` and ``skill_name``.
   177→
   178→        Raises
   179→        ------
   180→        OSError
   181→            If the file cannot be opened.
   182→        json.JSONDecodeError
   183→            If a line contains invalid JSON.
   184→        """
   185→        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   186→        with self._jsonl_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   187→            for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   188→                line = line.strip()
   189→                if not line:
   190→                    continue
   191→                try:
   192→                    raw: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(line)
   193→                except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   194→                    raise json.JSONDecodeError(
   195→                        f"Line {lineno}: {exc.msg}", exc.doc, exc.pos
   196→                    ) from exc
   197→
   198→                records.append(self._normalise_jsonl_record(raw))
   199→
   200→        logger.debug("Loaded %d records from %s", len(records), self._jsonl_path)
   201→        return records
   202→
   203→    def _load_from_collector(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   204→        """Load experiences via ExperienceCollector (three-tier fallback).
   205→
   206→        Returns
   207→        -------
   208→        list[dict]
   209→            Normalised records. Returns an empty list if collector is
   210→            unavailable or raises an unexpected exception.
   211→        """
   212→        try:
   213→            from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector
   214→
   215→            collector = ExperienceCollector()
   216→            raw_records = collector.collect_all(max_samples=100_000)
   217→        except ImportError as exc:
   218→            logger.warning("ExperienceCollector import failed: %s", exc)
   219→            return []
   220→        except Exception as exc:
   221→            logger.warning(
   222→                "ExperienceCollector.collect_all() failed (non-blocking): %s",
   223→                exc,
   224→                exc_info=True,
   225→            )
   226→            return []
   227→
   228→        records = [self._normalise_collector_record(r) for r in raw_records]
   229→        logger.debug("Loaded %d records from ExperienceCollector", len(records))
   230→        return records
   231→
   232→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→    # Schema normalisation
   234→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→
   236→    @staticmethod
   237→    def _normalise_jsonl_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238→        """Normalise a JSONL record to the canonical task dict schema.
   239→
   240→        Parameters
   241→        ----------
   242→        raw : dict
   243→            Raw JSON object from finetune_journeys.jsonl.
   244→
   245→        Returns
   246→        -------
   247→        dict
   248→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   249→        """
   250→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = raw.get("metadata", {})
   251→        return {
   252→            "instruction": raw.get("instruction", ""),
   253→            "output": raw.get("output", ""),
   254→            "phi_score": float(metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   255→            "skill_name": str(metadata.get("skill", "unknown")),
   256→            "mission_id": str(metadata.get("mission", "")),
   257→            "trajectory_12d": metadata.get("trajectory_12d", []),
   258→            # Preserve original metadata for downstream consumers
   259→            "metadata": metadata,
   260→        }
   261→
   262→    @staticmethod
   263→    def _normalise_collector_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   264→        """Normalise an ExperienceCollector record to the canonical task dict schema.
   265→
   266→        Parameters
   267→        ----------
   268→        raw : dict
   269→            Flat experience dict from ExperienceCollector.collect_all().
   270→
   271→        Returns
   272→        -------
   273→        dict
   274→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   275→        """
   276→        return {
   277→            "instruction": str(raw.get("input_preview", "")),
   278→            "output": "",  # Raw collector records may not have formatted output
   279→            "phi_score": float(raw.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   280→            "skill_name": str(raw.get("skill_name", "unknown")),
   281→            "mission_id": str(raw.get("mission_id", "")),
   282→            "trajectory_12d": raw.get("trajectory", [])[:12]
   283→            if isinstance(raw.get("trajectory"), list)
   284→            else [],
   285→            # Preserve all original fields for downstream consumers
   286→            "metadata": raw,
   287→        }
   288→

     1→"""CohezionWorkflow: wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet-compatible workflow.
     2→
     3→AgentJet calls run(task) which executes via CompoundExecutor and returns the
     4→output alongside a reward metadata dict containing phi_score, skill_name, and
     5→coherence. The phi_score is derived from JourneyTracker trajectory analysis.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import asyncio
    11→import logging
    12→from typing import Any
    13→
    14→from cohezion.compound.executor import CompoundExecutor
    15→from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→class CohezionWorkflow:
    22→    """Wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet Workflow.
    23→
    24→    AgentJet calls ``run(task)`` which delegates to CompoundExecutor and
    25→    tracks the execution trajectory via JourneyTracker to produce a phi_score
    26→    reward signal.
    27→
    28→    Parameters
    29→    ----------
    30→    executor : CompoundExecutor
    31→        Configured compound executor to delegate task execution to.
    32→    tracker : JourneyTracker, optional
    33→        Journey tracker for phi_score computation. A default instance is
    34→        created if not provided.
    35→    default_operation_type : str
    36→        Default operation type used when task dict omits ``operation_type``.
    37→        Must be one of: generate, analyze, search, transform, persist.
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    def __init__(
    41→        self,
    42→        executor: CompoundExecutor,
    43→        tracker: JourneyTracker | None = None,
    44→        default_operation_type: str = "generate",
    45→    ) -> None:
    46→        self._executor = executor
    47→        self._tracker = tracker if tracker is not None else JourneyTracker()
    48→        self._default_operation_type = default_operation_type
    49→
    50→    async def run(self, task: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    51→        """Execute a task and return output with phi_score reward metadata.
    52→
    53→        Parameters
    54→        ----------
    55→        task : dict
    56→            Task specification. Expected keys:
    57→
    58→            * ``description`` (str, required) — human-readable task description
    59→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill to apply (default: ``"general"``)
    60→            * ``operation_type`` (str) — operation type (default: ``"generate"``)
    61→            * ``project`` (str) — vault project scope (default: ``"cohezion"``)
    62→            * Any additional keys are forwarded as task context.
    63→
    64→        Returns
    65→        -------
    66→        tuple[str, dict]
    67→            ``(output, metadata)`` where metadata contains:
    68→
    69→            * ``phi_score`` (float) — trajectory quality [0.0, 1.0]
    70→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill used for execution
    71→            * ``coherence`` (float) — execution coherence [0.0, 1.0]
    72→            * ``success`` (bool) — whether execution succeeded
    73→            * ``duration_seconds`` (float) — wall-clock execution time
    74→        """
    75→        description: str = task.get("description", "")
    76→        skill_name: str = task.get("skill_name", "general")
    77→        operation_type: str = task.get("operation_type", self._default_operation_type)
    78→        project: str = task.get("project", "cohezion")
    79→
    80→        if not description:
    81→            logger.warning("CohezionWorkflow.run() called with empty task description")
    82→
    83→        # Build a synchronous execute function that captures task context.
    84→        # CompoundExecutor.execute_task is synchronous; it calls execute_fn
    85→        # internally in a sync context.
    86→        def _execute_fn() -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    87→            output_text = description  # Minimal fallback — executor enriches this.
    88→            exec_metrics: dict[str, Any] = {"coherence": 0.5}
    89→            return output_text, exec_metrics
    90→
    91→        logger.info(
    92→            "CohezionWorkflow.run: skill=%s operation=%s description=%r",
    93→            skill_name,
    94→            operation_type,
    95→            description[:80],
    96→        )
    97→
    98→        # Execute via CompoundExecutor (synchronous call wrapped in async context)
    99→        try:
   100→            result = await asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
   101→                None,
   102→                lambda: self._executor.execute_task(
   103→                    task_description=description,
   104→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   105→                    operation_type=operation_type,
   106→                    execute_fn=_execute_fn,
   107→                    project=project,
   108→                ),
   109→            )
   110→        except Exception as exc:
   111→            logger.error("CohezionWorkflow execution failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   112→            return (
   113→                f"[error] {exc}",
   114→                {
   115→                    "phi_score": 0.0,
   116→                    "skill_name": skill_name,
   117→                    "coherence": 0.0,
   118→                    "success": False,
   119→                    "duration_seconds": 0.0,
   120→                },
   121→            )
   122→
   123→        # Track execution trajectory to compute phi_score
   124→        try:
   125→            trajectory_point = self._tracker.track_execution(
   126→                execution_result=result,
   127→                task_description=description,
   128→                operation_type=operation_type,
   129→            )
   130→            phi_score: float = float(trajectory_point.metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0))
   131→            coherence: float = float(trajectory_point.coherence)
   132→        except Exception as exc:
   133→            logger.warning("Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   134→            phi_score = float(result.metrics.get("coherence", 0.0))
   135→            coherence = phi_score
   136→
   137→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = {
   138→            "phi_score": phi_score,
   139→            "skill_name": skill_name,
   140→            "coherence": coherence,
   141→            "success": result.success,
   142→            "duration_seconds": result.duration_seconds,
   143→        }
   144→
   145→        logger.debug(
   146→            "CohezionWorkflow.run complete: phi=%.3f coherence=%.3f success=%s",
   147→            phi_score,
   148→            coherence,
   149→            result.success,
   150→        )
   151→
   152→        return result.output, metadata
   153→

     1→"""Data-driven model tier optimization based on cross-session usage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import time
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from enum import Enum
     9→
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→# Tier name constants matching ModelTierPolicy enum values in model_pool_config.py
    14→_HOT = "hot"
    15→_WARM = "warm"
    16→_COLD = "cold"
    17→
    18→_TIER_ORDER = {_HOT: 2, _WARM: 1, _COLD: 0}
    19→
    20→
    21→class TierRecommendation(Enum):
    22→    PROMOTE = "promote"  # Move to higher tier (COLD→WARM, WARM→HOT)
    23→    DEMOTE = "demote"  # Move to lower tier
    24→    KEEP = "keep"  # No change
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class TierChange:
    29→    model_name: str
    30→    current_tier: str
    31→    recommended_tier: str
    32→    reason: str
    33→    recommendation: TierRecommendation
    34→    confidence: float  # 0.0 – 1.0
    35→
    36→
    37→class TierOptimizer:
    38→    """Usage-histogram-driven tier reassignment recommendations.
    39→
    40→    Rules applied in priority order:
    41→    1. Model used exclusively for training (never inference) → keep in COLD.
    42→    2. Model used >30 min/day avg AND appears in >50% of recent sessions → promote.
    43→    3. Model unused for >14 days → demote.
    44→    4. Otherwise → keep.
    45→    """
    46→
    47→    PROMOTE_THRESHOLD_HOURS: float = 0.5  # 30 min/day avg
    48→    PROMOTE_SESSION_PCT: float = 0.5  # 50% of sessions must have used it
    49→    DEMOTE_UNUSED_DAYS: int = 14
    50→
    51→    def recommend_tier_changes(
    52→        self,
    53→        usage_histogram: dict[str, float],  # model → hours/day (7-day avg)
    54→        session_records: list,  # list[SessionRecord]
    55→        current_tiers: dict[str, str],  # model → "hot"|"warm"|"cold"
    56→    ) -> list[TierChange]:
    57→        """Return a list of suggested tier changes.
    58→
    59→        Only models already present in current_tiers are evaluated.
    60→        """
    61→        changes: list[TierChange] = []
    62→
    63→        total_sessions = len(session_records)
    64→        # Build per-model session penetration set
    65→        session_per_model: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    66→        for record in session_records:
    67→            for ev in record.model_events:
    68→                session_per_model.setdefault(ev.model_name, set()).add(record.session_id)
    69→
    70→        # Build per-model task-type breakdown
    71→        inference_sessions: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    72→        training_sessions: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    73→        newest_use: dict[str, float] = {}
    74→        for record in session_records:
    75→            for ev in record.model_events:
    76→                m = ev.model_name
    77→                if ev.task_type == "inference":
    78→                    inference_sessions.setdefault(m, set()).add(record.session_id)
    79→                else:
    80→                    training_sessions.setdefault(m, set()).add(record.session_id)
    81→                newest_use[m] = max(newest_use.get(m, 0.0), ev.started_at)
    82→
    83→        for model, current_tier in current_tiers.items():
    84→            change = self._evaluate_model(
    85→                model=model,
    86→                current_tier=current_tier,
    87→                hours_per_day=usage_histogram.get(model, 0.0),
    88→                total_sessions=total_sessions,
    89→                sessions_used=len(session_per_model.get(model, set())),
    90→                has_inference=bool(inference_sessions.get(model)),
    91→                has_only_training=(
    92→                    bool(training_sessions.get(model)) and not inference_sessions.get(model)
    93→                ),
    94→                last_used_at=newest_use.get(model, 0.0),
    95→            )
    96→            if change is not None:
    97→                changes.append(change)
    98→
    99→        return changes
   100→
   101→    def _evaluate_model(
   102→        self,
   103→        model: str,
   104→        current_tier: str,
   105→        hours_per_day: float,
   106→        total_sessions: int,
   107→        sessions_used: int,
   108→        has_inference: bool,
   109→        has_only_training: bool,
   110→        last_used_at: float,
   111→    ) -> TierChange | None:
   112→        """Evaluate a single model and return a TierChange or None."""
   113→
   114→        # Rule 1: training-only models stay in COLD regardless of usage volume
   115→        if has_only_training:
   116→            if current_tier != _COLD:
   117→                return TierChange(
   118→                    model_name=model,
   119→                    current_tier=current_tier,
   120→                    recommended_tier=_COLD,
   121→                    reason="Model used exclusively for training; inference tier not warranted.",
   122→                    recommendation=TierRecommendation.DEMOTE,
   123→                    confidence=0.9,
   124→                )
   125→            return None  # Already COLD, keep
   126→
   127→        # Rule 2: high-traffic promotion candidate
   128→        session_pct = (sessions_used / total_sessions) if total_sessions > 0 else 0.0
   129→        if (
   130→            hours_per_day >= self.PROMOTE_THRESHOLD_HOURS
   131→            and session_pct >= self.PROMOTE_SESSION_PCT
   132→            and has_inference
   133→        ):
   134→            target = self._next_tier_up(current_tier)
   135→            if target != current_tier:
   136→                confidence = (
   137→                    min(
   138→                        1.0,
   139→                        (hours_per_day / self.PROMOTE_THRESHOLD_HOURS) * 0.5
   140→                        + (session_pct / self.PROMOTE_SESSION_PCT) * 0.5,
   141→                    )
   142→                    * 0.95
   143→                )
   144→                return TierChange(
   145→                    model_name=model,
   146→                    current_tier=current_tier,
   147→                    recommended_tier=target,
   148→                    reason=(
   149→                        f"High usage: {hours_per_day:.2f} h/day avg, "
   150→                        f"{session_pct:.0%} session penetration."
   151→                    ),
   152→                    recommendation=TierRecommendation.PROMOTE,
   153→                    confidence=round(confidence, 3),
   154→                )
   155→            return None  # Already HOT
   156→
   157→        # Rule 3: unused demotion
   158→        days_since_use = (time.time() - last_used_at) / 86400 if last_used_at > 0 else float("inf")
   159→        if days_since_use > self.DEMOTE_UNUSED_DAYS:
   160→            target = self._next_tier_down(current_tier)
   161→            if target != current_tier:
   162→                confidence = min(1.0, days_since_use / (self.DEMOTE_UNUSED_DAYS * 2))
   163→                return TierChange(
   164→                    model_name=model,
   165→                    current_tier=current_tier,
   166→                    recommended_tier=target,
   167→                    reason=(
   168→                        f"Unused for {days_since_use:.0f} days "
   169→                        f"(threshold: {self.DEMOTE_UNUSED_DAYS} days)."
   170→                    ),
   171→                    recommendation=TierRecommendation.DEMOTE,
   172→                    confidence=round(confidence, 3),
   173→                )
   174→
   175→        return None  # No change warranted
   176→
   177→    @staticmethod
   178→    def _next_tier_up(tier: str) -> str:
   179→        if tier == _COLD:
   180→            return _WARM
   181→        if tier == _WARM:
   182→            return _HOT
   183→        return _HOT
   184→
   185→    @staticmethod
   186→    def _next_tier_down(tier: str) -> str:
   187→        if tier == _HOT:
   188→            return _WARM
   189→        if tier == _WARM:
   190→            return _COLD
   191→        return _COLD
   192→

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
All 11 issues (C1–C3, I1–I7) are confirmed against the source. The highest-leverage fix: C1's `asyncio.wait_for()` used as a context manager. `asyncio.wait_for()` returns a coroutine, not an `_AsyncGeneratorContextManager`. The fix is to move the timeout into `aiohttp.ClientTimeout` — the same pattern `resource_manager.py` already uses correctly.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

     1→"""CohezionWorkflow: wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet-compatible workflow.
     2→
     3→AgentJet calls run(task) which executes via CompoundExecutor and returns the
     4→output alongside a reward metadata dict containing phi_score, skill_name, and
     5→coherence. The phi_score is derived from JourneyTracker trajectory analysis.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import asyncio
    11→import logging
    12→from typing import Any
    13→
    14→from cohezion.compound.executor import CompoundExecutor
    15→from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→class CohezionWorkflow:
    22→    """Wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet Workflow.
    23→
    24→    AgentJet calls ``run(task)`` which delegates to CompoundExecutor and
    25→    tracks the execution trajectory via JourneyTracker to produce a phi_score
    26→    reward signal.
    27→
    28→    Parameters
    29→    ----------
    30→    executor : CompoundExecutor
    31→        Configured compound executor to delegate task execution to.
    32→    tracker : JourneyTracker, optional
    33→        Journey tracker for phi_score computation. A default instance is
    34→        created if not provided.
    35→    default_operation_type : str
    36→        Default operation type used when task dict omits ``operation_type``.
    37→        Must be one of: generate, analyze, search, transform, persist.
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    def __init__(
    41→        self,
    42→        executor: CompoundExecutor,
    43→        tracker: JourneyTracker | None = None,
    44→        default_operation_type: str = "generate",
    45→    ) -> None:
    46→        self._executor = executor
    47→        self._tracker = tracker if tracker is not None else JourneyTracker()
    48→        self._default_operation_type = default_operation_type
    49→
    50→    async def run(self, task: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    51→        """Execute a task and return output with phi_score reward metadata.
    52→
    53→        Parameters
    54→        ----------
    55→        task : dict
    56→            Task specification. Expected keys:
    57→
    58→            * ``description`` (str, required) — human-readable task description
    59→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill to apply (default: ``"general"``)
    60→            * ``operation_type`` (str) — operation type (default: ``"generate"``)
    61→            * ``project`` (str) — vault project scope (default: ``"cohezion"``)
    62→            * Any additional keys are forwarded as task context.
    63→
    64→        Returns
    65→        -------
    66→        tuple[str, dict]
    67→            ``(output, metadata)`` where metadata contains:
    68→
    69→            * ``phi_score`` (float) — trajectory quality [0.0, 1.0]
    70→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill used for execution
    71→            * ``coherence`` (float) — execution coherence [0.0, 1.0]
    72→            * ``success`` (bool) — whether execution succeeded
    73→            * ``duration_seconds`` (float) — wall-clock execution time
    74→        """
    75→        description: str = task.get("description", "")
    76→        skill_name: str = task.get("skill_name", "general")
    77→        operation_type: str = task.get("operation_type", self._default_operation_type)
    78→        project: str = task.get("project", "cohezion")
    79→
    80→        if not description:
    81→            logger.warning("CohezionWorkflow.run() called with empty task description")
    82→
    83→        # Build a synchronous execute function that captures task context.
    84→        # CompoundExecutor.execute_task is synchronous; it calls execute_fn
    85→        # internally in a sync context.
    86→        def _execute_fn() -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    87→            output_text = description  # Minimal fallback — executor enriches this.
    88→            exec_metrics: dict[str, Any] = {"coherence": 0.5}
    89→            return output_text, exec_metrics
    90→
    91→        logger.info(
    92→            "CohezionWorkflow.run: skill=%s operation=%s description=%r",
    93→            skill_name,
    94→            operation_type,
    95→            description[:80],
    96→        )
    97→
    98→        # Execute via CompoundExecutor (synchronous call wrapped in async context)
    99→        try:
   100→            result = await asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
   101→                None,
   102→                lambda: self._executor.execute_task(
   103→                    task_description=description,
   104→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   105→                    operation_type=operation_type,
   106→                    execute_fn=_execute_fn,
   107→                    project=project,
   108→                ),
   109→            )
   110→        except Exception as exc:
   111→            logger.error("CohezionWorkflow execution failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   112→            return (
   113→                f"[error] {exc}",
   114→                {
   115→                    "phi_score": 0.0,
   116→                    "skill_name": skill_name,
   117→                    "coherence": 0.0,
   118→                    "success": False,
   119→                    "duration_seconds": 0.0,
   120→                },
   121→            )
   122→
   123→        # Track execution trajectory to compute phi_score
   124→        try:
   125→            trajectory_point = self._tracker.track_execution(
   126→                execution_result=result,
   127→                task_description=description,
   128→                operation_type=operation_type,
   129→            )
   130→            phi_score: float = float(trajectory_point.metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0))
   131→            coherence: float = float(trajectory_point.coherence)
   132→        except Exception as exc:
   133→            logger.warning("Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   134→            phi_score = float(result.metrics.get("coherence", 0.0))
   135→            coherence = phi_score
   136→
   137→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = {
   138→            "phi_score": phi_score,
   139→            "skill_name": skill_name,
   140→            "coherence": coherence,
   141→            "success": result.success,
   142→            "duration_seconds": result.duration_seconds,
   143→        }
   144→
   145→        logger.debug(
   146→            "CohezionWorkflow.run complete: phi=%.3f coherence=%.3f success=%s",
   147→            phi_score,
   148→            coherence,
   149→            result.success,
   150→        )
   151→
   152→        return result.output, metadata
   153→

     1→"""JourneyTaskReader: loads training tasks from journey JSONL or ExperienceCollector.
     2→
     3→Primary source: ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` produced by
     4→``JourneyToFinetuneConverter.run()``.
     5→
     6→Fallback source: ``ExperienceCollector.collect_all()`` which pulls from
     7→Parquet shards, SurrealDB, and vault experiment JSON files.
     8→
     9→JSONL record schema (from JourneyToFinetuneConverter._to_training_pairs):
    10→  {
    11→    "instruction": str,
    12→    "output": str,
    13→    "metadata": {
    14→      "skill": str,
    15→      "phi_score": float,
    16→      "mission": str,
    17→      "trajectory_12d": list[float]
    18→    }
    19→  }
    20→
    21→ExperienceCollector records use a flat schema with fields including:
    22→  phi_score, skill_name, mission_id, input_preview, trajectory, ...
    23→
    24→JourneyTaskReader normalises both schemas into a consistent task dict with
    25→top-level ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, and ``output``.
    26→"""
    27→
    28→from __future__ import annotations
    29→
    30→import json
    31→import logging
    32→from pathlib import Path
    33→from typing import Any
    34→
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→_DEFAULT_PATH = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
    39→
    40→
    41→class JourneyTaskReader:
    42→    """Reads journey data as task dicts for AgentJet training.
    43→
    44→    Loads from ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` when available;
    45→    falls back to ``ExperienceCollector`` when the file does not exist or
    46→    cannot be read.
    47→
    48→    Parameters
    49→    ----------
    50→    jsonl_path : Path, optional
    51→        Path to the JSONL file produced by ``JourneyToFinetuneConverter``.
    52→        Defaults to ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl``.
    53→    """
    54→
    55→    DEFAULT_PATH: Path = _DEFAULT_PATH
    56→
    57→    def __init__(self, jsonl_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    58→        self._jsonl_path: Path = jsonl_path if jsonl_path is not None else self.DEFAULT_PATH
    59→
    60→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→    # Public API
    62→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→    def read(
    65→        self,
    66→        skill_filter: str | None = None,
    67→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    68→        max_samples: int = 1000,
    69→    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    70→        """Load tasks, optionally filtered by skill name and minimum phi_score.
    71→
    72→        Parameters
    73→        ----------
    74→        skill_filter : str, optional
    75→            If provided, only tasks whose ``skill_name`` matches this string
    76→            (case-insensitive) are returned.
    77→        min_phi : float
    78→            Minimum phi_score threshold. Tasks below this value are excluded.
    79→        max_samples : int
    80→            Maximum number of tasks to return.
    81→
    82→        Returns
    83→        -------
    84→        list[dict]
    85→            Normalised task dicts, each containing at minimum:
    86→            ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, ``output``.
    87→        """
    88→        records = self._load_records()
    89→
    90→        filtered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    91→        for rec in records:
    92→            phi = float(rec.get("phi_score", 0.0))
    93→            if phi < min_phi:
    94→                continue
    95→            if skill_filter is not None:
    96→                skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "")).lower()
    97→                if skill != skill_filter.lower():
    98→                    continue
    99→            filtered.append(rec)
   100→            if len(filtered) >= max_samples:
   101→                break
   102→
   103→        logger.info(
   104→            "JourneyTaskReader.read: %d/%d records pass filters (skill_filter=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   105→            len(filtered),
   106→            len(records),
   107→            skill_filter,
   108→            min_phi,
   109→        )
   110→        return filtered
   111→
   112→    def group_by_skill(self, min_phi: float = 0.7) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   113→        """Group tasks by skill name, filtered by minimum phi_score.
   114→
   115→        Parameters
   116→        ----------
   117→        min_phi : float
   118→            Only tasks with phi_score >= min_phi are included.
   119→
   120→        Returns
   121→        -------
   122→        dict[str, list[dict]]
   123→            Mapping of skill_name → list of task dicts.
   124→        """
   125→        records = self.read(min_phi=min_phi, max_samples=100_000)
   126→        groups: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
   127→        for rec in records:
   128→            skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "unknown"))
   129→            groups.setdefault(skill, []).append(rec)
   130→
   131→        logger.info(
   132→            "JourneyTaskReader.group_by_skill: %d skills, %d total tasks (min_phi=%.2f)",
   133→            len(groups),
   134→            sum(len(v) for v in groups.values()),
   135→            min_phi,
   136→        )
   137→        return groups
   138→
   139→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   140→    # Private loaders
   141→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→
   143→    def _load_records(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   144→        """Load records from JSONL or fall back to ExperienceCollector."""
   145→        if self._jsonl_path.exists():
   146→            try:
   147→                return self._load_from_jsonl()
   148→            except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
   149→                logger.warning(
   150→                    "Failed to load JSONL from %s (%s), falling back to collector",
   151→                    self._jsonl_path,
   152→                    exc,
   153→                )
   154→        else:
   155→            logger.debug(
   156→                "JSONL file %s not found, using ExperienceCollector fallback",
   157→                self._jsonl_path,
   158→            )
   159→
   160→        return self._load_from_collector()
   161→
   162→    def _load_from_jsonl(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   163→        """Parse JSONL produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
   164→
   165→        Each line is a JSON object with schema::
   166→
   167→            {
   168→              "instruction": str,
   169→              "output": str,
   170→              "metadata": {"skill": str, "phi_score": float, ...}
   171→            }
   172→
   173→        Returns
   174→        -------
   175→        list[dict]
   176→            Normalised records with top-level ``phi_score`` and ``skill_name``.
   177→
   178→        Raises
   179→        ------
   180→        OSError
   181→            If the file cannot be opened.
   182→        json.JSONDecodeError
   183→            If a line contains invalid JSON.
   184→        """
   185→        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   186→        with self._jsonl_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   187→            for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   188→                line = line.strip()
   189→                if not line:
   190→                    continue
   191→                try:
   192→                    raw: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(line)
   193→                except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   194→                    raise json.JSONDecodeError(
   195→                        f"Line {lineno}: {exc.msg}", exc.doc, exc.pos
   196→                    ) from exc
   197→
   198→                records.append(self._normalise_jsonl_record(raw))
   199→
   200→        logger.debug("Loaded %d records from %s", len(records), self._jsonl_path)
   201→        return records
   202→
   203→    def _load_from_collector(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   204→        """Load experiences via ExperienceCollector (three-tier fallback).
   205→
   206→        Returns
   207→        -------
   208→        list[dict]
   209→            Normalised records. Returns an empty list if collector is
   210→            unavailable or raises an unexpected exception.
   211→        """
   212→        try:
   213→            from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector
   214→
   215→            collector = ExperienceCollector()
   216→            raw_records = collector.collect_all(max_samples=100_000)
   217→        except ImportError as exc:
   218→            logger.warning("ExperienceCollector import failed: %s", exc)
   219→            return []
   220→        except Exception as exc:
   221→            logger.warning(
   222→                "ExperienceCollector.collect_all() failed (non-blocking): %s",
   223→                exc,
   224→                exc_info=True,
   225→            )
   226→            return []
   227→
   228→        records = [self._normalise_collector_record(r) for r in raw_records]
   229→        logger.debug("Loaded %d records from ExperienceCollector", len(records))
   230→        return records
   231→
   232→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→    # Schema normalisation
   234→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→
   236→    @staticmethod
   237→    def _normalise_jsonl_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238→        """Normalise a JSONL record to the canonical task dict schema.
   239→
   240→        Parameters
   241→        ----------
   242→        raw : dict
   243→            Raw JSON object from finetune_journeys.jsonl.
   244→
   245→        Returns
   246→        -------
   247→        dict
   248→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   249→        """
   250→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = raw.get("metadata", {})
   251→        return {
   252→            "instruction": raw.get("instruction", ""),
   253→            "output": raw.get("output", ""),
   254→            "phi_score": float(metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   255→            "skill_name": str(metadata.get("skill", "unknown")),
   256→            "mission_id": str(metadata.get("mission", "")),
   257→            "trajectory_12d": metadata.get("trajectory_12d", []),
   258→            # Preserve original metadata for downstream consumers
   259→            "metadata": metadata,
   260→        }
   261→
   262→    @staticmethod
   263→    def _normalise_collector_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   264→        """Normalise an ExperienceCollector record to the canonical task dict schema.
   265→
   266→        Parameters
   267→        ----------
   268→        raw : dict
   269→            Flat experience dict from ExperienceCollector.collect_all().
   270→
   271→        Returns
   272→        -------
   273→        dict
   274→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   275→        """
   276→        return {
   277→            "instruction": str(raw.get("input_preview", "")),
   278→            "output": "",  # Raw collector records may not have formatted output
   279→            "phi_score": float(raw.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   280→            "skill_name": str(raw.get("skill_name", "unknown")),
   281→            "mission_id": str(raw.get("mission_id", "")),
   282→            "trajectory_12d": raw.get("trajectory", [])[:12]
   283→            if isinstance(raw.get("trajectory"), list)
   284→            else [],
   285→            # Preserve all original fields for downstream consumers
   286→            "metadata": raw,
   287→        }
   288→

     1→"""Unsloth Studio integration for Phase 2 QLoRA training.
     2→
     3→Available NOW:  Data Recipes API (convert vault → dataset).
     4→Available SOON: QLoRA training (when AMD ROCm support ships in Unsloth).
     5→
     6→Phase 1 (current): Falls back to LocalFinetuner (llama.cpp / llamafactory).
     7→Phase 2 (planned): QLoRA via Unsloth Studio once AMD support is confirmed.
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from __future__ import annotations
    11→
    12→import json
    13→import logging
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→import aiohttp
    18→
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL = "https://studio.unsloth.ai"
    23→
    24→# Data Recipes endpoint (available now — no AMD dependency)
    25→_RECIPES_ENDPOINT = "/api/v1/recipes"
    26→
    27→
    28→class UnslothBridge:
    29→    """Interface to Unsloth Studio for dataset preparation and QLoRA training.
    30→
    31→    Phases
    32→    ------
    33→    Phase 1 (active): LocalFinetuner fallback — llama.cpp / llamafactory config.
    34→    Phase 2 (pending): QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio when AMD support ships.
    35→    Data Recipes (active): ``create_dataset_from_vault`` is available now and
    36→    calls the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API to convert vault notes to JSONL.
    37→
    38→    Parameters
    39→    ----------
    40→    api_key : str, optional
    41→        Unsloth Studio API key. If None, Data Recipes API calls will be
    42→        attempted without auth (may fail for private endpoints).
    43→    studio_url : str
    44→        Base URL for the Unsloth Studio API.
    45→    """
    46→
    47→    def __init__(
    48→        self,
    49→        api_key: str | None = None,
    50→        studio_url: str = UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL,
    51→    ) -> None:
    52→        self._api_key = api_key
    53→        self._studio_url = studio_url.rstrip("/")
    54→
    55→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→    # Capability probes
    57→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→
    59→    def is_training_available(self) -> bool:
    60→        """Return True if Unsloth QLoRA training is supported on current hardware.
    61→
    62→        Currently returns False because Unsloth Studio QLoRA training requires
    63→        CUDA (NVIDIA) and AMD ROCm support has not shipped yet.  When AMD support
    64→        arrives this method should be updated to probe the runtime.
    65→        """
    66→        return False
    67→
    68→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→    # Data Recipes (available now)
    70→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    71→
    72→    async def create_dataset_from_vault(
    73→        self,
    74→        vault_query: str,
    75→        output_path: Path | None = None,
    76→    ) -> Path:
    77→        """Convert vault notes to a JSONL training dataset via Unsloth Data Recipes.
    78→
    79→        Attempts to call the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API.  If the API is
    80→        unavailable (no key, network error, or endpoint not reachable) the
    81→        method falls back to reading the local finetune_journeys.jsonl file
    82→        produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
    83→
    84→        Parameters
    85→        ----------
    86→        vault_query : str
    87→            Search query used to select relevant vault notes (forwarded to the
    88→            Data Recipes API as the ``query`` field).
    89→        output_path : Path, optional
    90→            Where to write the output JSONL.  Defaults to
    91→            ``data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl``.
    92→
    93→        Returns
    94→        -------
    95→        Path
    96→            Absolute path to the generated JSONL dataset file.
    97→        """
    98→        out = (
    99→            output_path if output_path is not None else Path("data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl")
   100→        )
   101→        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   102→
   103→        # Try Unsloth Data Recipes API first
   104→        api_data = await self._call_data_recipes_api(vault_query)
   105→        if api_data is not None:
   106→            logger.info(
   107→                "UnslothBridge: received %d records from Data Recipes API",
   108→                len(api_data),
   109→            )
   110→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   111→                for record in api_data:
   112→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   113→            logger.info("UnslothBridge: dataset written to %s", out)
   114→            return out
   115→
   116→        # Fallback: copy local finetune_journeys.jsonl
   117→        local_source = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
   118→        if local_source.exists():
   119→            logger.info(
   120→                "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unavailable; copying %s → %s",
   121→                local_source,
   122→                out,
   123→            )
   124→            records = _read_jsonl(local_source)
   125→            # Filter records loosely by vault_query in instruction/output text
   126→            if vault_query:
   127→                query_lower = vault_query.lower()
   128→                filtered = [
   129→                    r
   130→                    for r in records
   131→                    if query_lower in str(r.get("instruction", "")).lower()
   132→                    or query_lower in str(r.get("output", "")).lower()
   133→                ]
   134→                # If filter is too aggressive, keep all records
   135→                if not filtered:
   136→                    filtered = records
   137→            else:
   138→                filtered = records
   139→
   140→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   141→                for record in filtered:
   142→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   143→            logger.info(
   144→                "UnslothBridge: fallback dataset written (%d records) to %s",
   145→                len(filtered),
   146→                out,
   147→            )
   148→            return out
   149→
   150→        # Last resort: empty dataset with a warning
   151→        logger.warning(
   152→            "UnslothBridge: no data source available (API down, no local JSONL). "
   153→            "Writing empty dataset to %s",
   154→            out,
   155→        )
   156→        out.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
   157→        return out
   158→
   159→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   160→    # QLoRA training (Phase 2 — not yet available on AMD)
   161→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   162→
   163→    async def train_qlora(
   164→        self,
   165→        dataset: Path,
   166→        model: str,
   167→        config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   168→    ) -> Path | None:
   169→        """Attempt QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio.
   170→
   171→        Returns None when AMD training is not yet supported (current state).
   172→        Phase 2 will implement full QLoRA when Unsloth ships AMD ROCm support.
   173→
   174→        Parameters
   175→        ----------
   176→        dataset : Path
   177→            Path to the JSONL training dataset (e.g., from create_dataset_from_vault).
   178→        model : str
   179→            Base model identifier (e.g., "qwen3.5:9b").
   180→        config : dict, optional
   181→            Additional training configuration forwarded to the Unsloth API.
   182→
   183→        Returns
   184→        -------
   185→        Path | None
   186→            Path to the trained model checkpoint, or None if training is
   187→            unavailable on the current hardware.
   188→        """
   189→        if not self.is_training_available():
   190→            logger.info(
   191→                "UnslothBridge.train_qlora: AMD QLoRA not available yet (Phase 2). "
   192→                "Falling back to LocalFinetuner for %s.",
   193→                model,
   194→            )
   195→            return await self._llamafactory_fallback(dataset, model, config)
   196→
   197→        # Phase 2 placeholder — implement when AMD Unsloth support ships
   198→        raise NotImplementedError(
   199→            "Unsloth QLoRA training on AMD is not yet implemented. "
   200→            "Set is_training_available() → True once AMD support ships."
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   204→    # Private helpers
   205→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   206→
   207→    async def _call_data_recipes_api(self, vault_query: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:
   208→        """Call Unsloth Studio Data Recipes endpoint.
   209→
   210→        Returns the parsed list of records on success, or None on any failure.
   211→        """
   212→        url = f"{self._studio_url}{_RECIPES_ENDPOINT}"
   213→        headers: dict[str, str] = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   214→        if self._api_key:
   215→            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self._api_key}"
   216→
   217→        payload = {"query": vault_query, "format": "jsonl"}
   218→
   219→        try:
   220→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session, session.post(
   221→                url,
   222→                json=payload,
   223→                headers=headers,
   224→                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=30.0),
   225→            ) as resp:
   226→                if resp.status == 200:
   227→                    data = await resp.json()
   228→                    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = data.get("records", [])
   229→                    return records
   230→                logger.debug(
   231→                    "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API returned HTTP %d — using fallback",
   232→                    resp.status,
   233→                )
   234→                return None
   235→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   236→            logger.debug("UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unreachable: %s", exc)
   237→            return None
   238→
   239→    async def _llamafactory_fallback(
   240→        self,
   241→        dataset: Path,
   242→        model: str,
   243→        config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   244→    ) -> Path | None:
   245→        """Generate a llamafactory training config as a Phase 2 fallback."""
   246→        import asyncio
   247→
   248→        try:
   249→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   250→
   251→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   252→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   253→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   254→                "phi4": "phi4",
   255→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   256→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   257→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   258→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   259→            }
   260→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   261→            epochs = int((config or {}).get("epochs", 3))
   262→
   263→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name="cohezion_unsloth_phase2")
   264→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   265→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   266→                None,
   267→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   268→            )
   269→            logger.info("UnslothBridge: llamafactory fallback config written to %s", config_path)
   270→            return config_path
   271→        except Exception as exc:
   272→            logger.error("UnslothBridge._llamafactory_fallback failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   273→            return None
   274→
   275→
   276→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   277→# Module-level helpers
   278→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   279→
   280→
   281→def _read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   282→    """Read a JSONL file and return a list of parsed dicts. Skips invalid lines."""
   283→    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   284→    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   285→        for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   286→            line = line.strip()
   287→            if not line:
   288→                continue
   289→            try:
   290→                records.append(json.loads(line))
   291→            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   292→                logger.warning(
   293→                    "_read_jsonl: skipping invalid JSON at line %d in %s: %s",
   294→                    lineno,
   295→                    path,
   296→                    exc,
   297→                )
   298→    return records
   299→

  1567→async def agentjet_train(request: TrainRequest) -> TrainResponse:
  1568→    """Start an AgentJet CALL training cycle."""
  1569→    try:
  1570→        from cohezion.agentjet.trainer import AgentJetTrainer
  1571→
  1572→        trainer = AgentJetTrainer()
  1573→        result = await trainer.train(
  1574→            target_model=request.target_model,
  1575→            skill_domain=request.skill_domain,
  1576→            epochs=request.epochs,
  1577→            min_phi=request.min_phi,
  1578→            dry_run=request.dry_run,
  1579→        )
  1580→        return TrainResponse(
  1581→            success=result.success,
  1582→            model_name=result.model_name,
  1583→            base_model=result.base_model,
  1584→            skill_domain=result.skill_domain,
  1585→            epochs_completed=result.epochs_completed,
  1586→            samples_used=result.samples_used,
  1587→            avg_reward=result.avg_reward,
  1588→            training_duration_s=result.training_duration_s,
  1589→            dry_run=result.dry_run,
  1590→            error=result.error,
  1591→        )
  1592→    except Exception as e:
  1593→        return TrainResponse(
  1594→            success=False,
  1595→            model_name="",
  1596→            base_model=request.target_model,
  1597→            skill_domain=request.skill_domain,

  1607→@app.get("/agentjet/status")
  1608→async def agentjet_status() -> dict:
  1609→    """Get AgentJet CALL system status."""
  1610→    try:
  1611→        from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager
  1612→
  1613→        mgr = OllamaContextManager()
  1614→        available_gb = await mgr.get_available_memory_gb()
  1615→        loaded_models = await mgr.get_loaded_models()
  1616→        return {

  1619→            "loaded_models": loaded_models,
  1620→            "backend": "llamafactory",  # Phase 1 default
  1621→        }
  1622→    except Exception as e:
  1623→        return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
  1624→
  1625→
  1626→@app.get("/agentjet/models")
  1627→async def agentjet_models() -> dict:
  1628→    """List available training target models."""
  1629→    from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import CONTEXT_PROFILES

  1631→    targets = [

  1638→        "recommended": ["qwen3.5:9b", "nemotron-3-nano:30b", "phi3:mini"],
  1639→    }

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The `asyncio.wait_for()` pitfall is subtle: it wraps a coroutine to add a timeout, returning another coroutine — not an async context manager. `aiohttp.ClientSession.get/post()` returns a native async context manager, so passing a `timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(...)` argument is the correct fix. The existing `resource_manager.py` already uses this pattern correctly, making it a good reference.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Starting with `context_optimizer.py` (C1 blocking fix + I1/I3/I6):

  "new_string": "@app.post(\"/agentjet/train\")\nasync def agentjet_train(request: TrainRequest) -> TrainResponse:\n    \"\"\"Start an AgentJet CALL training cycle.\"\"\"\n    from fastapi import HTTPException\n\n    try:\n        from cohezion.agentjet.trainer import AgentJetTrainer\n\n        trainer = AgentJetTrainer()\n        result = await trainer.train(\n            target_model=request.target_model,\n            skill_domain=request.skill_domain,\n            epochs=request.epochs,\n            min_phi=request.min_phi,\n            dry_run=request.dry_run,\n        )\n        return TrainResponse(\n            success=result.success,\n            model_name=result.model_name,\n            base_model=result.base_model,\n            skill_domain=result.skill_domain,\n            epochs_completed=result.epochs_completed,\n            samples_used=result.samples_used,\n            avg_reward=result.avg_reward,\n            training_duration_s=result.training_duration_s,\n            dry_run=result.dry_run,\n            error=result.error,\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        # OOM or resource contention — tell the caller to back off and retry\n        _oom_names = (\"OOMRiskError\", \"ResourceUnavailableError\")\n        if type(e).__name__ in _oom_names:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(e)) from e\n        return TrainResponse(\n            success=False,\n            model_name=\"\",\n            base_model=request.target_model,\n            skill_domain=request.skill_domain,\n            epochs_completed=0,\n            samples_used=0,\n            avg_reward=0.0,\n            training_duration_s=0.0,\n            dry_run=request.dry_run,\n            error=str(e),\n        )"

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The systemd `%h` specifier expands to the user's home directory at unit load time, and `%i` is the instance name from `systemctl enable cohezion-resources@mike-anderson`. This makes the unit portable across machines while still being user-scoped. The `WorkingDirectory=%h/dev/cohezion` assumption (same repo path) is still hardcoded, but is a reasonable convention — a more complete fix would add an `Environment=COHEZION_ROOT=...` variable and use `EnvironmentFile=` for site-specific overrides.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

# Pre-commit hooks for code quality enforcement
# Install: pip install pre-commit && pre-commit install
# Run manually: pre-commit run --all-files
#
# Hook stages:
#   - [commit] (fast): Runs on `git commit` - ~3-5s for quick feedback
#   - [push] (safety): Runs on `git push` - large files and secrets only (linting in CI)
#
# Usage:
#   pre-commit run --all-files                   # Run all hooks, all stages
#   pre-commit run --all-files --hook-stage=commit # Run only fast checks

repos:
  # ============================================================================
  # FAST CHECKS (commit stage) - Quick syntax and formatting validation
  # ============================================================================

  # Ruff - Fast Python formatter and quick linter
  - repo: https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff-pre-commit
    rev: v0.15.0
    hooks:
      # Format check (no auto-fix on commit, just warn)
      - id: ruff-format
        stages: [pre-commit]
        args: [--check]
      # Quick lint - only basic syntax errors and auto-fixes
      - id: ruff
        stages: [pre-commit]
        args: [--select=F,E9,E501, --fix]
        name: ruff-quick (syntax errors only)
---
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": "",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command",
            "command": "entire hooks claude-code session-start"
          }
        ]
      }
--
        "hooks": [
          {
            "type": "command",
            "command": "entire hooks claude-code session-end"
          }
        ]
      }
--
