     1→"""Per-model context profiles and OOM-safe Ollama lifecycle management."""
     2→from __future__ import annotations
     3→
     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→import aiohttp
     9→
    10→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    11→
    12→OLLAMA_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:11434"
    13→TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB = 128.0
    14→SAFETY_BUFFER_GB = 10.0
    15→OS_OVERHEAD_GB = 8.0
    16→
    17→_DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS = ["phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", "nomic-embed-text:latest"]
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class ModelContextProfile:
    22→    """Per-model Ollama context settings to prevent KV-cache OOM."""
    23→
    24→    model_name: str
    25→    num_ctx: int  # Context window (KV cache driver)
    26→    num_gpu: int = -1  # GPU layers (-1 = all)
    27→    flash_attention: bool = True  # Reduces KV cache ~50%
    28→    rope_scaling: str = "linear"  # "linear"|"ntk"|"yarn"
    29→    num_parallel: int = 1  # Concurrent requests
    30→    keep_alive: str = "5m"  # How long to keep loaded ("0" = unload immediately)
    31→    size_gb: float = 0.0  # Approximate model size in GB (for OOM checks)
    32→
    33→
    34→def _mk(model_name: str, num_ctx: int, size_gb: float, flash_attention: bool = True, **kw: object) -> ModelContextProfile:
    35→    return ModelContextProfile(model_name=model_name, num_ctx=num_ctx, size_gb=size_gb, flash_attention=flash_attention, **kw)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    36→
    37→
    38→def _training(base: ModelContextProfile) -> ModelContextProfile:
    39→    """Return a copy of a profile with training overrides (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
    40→    return ModelContextProfile(
    41→        model_name=base.model_name,
    42→        num_ctx=2048,
    43→        num_gpu=base.num_gpu,
    44→        flash_attention=base.flash_attention,
    45→        rope_scaling=base.rope_scaling,
    46→        num_parallel=base.num_parallel,
    47→        keep_alive="0",
    48→        size_gb=base.size_gb,
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→# Per-model context profiles
    54→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55→_BASE_PROFILES: list[ModelContextProfile] = [
    56→    # Routing models (0.8B–2B): 32768 ctx
    57→    _mk("qwen3.5:0.8b", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=1.0, flash_attention=True),
    58→    _mk("phi4-mini-reasoning:latest", num_ctx=32768, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    59→    _mk("nomic-embed-text:latest", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=0.5, flash_attention=False),
    60→    # Execution models (9B–30B): 16384 ctx
    61→    _mk("qwen3.5:9b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    62→    _mk("nemotron-3-nano:30b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    63→    _mk("glm-4.7-flash:latest", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=8.0, flash_attention=True),
    64→    _mk("qwen3-coder:30b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),
    65→    _mk("deepcoder:14b", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=14.0, flash_attention=True),
    66→    _mk("qwen3-coder-next", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=50.0, flash_attention=True),
    67→    _mk("minimax-m2.7", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=25.0, flash_attention=True),
    68→    # Deep reasoning (70B+): 8192 ctx
    69→    _mk("nemotron-3-super:120b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=30.0, flash_attention=True),  # MoE, 12B active
    70→    _mk("glm-5", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=20.0, flash_attention=True),  # 40B active params
    71→    _mk("openai/gpt-oss-20b", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=22.0, flash_attention=True),
    72→    # Utility / fine-tuned domain
    73→    _mk("phi3:mini", num_ctx=8192, size_gb=4.0, flash_attention=True),
    74→    # Fallback
    75→    _mk("default", num_ctx=16384, size_gb=10.0, flash_attention=True),
    76→]
    77→
    78→# Training variants that override the base profile with reduced context and no keep_alive
    79→_TRAINING_VARIANTS = ["qwen3.5:9b", "nemotron-3-nano:30b", "phi3:mini"]
    80→
    81→CONTEXT_PROFILES: dict[str, ModelContextProfile] = {p.model_name: p for p in _BASE_PROFILES}
    82→
    83→for _model_name in _TRAINING_VARIANTS:
    84→    _key = f"{_model_name}:training"
    85→    CONTEXT_PROFILES[_key] = _training(CONTEXT_PROFILES[_model_name])
    86→
    87→
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→# OllamaContextManager
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→
    92→
    93→class OllamaContextManager:
    94→    """Apply context profiles to Ollama models; enforce OOM-safe training lifecycle."""
    95→
    96→    def __init__(self, base_url: str = OLLAMA_BASE_URL) -> None:
    97→        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
    98→        self._cached_available_gb: float = TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB
    99→
   100→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→    # Profile lookup
   102→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→    def get_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   105→        """Return profile for model; falls back to 'default' if not found."""
   106→        return CONTEXT_PROFILES.get(model_name, CONTEXT_PROFILES["default"])
   107→
   108→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→    # Ollama API helpers
   110→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→    async def apply_profile(self, model: str, profile: ModelContextProfile) -> None:
   113→        """Log the intended context options for a model (advisory; Ollama applies these at generate time)."""
   114→        logger.info(
   115→            "Context profile for %s: num_ctx=%d flash_attention=%s keep_alive=%s",
   116→            model,
   117→            profile.num_ctx,
   118→            profile.flash_attention,
   119→            profile.keep_alive,
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    async def get_loaded_models(self) -> list[str]:
   123→        """Query /api/ps for currently loaded models. Returns empty list on error."""
   124→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   125→        try:
   126→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   127→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   128→                    if resp.status != 200:
   129→                        logger.warning("GET /api/ps returned HTTP %d", resp.status)
   130→                        return []
   131→                    data = await resp.json()
   132→                    models: list[str] = [m["name"] for m in data.get("models", [])]
   133→                    return models
   134→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   135→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps")
   136→            return []
   137→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   138→            logger.warning("ClientError querying Ollama /api/ps: %s", exc)
   139→            return []
   140→
   141→    async def unload_model(self, model: str) -> None:
   142→        """Unload a specific model via POST /api/generate with keep_alive=0."""
   143→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   144→        payload = {"model": model, "keep_alive": 0}
   145→        try:
   146→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   147→                async with asyncio.wait_for(
   148→                    session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=30.0
   149→                ) as resp:
   150→                    if resp.status not in (200, 404):
   151→                        logger.warning("Unload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   152→                    else:
   153→                        logger.info("Unloaded model: %s", model)
   154→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   155→            logger.warning("Timeout unloading model %s", model)
   156→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   157→            logger.warning("ClientError unloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   158→
   159→    async def unload_all_for_training(self) -> None:
   160→        """CRITICAL OOM prevention: unload ALL models before training begins.
   161→
   162→        Steps:
   163→        1. GET /api/ps to find all loaded models.
   164→        2. POST /api/generate keep_alive=0 for each.
   165→        3. Poll /api/ps until empty (max 60 s timeout).
   166→        """
   167→        loaded = await self.get_loaded_models()
   168→        if not loaded:
   169→            logger.info("No models loaded; nothing to unload before training.")
   170→            return
   171→
   172→        logger.info("Unloading %d model(s) before training: %s", len(loaded), loaded)
   173→        await asyncio.gather(*(self.unload_model(m) for m in loaded))
   174→
   175→        # Poll until /api/ps is empty or timeout
   176→        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 60.0
   177→        while asyncio.get_event_loop().time() < deadline:
   178→            remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   179→            if not remaining:
   180→                logger.info("All models unloaded; safe to begin training.")
   181→                return
   182→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   183→
   184→        remaining = await self.get_loaded_models()
   185→        if remaining:
   186→            logger.warning("Training unload timeout; %d model(s) still loaded: %s", len(remaining), remaining)
   187→
   188→    async def reload_inference_models(self, hot_models: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
   189→        """Restore hot-tier models after training completes.
   190→
   191→        Sends a lightweight /api/generate prompt to each model so Ollama pre-loads it.
   192→        """
   193→        targets = hot_models if hot_models is not None else _DEFAULT_HOT_MODELS
   194→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/generate"
   195→
   196→        async def _warm(model: str) -> None:
   197→            payload = {"model": model, "prompt": "", "keep_alive": "5m", "stream": False}
   198→            try:
   199→                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   200→                    async with asyncio.wait_for(
   201→                        session.post(url, json=payload), timeout=60.0
   202→                    ) as resp:
   203→                        if resp.status == 200:
   204→                            logger.info("Reloaded inference model: %s", model)
   205→                        else:
   206→                            logger.warning("Reload of %s returned HTTP %d", model, resp.status)
   207→            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   208→                logger.warning("Timeout reloading model %s", model)
   209→            except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   210→                logger.warning("ClientError reloading model %s: %s", model, exc)
   211→
   212→        await asyncio.gather(*(_warm(m) for m in targets))
   213→
   214→    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:
   215→        """Estimate available unified memory in GB.
   216→
   217→        Formula: 128 - sum(loaded model sizes from /api/ps) - OS_OVERHEAD_GB.
   218→        Clamps to >= 0.0. Updates internal cache used by check_oom_risk().
   219→        """
   220→        url = f"{self._base_url}/api/ps"
   221→        loaded_gb = 0.0
   222→        try:
   223→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   224→                async with asyncio.wait_for(session.get(url), timeout=10.0) as resp:
   225→                    if resp.status == 200:
   226→                        data = await resp.json()
   227→                        for m in data.get("models", []):
   228→                            size_bytes: int = m.get("size", 0)
   229→                            loaded_gb += size_bytes / (1024**3)
   230→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   231→            logger.warning("Timeout querying Ollama /api/ps for memory estimate")
   232→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   233→            logger.warning("ClientError querying /api/ps for memory: %s", exc)
   234→
   235→        available = max(0.0, TOTAL_SYSTEM_MEMORY_GB - loaded_gb - OS_OVERHEAD_GB - SAFETY_BUFFER_GB)
   236→        self._cached_available_gb = available
   237→        return available
   238→
   239→    def check_oom_risk(
   240→        self,
   241→        model_size_gb: float,
   242→        training_overhead_factor: float = 3.0,
   243→    ) -> bool:
   244→        """Return True if loading model for training risks OOM.
   245→
   246→        Uses cached available GB (call get_available_memory_gb() first for accuracy).
   247→        OOM risk when: available_gb < model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   248→        """
   249→        required = model_size_gb * training_overhead_factor * 1.2
   250→        return self._cached_available_gb < required
   251→
   252→    @property
   253→    def cached_available_gb(self) -> float:
   254→        """Last known available GB. Updated by get_available_memory_gb()."""
   255→        return self._cached_available_gb
   256→
   257→
   258→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   259→# ContextOptimizer (simple helper)
   260→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   261→
   262→
   263→class ContextOptimizer:
   264→    """Helper to select optimal context settings for a given task type."""
   265→
   266→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   267→        self._manager = OllamaContextManager()
   268→
   269→    def get_training_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   270→        """Return reduced-context profile for training (num_ctx=2048, keep_alive=0)."""
   271→        training_key = f"{model_name}:training"
   272→        if training_key in CONTEXT_PROFILES:
   273→            return CONTEXT_PROFILES[training_key]
   274→        # Build an ad-hoc training profile from whatever base we find
   275→        base = self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   276→        return _training(base)
   277→
   278→    def get_inference_profile(self, model_name: str) -> ModelContextProfile:
   279→        """Return standard inference profile for a model."""
   280→        return self._manager.get_profile(model_name)
   281→
   282→    def estimate_kv_cache_gb(self, num_ctx: int, model_size_gb: float) -> float:
   283→        """Estimate KV cache memory in GB.
   284→
   285→        Approximation: num_ctx * model_size_gb / 128_000.
   286→        KV cache grows roughly linearly with context length for a given model size.
   287→        """
   288→        return (num_ctx * model_size_gb) / 128_000
   289→

     2→from __future__ import annotations

     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→import time
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Literal
    10→
    11→from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager
    12→from cohezion.agentjet.judger import PhiScoreJudger
    13→from cohezion.agentjet.task_reader import JourneyTaskReader
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from cohezion.agentjet.workflow import CohezionWorkflow
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class TrainingResult:
    23→    """Result of a single AgentJet CALL training cycle."""
    24→
    25→    success: bool
    26→    model_name: str           # Final Ollama model name after training
    27→    base_model: str           # Model that was fine-tuned
    28→    skill_domain: str | None  # Which skill domain was trained
    29→    epochs_completed: int
    30→    samples_used: int
    31→    avg_reward: float         # Average reward from PhiScoreJudger
    32→    training_duration_s: float
    33→    output_path: Path | None  # Path to GGUF or config script
    34→    error: str | None = None  # Error message if success=False
    35→    dry_run: bool = False
    36→
    37→
    38→class AgentJetTrainer:
    39→    """Orchestrates the full CALL RL cycle with OOM-safe training.
    40→
    41→    Safety protocol (enforced in train()):
    42→    1. Check available memory via ResourceClient or OllamaContextManager.
    43→    2. Unload ALL inference models.
    44→    3. Run training backend (llamafactory Phase 1, or agentjet Phase 3).
    45→    4. Export GGUF → ollama create cohezion-{domain}-v{n}.
    46→    5. Reload inference models.
    47→    6. Update SmartRouter with new model name.
    48→    All steps run inside try/finally so reload always happens.
    49→
    50→    Parameters
    51→    ----------
    52→    workflow : CohezionWorkflow, optional
    53→        Workflow instance for generating rollouts. Created lazily if not provided.
    54→    judger : PhiScoreJudger, optional
    55→        Reward judger. Defaults to PhiScoreJudger().
    56→    reader : JourneyTaskReader, optional
    57→        Task loader. Defaults to JourneyTaskReader().
    58→    context_manager : OllamaContextManager, optional
    59→        Manages Ollama model lifecycle. Defaults to OllamaContextManager().
    60→    backend : Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"]
    61→        Training backend to use. "llamafactory" uses LocalFinetuner (Phase 1).
    62→        "agentjet" targets verl/PPO (Phase 3, not yet implemented).
    63→    """
    64→
    65→    def __init__(
    66→        self,
    67→        workflow: CohezionWorkflow | None = None,
    68→        judger: PhiScoreJudger | None = None,
    69→        reader: JourneyTaskReader | None = None,
    70→        context_manager: OllamaContextManager | None = None,
    71→        backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = "llamafactory",
    72→    ) -> None:
    73→        self._workflow = workflow  # Lazily resolved; not required for training
    74→        self.judger = judger if judger is not None else PhiScoreJudger()
    75→        self.reader = reader if reader is not None else JourneyTaskReader()
    76→        self.context_manager = (
    77→            context_manager if context_manager is not None else OllamaContextManager()
    78→        )
    79→        self.backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = backend
    80→        self._training_lock: object | None = None  # TrainingLock from ResourceClient
    81→

    83→        self,

    90→        """Run the full CALL cycle: load data → safety check → train → reload.
    91→
    92→        Parameters

    94→        target_model : str
    95→            Base Ollama model to fine-tune.
    96→        skill_domain : str, optional
    97→            Filter training data to this skill domain. None means all domains.
    98→        epochs : int
    99→            Number of training epochs to pass to the backend.
   100→        min_phi : float
   101→            Minimum phi_score threshold for including a task in the training set.
   102→        dry_run : bool
   103→            If True, skip actual training and model unload/reload. Useful for
   104→            validating the pipeline without touching Ollama.

   109→            Describes the outcome of the training run.
   110→        """
   111→        start_time = time.monotonic()
   112→
   113→        # 1. Load training data
   114→        tasks = self.reader.read(skill_filter=skill_domain, min_phi=min_phi)
   115→        if not tasks:
   116→            logger.warning(
   117→                "AgentJetTrainer.train: no tasks matched filters "
   118→                "(skill_domain=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   119→                skill_domain,
   120→                min_phi,
   121→            )
   122→            return TrainingResult(
   123→                success=False,
   124→                model_name="",
   125→                base_model=target_model,
   126→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   127→                epochs_completed=0,
   128→                samples_used=0,
   129→                avg_reward=0.0,
   130→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   131→                output_path=None,
   132→                error="No training data matched filters",
   133→                dry_run=dry_run,
   134→            )
   135→
   136→        # 2. OOM safety check — acquires lock or raises OOMRiskError
   137→        await self._safety_check(target_model)
   138→
   139→        output_path: Path | None = None
   140→        try:
   141→            # 3. Unload all inference models before training
   142→            if not dry_run:
   143→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: unloading inference models for training")
   144→                await self.context_manager.unload_all_for_training()
   145→
   146→            # 4. Compute rewards for dataset quality metrics
   147→            rewards = self.judger.batch_judge(tasks)
   148→            avg_reward = sum(rewards) / len(rewards) if rewards else 0.0
   149→            logger.info(
   150→                "AgentJetTrainer: %d tasks, avg_reward=%.3f",
   151→                len(tasks),
   152→                avg_reward,
   153→            )
   154→
   155→            # 5. Run training backend
   156→            if not dry_run:
   157→                output_path = await self._run_training(
   158→                    target_model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs
   159→                )
   160→
   161→            # 6. Build final model name
   162→            domain = skill_domain or "general"
   163→            version = int(time.time()) % 10000
   164→            final_model_name = f"cohezion-{domain}-v{version}"
   165→
   166→            logger.info(
   167→                "AgentJetTrainer: training complete — model=%s output=%s",
   168→                final_model_name,
   169→                output_path,
   170→            )
   171→            return TrainingResult(
   172→                success=True,
   173→                model_name=final_model_name,
   174→                base_model=target_model,
   175→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   176→                epochs_completed=epochs,
   177→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   178→                avg_reward=avg_reward,
   179→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   180→                output_path=output_path,
   181→                dry_run=dry_run,
   182→            )
   183→
   184→        except Exception as exc:
   185→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer.train failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   186→            return TrainingResult(
   187→                success=False,
   188→                model_name="",
   189→                base_model=target_model,
   190→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   191→                epochs_completed=0,
   192→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   193→                avg_reward=0.0,
   194→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   195→                output_path=None,
   196→                error=str(exc),
   197→                dry_run=dry_run,
   198→            )
   199→
   200→        finally:
   201→            # Always reload inference models — even if training failed
   202→            if not dry_run:
   203→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: reloading inference models")
   204→                try:
   205→                    await self.context_manager.reload_inference_models()
   206→                except Exception as reload_exc:
   207→                    logger.warning(
   208→                        "Inference model reload failed (non-fatal): %s", reload_exc
   209→                    )
   210→
   211→            # Release training lock regardless of outcome
   212→            if self._training_lock is not None:
   213→                try:
   214→                    from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import ResourceClient
   215→
   216→                    client = ResourceClient()
   217→                    lock_id: str = self._training_lock.lock_id  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   218→                    await client.release_training_lock(lock_id)
   219→                    logger.debug("AgentJetTrainer: released training lock %s", lock_id)
   220→                except Exception as lock_exc:
   221→                    logger.warning(
   222→                        "Training lock release failed (non-fatal): %s", lock_exc
   223→                    )
   224→                finally:
   225→                    self._training_lock = None
   226→
   227→    async def _safety_check(self, model: str) -> None:
   228→        """OOM check before training begins.
   229→
   230→        Tries the platform ResourceClient daemon first (cross-session coordination).
   231→        Falls back to a local Ollama memory estimate when the daemon is unavailable.
   232→
   233→        Raises
   234→        ------
   235→        OOMRiskError
   236→            When local memory check indicates insufficient headroom.
   237→        ResourceUnavailableError
   238→            When the platform daemon explicitly denies the training lock.
   239→        """
   240→        profile = self.context_manager.get_profile(model)
   241→        required_gb = profile.size_gb * 3.0  # Training needs ~3x model size
   242→
   243→        # Attempt platform daemon coordination first
   244→        try:
   245→            from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import (
   246→                ResourceClient,
   247→                ResourceUnavailableError,
   248→            )
   249→

   297→    async def _run_training(
   298→        self,
   299→        model: str,
   300→        tasks: list[dict],
   301→        skill_domain: str | None,
   302→        epochs: int,
   303→    ) -> Path | None:
   304→        """Dispatch to the configured training backend.
   305→
   306→        Parameters
   307→        ----------
   308→        model : str
   309→            Base model identifier.
   310→        tasks : list[dict]
   311→            Normalised task dicts from JourneyTaskReader.
   312→        skill_domain : str, optional
   313→            Skill domain being trained (used for output naming).
   314→        epochs : int
   315→            Number of training epochs.
   316→
   317→        Returns
   318→        -------
   319→        Path | None
   320→            Path to generated training config/script, or None on failure.
   321→        """
   322→        if self.backend == "llamafactory":
   323→            return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   324→        if self.backend == "agentjet":
   325→            return await self._run_agentjet_rl(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   326→        logger.warning("Unknown backend %r; falling back to llamafactory", self.backend)
   327→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)

   329→    async def _run_llamafactory(
   330→        self,
   331→        model: str,
   332→        tasks: list[dict],
   333→        skill_domain: str | None,
   334→        epochs: int,
   335→    ) -> Path | None:
   336→        """Phase 1: generate a llamafactory training config via LocalFinetuner.
   337→
   338→        Writes tasks to the standard JSONL location, then calls
   339→        LocalFinetuner.run_qlora_training() which produces a bash script / config.
   340→
   341→        Returns the path to the generated config file, or None on error.
   342→        """
   343→        try:
   344→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   345→
   346→            output_name = f"cohezion_{skill_domain or 'general'}_v{int(time.time()) % 10000}"
   347→
   348→            # Map full Ollama model names to LocalFinetuner's known base keys
   349→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   350→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   351→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   352→                "phi4": "phi4",
   353→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   354→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   355→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   356→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   357→            }
   358→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   359→
   360→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name=output_name)
   361→
   362→            # Ensure training data directory and JSONL exist for LocalFinetuner
   363→            import json
   364→
   365→            data_dir = Path("data/training")
   366→            data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   367→            journey_path = data_dir / "finetune_journeys.jsonl"
   368→
   369→            if not journey_path.exists():
   370→                logger.info(
   371→                    "AgentJetTrainer: writing %d tasks to %s for LocalFinetuner",
   372→                    len(tasks),
   373→                    journey_path,
   374→                )
   375→                with journey_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   376→                    for task in tasks:
   377→                        fh.write(json.dumps(task) + "\n")
   378→
   379→            # Run in executor — LocalFinetuner is synchronous
   380→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   381→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   382→                None,
   383→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   384→            )
   385→            logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: llamafactory config generated: %s", config_path)
   386→            return config_path
   387→
   388→        except Exception as exc:
   389→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer._run_llamafactory failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   390→            return None
   391→
   392→    async def _run_agentjet_rl(
   393→        self,
   394→        model: str,
   395→        tasks: list[dict],
   396→        skill_domain: str | None,
   397→        epochs: int,

   404→        logger.warning(

   406→            "Falling back to llamafactory."

   408→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)

     1→"""JourneyTaskReader: loads training tasks from journey JSONL or ExperienceCollector.
     2→
     3→Primary source: ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` produced by
     4→``JourneyToFinetuneConverter.run()``.
     5→
     6→Fallback source: ``ExperienceCollector.collect_all()`` which pulls from
     7→Parquet shards, SurrealDB, and vault experiment JSON files.
     8→
     9→JSONL record schema (from JourneyToFinetuneConverter._to_training_pairs):
    10→  {
    11→    "instruction": str,
    12→    "output": str,
    13→    "metadata": {
    14→      "skill": str,
    15→      "phi_score": float,
    16→      "mission": str,
    17→      "trajectory_12d": list[float]
    18→    }
    19→  }
    20→
    21→ExperienceCollector records use a flat schema with fields including:
    22→  phi_score, skill_name, mission_id, input_preview, trajectory, ...
    23→
    24→JourneyTaskReader normalises both schemas into a consistent task dict with
    25→top-level ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, and ``output``.
    26→"""
    27→
    28→from __future__ import annotations
    29→
    30→import json
    31→import logging
    32→from pathlib import Path
    33→from typing import Any
    34→
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→_DEFAULT_PATH = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
    39→
    40→
    41→class JourneyTaskReader:
    42→    """Reads journey data as task dicts for AgentJet training.
    43→
    44→    Loads from ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl`` when available;
    45→    falls back to ``ExperienceCollector`` when the file does not exist or
    46→    cannot be read.
    47→
    48→    Parameters
    49→    ----------
    50→    jsonl_path : Path, optional
    51→        Path to the JSONL file produced by ``JourneyToFinetuneConverter``.
    52→        Defaults to ``data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl``.
    53→    """
    54→
    55→    DEFAULT_PATH: Path = _DEFAULT_PATH
    56→
    57→    def __init__(self, jsonl_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    58→        self._jsonl_path: Path = jsonl_path if jsonl_path is not None else self.DEFAULT_PATH
    59→
    60→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→    # Public API
    62→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→    def read(
    65→        self,
    66→        skill_filter: str | None = None,
    67→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    68→        max_samples: int = 1000,
    69→    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    70→        """Load tasks, optionally filtered by skill name and minimum phi_score.
    71→
    72→        Parameters
    73→        ----------
    74→        skill_filter : str, optional
    75→            If provided, only tasks whose ``skill_name`` matches this string
    76→            (case-insensitive) are returned.
    77→        min_phi : float
    78→            Minimum phi_score threshold. Tasks below this value are excluded.
    79→        max_samples : int
    80→            Maximum number of tasks to return.
    81→
    82→        Returns
    83→        -------
    84→        list[dict]
    85→            Normalised task dicts, each containing at minimum:
    86→            ``phi_score``, ``skill_name``, ``instruction``, ``output``.
    87→        """
    88→        records = self._load_records()
    89→
    90→        filtered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    91→        for rec in records:
    92→            phi = float(rec.get("phi_score", 0.0))
    93→            if phi < min_phi:
    94→                continue
    95→            if skill_filter is not None:
    96→                skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "")).lower()
    97→                if skill != skill_filter.lower():
    98→                    continue
    99→            filtered.append(rec)
   100→            if len(filtered) >= max_samples:
   101→                break
   102→
   103→        logger.info(
   104→            "JourneyTaskReader.read: %d/%d records pass filters "
   105→            "(skill_filter=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   106→            len(filtered),
   107→            len(records),
   108→            skill_filter,
   109→            min_phi,
   110→        )
   111→        return filtered
   112→
   113→    def group_by_skill(self, min_phi: float = 0.7) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   114→        """Group tasks by skill name, filtered by minimum phi_score.
   115→
   116→        Parameters
   117→        ----------
   118→        min_phi : float
   119→            Only tasks with phi_score >= min_phi are included.
   120→
   121→        Returns
   122→        -------
   123→        dict[str, list[dict]]
   124→            Mapping of skill_name → list of task dicts.
   125→        """
   126→        records = self.read(min_phi=min_phi, max_samples=100_000)
   127→        groups: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
   128→        for rec in records:
   129→            skill = str(rec.get("skill_name", "unknown"))
   130→            groups.setdefault(skill, []).append(rec)
   131→
   132→        logger.info(
   133→            "JourneyTaskReader.group_by_skill: %d skills, %d total tasks (min_phi=%.2f)",
   134→            len(groups),
   135→            sum(len(v) for v in groups.values()),
   136→            min_phi,
   137→        )
   138→        return groups
   139→
   140→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→    # Private loaders
   142→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   143→
   144→    def _load_records(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   145→        """Load records from JSONL or fall back to ExperienceCollector."""
   146→        if self._jsonl_path.exists():
   147→            try:
   148→                return self._load_from_jsonl()
   149→            except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
   150→                logger.warning(
   151→                    "Failed to load JSONL from %s (%s), falling back to collector",
   152→                    self._jsonl_path,
   153→                    exc,
   154→                )
   155→        else:
   156→            logger.debug(
   157→                "JSONL file %s not found, using ExperienceCollector fallback",
   158→                self._jsonl_path,
   159→            )
   160→
   161→        return self._load_from_collector()
   162→
   163→    def _load_from_jsonl(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   164→        """Parse JSONL produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
   165→
   166→        Each line is a JSON object with schema::
   167→
   168→            {
   169→              "instruction": str,
   170→              "output": str,
   171→              "metadata": {"skill": str, "phi_score": float, ...}
   172→            }
   173→
   174→        Returns
   175→        -------
   176→        list[dict]
   177→            Normalised records with top-level ``phi_score`` and ``skill_name``.
   178→
   179→        Raises
   180→        ------
   181→        OSError
   182→            If the file cannot be opened.
   183→        json.JSONDecodeError
   184→            If a line contains invalid JSON.
   185→        """
   186→        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   187→        with self._jsonl_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   188→            for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   189→                line = line.strip()
   190→                if not line:
   191→                    continue
   192→                try:
   193→                    raw: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(line)
   194→                except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   195→                    raise json.JSONDecodeError(
   196→                        f"Line {lineno}: {exc.msg}", exc.doc, exc.pos
   197→                    ) from exc
   198→
   199→                records.append(self._normalise_jsonl_record(raw))
   200→
   201→        logger.debug("Loaded %d records from %s", len(records), self._jsonl_path)
   202→        return records
   203→
   204→    def _load_from_collector(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   205→        """Load experiences via ExperienceCollector (three-tier fallback).
   206→
   207→        Returns
   208→        -------
   209→        list[dict]
   210→            Normalised records. Returns an empty list if collector is
   211→            unavailable or raises an unexpected exception.
   212→        """
   213→        try:
   214→            from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector
   215→
   216→            collector = ExperienceCollector()
   217→            raw_records = collector.collect_all(max_samples=100_000)
   218→        except ImportError as exc:
   219→            logger.warning("ExperienceCollector import failed: %s", exc)
   220→            return []
   221→        except Exception as exc:
   222→            logger.warning(
   223→                "ExperienceCollector.collect_all() failed (non-blocking): %s",
   224→                exc,
   225→                exc_info=True,
   226→            )
   227→            return []
   228→
   229→        records = [self._normalise_collector_record(r) for r in raw_records]
   230→        logger.debug(
   231→            "Loaded %d records from ExperienceCollector", len(records)
   232→        )
   233→        return records
   234→
   235→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   236→    # Schema normalisation
   237→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   238→
   239→    @staticmethod
   240→    def _normalise_jsonl_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   241→        """Normalise a JSONL record to the canonical task dict schema.
   242→
   243→        Parameters
   244→        ----------
   245→        raw : dict
   246→            Raw JSON object from finetune_journeys.jsonl.
   247→
   248→        Returns
   249→        -------
   250→        dict
   251→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   252→        """
   253→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = raw.get("metadata", {})
   254→        return {
   255→            "instruction": raw.get("instruction", ""),
   256→            "output": raw.get("output", ""),
   257→            "phi_score": float(metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   258→            "skill_name": str(metadata.get("skill", "unknown")),
   259→            "mission_id": str(metadata.get("mission", "")),
   260→            "trajectory_12d": metadata.get("trajectory_12d", []),
   261→            # Preserve original metadata for downstream consumers
   262→            "metadata": metadata,
   263→        }
   264→
   265→    @staticmethod
   266→    def _normalise_collector_record(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   267→        """Normalise an ExperienceCollector record to the canonical task dict schema.
   268→
   269→        Parameters
   270→        ----------
   271→        raw : dict
   272→            Flat experience dict from ExperienceCollector.collect_all().
   273→
   274→        Returns
   275→        -------
   276→        dict
   277→            Canonical task dict with top-level phi_score and skill_name.
   278→        """
   279→        return {
   280→            "instruction": str(raw.get("input_preview", "")),
   281→            "output": "",  # Raw collector records may not have formatted output
   282→            "phi_score": float(raw.get("phi_score", 0.0)),
   283→            "skill_name": str(raw.get("skill_name", "unknown")),
   284→            "mission_id": str(raw.get("mission_id", "")),
   285→            "trajectory_12d": raw.get("trajectory", [])[:12]
   286→            if isinstance(raw.get("trajectory"), list)
   287→            else [],
   288→            # Preserve all original fields for downstream consumers
   289→            "metadata": raw,
   290→        }
   291→

     1→"""AgentJet RL training orchestrator for the CALL loop."""
     2→from __future__ import annotations
     3→
     4→import asyncio
     5→import logging
     6→import time
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Literal
    10→
    11→from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import MODEL_OLLAMA_KEY_MAP, OllamaContextManager
    12→from cohezion.agentjet.judger import PhiScoreJudger
    13→from cohezion.agentjet.task_reader import JourneyTaskReader
    14→
    15→_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    16→_DATA_DIR = _PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "training"
    17→
    18→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    19→    from cohezion.agentjet.workflow import CohezionWorkflow
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class TrainingResult:
    26→    """Result of a single AgentJet CALL training cycle."""
    27→
    28→    success: bool
    29→    model_name: str           # Final Ollama model name after training
    30→    base_model: str           # Model that was fine-tuned
    31→    skill_domain: str | None  # Which skill domain was trained
    32→    epochs_completed: int
    33→    samples_used: int
    34→    avg_reward: float         # Average reward from PhiScoreJudger
    35→    training_duration_s: float
    36→    output_path: Path | None  # Path to GGUF or config script
    37→    error: str | None = None  # Error message if success=False
    38→    dry_run: bool = False
    39→
    40→
    41→class AgentJetTrainer:
    42→    """Orchestrates the full CALL RL cycle with OOM-safe training.
    43→
    44→    Safety protocol (enforced in train()):
    45→    1. Check available memory via ResourceClient or OllamaContextManager.
    46→    2. Unload ALL inference models.
    47→    3. Run training backend (llamafactory Phase 1, or agentjet Phase 3).
    48→    4. Export GGUF → ollama create cohezion-{domain}-v{n}.
    49→    5. Reload inference models.
    50→    6. Update SmartRouter with new model name.
    51→    All steps run inside try/finally so reload always happens.
    52→
    53→    Parameters
    54→    ----------
    55→    workflow : CohezionWorkflow, optional
    56→        Workflow instance for generating rollouts. Created lazily if not provided.
    57→    judger : PhiScoreJudger, optional
    58→        Reward judger. Defaults to PhiScoreJudger().
    59→    reader : JourneyTaskReader, optional
    60→        Task loader. Defaults to JourneyTaskReader().
    61→    context_manager : OllamaContextManager, optional
    62→        Manages Ollama model lifecycle. Defaults to OllamaContextManager().
    63→    backend : Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"]
    64→        Training backend to use. "llamafactory" uses LocalFinetuner (Phase 1).
    65→        "agentjet" targets verl/PPO (Phase 3, not yet implemented).
    66→    """
    67→
    68→    def __init__(
    69→        self,
    70→        workflow: CohezionWorkflow | None = None,
    71→        judger: PhiScoreJudger | None = None,
    72→        reader: JourneyTaskReader | None = None,
    73→        context_manager: OllamaContextManager | None = None,
    74→        backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = "llamafactory",
    75→    ) -> None:
    76→        self._workflow = workflow  # Lazily resolved; not required for training
    77→        self.judger = judger if judger is not None else PhiScoreJudger()
    78→        self.reader = reader if reader is not None else JourneyTaskReader()
    79→        self.context_manager = (
    80→            context_manager if context_manager is not None else OllamaContextManager()
    81→        )
    82→        self.backend: Literal["agentjet", "llamafactory"] = backend
    83→
    84→    async def train(
    85→        self,
    86→        target_model: str = "qwen3.5:9b",
    87→        skill_domain: str | None = None,
    88→        epochs: int = 3,
    89→        min_phi: float = 0.7,
    90→        dry_run: bool = False,
    91→    ) -> TrainingResult:
    92→        """Run the full CALL cycle: load data → safety check → train → reload.
    93→
    94→        Parameters
    95→        ----------
    96→        target_model : str
    97→            Base Ollama model to fine-tune.
    98→        skill_domain : str, optional
    99→            Filter training data to this skill domain. None means all domains.
   100→        epochs : int
   101→            Number of training epochs to pass to the backend.
   102→        min_phi : float
   103→            Minimum phi_score threshold for including a task in the training set.
   104→        dry_run : bool
   105→            If True, skip actual training and model unload/reload. Useful for
   106→            validating the pipeline without touching Ollama.
   107→
   108→        Returns
   109→        -------
   110→        TrainingResult
   111→            Describes the outcome of the training run.
   112→        """
   113→        start_time = time.monotonic()
   114→
   115→        # 1. Load training data
   116→        tasks = self.reader.read(skill_filter=skill_domain, min_phi=min_phi)
   117→        if not tasks:
   118→            logger.warning(
   119→                "AgentJetTrainer.train: no tasks matched filters "
   120→                "(skill_domain=%r, min_phi=%.2f)",
   121→                skill_domain,
   122→                min_phi,
   123→            )
   124→            return TrainingResult(
   125→                success=False,
   126→                model_name="",
   127→                base_model=target_model,
   128→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   129→                epochs_completed=0,
   130→                samples_used=0,
   131→                avg_reward=0.0,
   132→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   133→                output_path=None,
   134→                error="No training data matched filters",
   135→                dry_run=dry_run,
   136→            )
   137→
   138→        training_lock: object | None = None
   139→        output_path: Path | None = None
   140→        try:
   141→            # 2. OOM safety check — acquires lock or raises OOMRiskError
   142→            training_lock = await self._safety_check(target_model)
   143→            # 3. Unload all inference models before training
   144→            if not dry_run:
   145→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: unloading inference models for training")
   146→                await self.context_manager.unload_all_for_training()
   147→
   148→            # 4. Compute rewards for dataset quality metrics
   149→            rewards = self.judger.batch_judge(tasks)
   150→            avg_reward = sum(rewards) / len(rewards) if rewards else 0.0
   151→            logger.info(
   152→                "AgentJetTrainer: %d tasks, avg_reward=%.3f",
   153→                len(tasks),
   154→                avg_reward,
   155→            )
   156→
   157→            # 5. Run training backend
   158→            if not dry_run:
   159→                output_path = await self._run_training(
   160→                    target_model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs
   161→                )
   162→
   163→            # 6. Build final model name
   164→            domain = skill_domain or "general"
   165→            version = int(time.time()) % 10000
   166→            final_model_name = f"cohezion-{domain}-v{version}"
   167→
   168→            logger.info(
   169→                "AgentJetTrainer: training complete — model=%s output=%s",
   170→                final_model_name,
   171→                output_path,
   172→            )
   173→            return TrainingResult(
   174→                success=True,
   175→                model_name=final_model_name,
   176→                base_model=target_model,
   177→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   178→                epochs_completed=epochs,
   179→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   180→                avg_reward=avg_reward,
   181→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   182→                output_path=output_path,
   183→                dry_run=dry_run,
   184→            )
   185→
   186→        except Exception as exc:
   187→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer.train failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   188→            return TrainingResult(
   189→                success=False,
   190→                model_name="",
   191→                base_model=target_model,
   192→                skill_domain=skill_domain,
   193→                epochs_completed=0,
   194→                samples_used=len(tasks),
   195→                avg_reward=0.0,
   196→                training_duration_s=time.monotonic() - start_time,
   197→                output_path=None,
   198→                error=str(exc),
   199→                dry_run=dry_run,
   200→            )
   201→
   202→        finally:
   203→            # Always reload inference models — even if training failed
   204→            if not dry_run:
   205→                logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: reloading inference models")
   206→                try:
   207→                    await self.context_manager.reload_inference_models()
   208→                except Exception as reload_exc:
   209→                    logger.warning(
   210→                        "Inference model reload failed (non-fatal): %s", reload_exc
   211→                    )
   212→
   213→            # Release training lock regardless of outcome
   214→            if training_lock is not None:
   215→                try:
   216→                    from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import ResourceClient
   217→
   218→                    client = ResourceClient()
   219→                    lock_id: str = training_lock.lock_id  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   220→                    await client.release_training_lock(lock_id)
   221→                    logger.debug("AgentJetTrainer: released training lock %s", lock_id)
   222→                except Exception as lock_exc:
   223→                    logger.warning(
   224→                        "Training lock release failed (non-fatal): %s", lock_exc
   225→                    )
   226→                finally:
   227→                    training_lock = None
   228→
   229→    async def _safety_check(self, model: str) -> None:
   230→        """OOM check before training begins.
   231→
   232→        Tries the platform ResourceClient daemon first (cross-session coordination).
   233→        Falls back to a local Ollama memory estimate when the daemon is unavailable.
   234→
   235→        Raises
   236→        ------
   237→        OOMRiskError
   238→            When local memory check indicates insufficient headroom.
   239→        ResourceUnavailableError
   240→            When the platform daemon explicitly denies the training lock.
   241→        """
   242→        profile = self.context_manager.get_profile(model)
   243→        required_gb = profile.size_gb * 3.0  # Training needs ~3x model size
   244→
   245→        # Attempt platform daemon coordination first
   246→        try:
   247→            from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import (
   248→                ResourceClient,
   249→                ResourceUnavailableError,
   250→            )
   251→
   252→            client = ResourceClient()
   253→            if client.is_daemon_running():
   254→                lock = await client.acquire_training_lock(
   255→                    model, required_gb, timeout_s=30.0
   256→                )
   257→                if lock is None:
   258→                    raise ResourceUnavailableError(
   259→                        f"Platform daemon denied training lock for {model} "
   260→                        f"(need {required_gb:.1f} GiB)"
   261→                    )
   262→                self._training_lock = lock
   263→                logger.info(
   264→                    "AgentJetTrainer: acquired training lock %s (%.1f GiB reserved)",
   265→                    lock.lock_id,
   266→                    required_gb,
   267→                )
   268→                return
   269→        except ImportError:
   270→            logger.debug(
   271→                "cohezion.platform.resource_manager unavailable; using local OOM check"
   272→            )
   273→
   274→        # Fallback: local available memory estimate
   275→        available = await self.context_manager.get_available_memory_gb()
   276→        headroom_required = required_gb * 1.2
   277→        if available < headroom_required:
   278→            from cohezion.agentjet.context_optimizer import OllamaContextManager  # noqa: F401
   279→
   280→            # Import OOMRiskError from resource_manager if available, else define locally
   281→            try:
   282→                from cohezion.platform.resource_manager import OOMRiskError
   283→            except ImportError:
   284→
   285→                class OOMRiskError(RuntimeError):  # type: ignore[no-redef]
   286→                    pass
   287→
   288→            raise OOMRiskError(
   289→                f"OOM risk: need {headroom_required:.1f} GiB (with 20% headroom), "
   290→                f"have {available:.1f} GiB available"
   291→            )
   292→
   293→        logger.info(
   294→            "AgentJetTrainer: local OOM check passed (need %.1f GiB, have %.1f GiB)",
   295→            headroom_required,
   296→            available,
   297→        )
   298→
   299→    async def _run_training(
   300→        self,
   301→        model: str,
   302→        tasks: list[dict],
   303→        skill_domain: str | None,
   304→        epochs: int,
   305→    ) -> Path | None:
   306→        """Dispatch to the configured training backend.
   307→
   308→        Parameters
   309→        ----------
   310→        model : str
   311→            Base model identifier.
   312→        tasks : list[dict]
   313→            Normalised task dicts from JourneyTaskReader.
   314→        skill_domain : str, optional
   315→            Skill domain being trained (used for output naming).
   316→        epochs : int
   317→            Number of training epochs.
   318→
   319→        Returns
   320→        -------
   321→        Path | None
   322→            Path to generated training config/script, or None on failure.
   323→        """
   324→        if self.backend == "llamafactory":
   325→            return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   326→        if self.backend == "agentjet":
   327→            return await self._run_agentjet_rl(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   328→        logger.warning("Unknown backend %r; falling back to llamafactory", self.backend)
   329→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   330→
   331→    async def _run_llamafactory(
   332→        self,
   333→        model: str,
   334→        tasks: list[dict],
   335→        skill_domain: str | None,
   336→        epochs: int,
   337→    ) -> Path | None:
   338→        """Phase 1: generate a llamafactory training config via LocalFinetuner.
   339→
   340→        Writes tasks to the standard JSONL location, then calls
   341→        LocalFinetuner.run_qlora_training() which produces a bash script / config.
   342→
   343→        Returns the path to the generated config file, or None on error.
   344→        """
   345→        try:
   346→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   347→
   348→            output_name = f"cohezion_{skill_domain or 'general'}_v{int(time.time()) % 10000}"
   349→
   350→            # Map full Ollama model names to LocalFinetuner's known base keys
   351→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   352→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   353→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   354→                "phi4": "phi4",
   355→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   356→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   357→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   358→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   359→            }
   360→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   361→
   362→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name=output_name)
   363→
   364→            # Ensure training data directory and JSONL exist for LocalFinetuner
   365→            import json
   366→
   367→            data_dir = Path("data/training")
   368→            data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   369→            journey_path = data_dir / "finetune_journeys.jsonl"
   370→
   371→            if not journey_path.exists():
   372→                logger.info(
   373→                    "AgentJetTrainer: writing %d tasks to %s for LocalFinetuner",
   374→                    len(tasks),
   375→                    journey_path,
   376→                )
   377→                with journey_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   378→                    for task in tasks:
   379→                        fh.write(json.dumps(task) + "\n")
   380→
   381→            # Run in executor — LocalFinetuner is synchronous
   382→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   383→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   384→                None,
   385→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   386→            )
   387→            logger.info("AgentJetTrainer: llamafactory config generated: %s", config_path)
   388→            return config_path
   389→
   390→        except Exception as exc:
   391→            logger.error("AgentJetTrainer._run_llamafactory failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   392→            return None
   393→
   394→    async def _run_agentjet_rl(
   395→        self,
   396→        model: str,
   397→        tasks: list[dict],
   398→        skill_domain: str | None,
   399→        epochs: int,
   400→    ) -> Path | None:
   401→        """Phase 3: AgentJet verl/PPO RL training.
   402→
   403→        Not yet implemented (AMD ROCm support for verl is pending).
   404→        Falls back to llamafactory until Phase 3 ships.
   405→        """
   406→        logger.warning(
   407→            "AgentJet RL backend (verl/PPO) is not yet implemented for AMD. "
   408→            "Falling back to llamafactory."
   409→        )
   410→        return await self._run_llamafactory(model, tasks, skill_domain, epochs)
   411→

     1→"""Unsloth Studio integration for Phase 2 QLoRA training.
     2→
     3→Available NOW:  Data Recipes API (convert vault → dataset).
     4→Available SOON: QLoRA training (when AMD ROCm support ships in Unsloth).
     5→
     6→Phase 1 (current): Falls back to LocalFinetuner (llama.cpp / llamafactory).
     7→Phase 2 (planned): QLoRA via Unsloth Studio once AMD support is confirmed.
     8→"""
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import json
    12→import logging
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→import aiohttp
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL = "https://studio.unsloth.ai"
    21→
    22→# Data Recipes endpoint (available now — no AMD dependency)
    23→_RECIPES_ENDPOINT = "/api/v1/recipes"
    24→
    25→
    26→class UnslothBridge:
    27→    """Interface to Unsloth Studio for dataset preparation and QLoRA training.
    28→
    29→    Phases
    30→    ------
    31→    Phase 1 (active): LocalFinetuner fallback — llama.cpp / llamafactory config.
    32→    Phase 2 (pending): QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio when AMD support ships.
    33→    Data Recipes (active): ``create_dataset_from_vault`` is available now and
    34→    calls the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API to convert vault notes to JSONL.
    35→
    36→    Parameters
    37→    ----------
    38→    api_key : str, optional
    39→        Unsloth Studio API key. If None, Data Recipes API calls will be
    40→        attempted without auth (may fail for private endpoints).
    41→    studio_url : str
    42→        Base URL for the Unsloth Studio API.
    43→    """
    44→
    45→    def __init__(
    46→        self,
    47→        api_key: str | None = None,
    48→        studio_url: str = UNSLOTH_STUDIO_URL,
    49→    ) -> None:
    50→        self._api_key = api_key
    51→        self._studio_url = studio_url.rstrip("/")
    52→
    53→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→    # Capability probes
    55→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→
    57→    def is_training_available(self) -> bool:
    58→        """Return True if Unsloth QLoRA training is supported on current hardware.
    59→
    60→        Currently returns False because Unsloth Studio QLoRA training requires
    61→        CUDA (NVIDIA) and AMD ROCm support has not shipped yet.  When AMD support
    62→        arrives this method should be updated to probe the runtime.
    63→        """
    64→        return False
    65→
    66→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    67→    # Data Recipes (available now)
    68→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→
    70→    async def create_dataset_from_vault(
    71→        self,
    72→        vault_query: str,
    73→        output_path: Path | None = None,
    74→    ) -> Path:
    75→        """Convert vault notes to a JSONL training dataset via Unsloth Data Recipes.
    76→
    77→        Attempts to call the Unsloth Studio Data Recipes API.  If the API is
    78→        unavailable (no key, network error, or endpoint not reachable) the
    79→        method falls back to reading the local finetune_journeys.jsonl file
    80→        produced by JourneyToFinetuneConverter.
    81→
    82→        Parameters
    83→        ----------
    84→        vault_query : str
    85→            Search query used to select relevant vault notes (forwarded to the
    86→            Data Recipes API as the ``query`` field).
    87→        output_path : Path, optional
    88→            Where to write the output JSONL.  Defaults to
    89→            ``data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl``.
    90→
    91→        Returns
    92→        -------
    93→        Path
    94→            Absolute path to the generated JSONL dataset file.
    95→        """
    96→        out = output_path if output_path is not None else Path("data/training/unsloth_dataset.jsonl")
    97→        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    98→
    99→        # Try Unsloth Data Recipes API first
   100→        api_data = await self._call_data_recipes_api(vault_query)
   101→        if api_data is not None:
   102→            logger.info(
   103→                "UnslothBridge: received %d records from Data Recipes API",
   104→                len(api_data),
   105→            )
   106→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   107→                for record in api_data:
   108→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   109→            logger.info("UnslothBridge: dataset written to %s", out)
   110→            return out
   111→
   112→        # Fallback: copy local finetune_journeys.jsonl
   113→        local_source = Path("data/training/finetune_journeys.jsonl")
   114→        if local_source.exists():
   115→            logger.info(
   116→                "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unavailable; "
   117→                "copying %s → %s",
   118→                local_source,
   119→                out,
   120→            )
   121→            records = _read_jsonl(local_source)
   122→            # Filter records loosely by vault_query in instruction/output text
   123→            if vault_query:
   124→                query_lower = vault_query.lower()
   125→                filtered = [
   126→                    r for r in records
   127→                    if query_lower in str(r.get("instruction", "")).lower()
   128→                    or query_lower in str(r.get("output", "")).lower()
   129→                ]
   130→                # If filter is too aggressive, keep all records
   131→                if not filtered:
   132→                    filtered = records
   133→            else:
   134→                filtered = records
   135→
   136→            with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   137→                for record in filtered:
   138→                    fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
   139→            logger.info(
   140→                "UnslothBridge: fallback dataset written (%d records) to %s",
   141→                len(filtered),
   142→                out,
   143→            )
   144→            return out
   145→
   146→        # Last resort: empty dataset with a warning
   147→        logger.warning(
   148→            "UnslothBridge: no data source available (API down, no local JSONL). "
   149→            "Writing empty dataset to %s",
   150→            out,
   151→        )
   152→        out.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
   153→        return out
   154→
   155→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   156→    # QLoRA training (Phase 2 — not yet available on AMD)
   157→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   158→
   159→    async def train_qlora(
   160→        self,
   161→        dataset: Path,
   162→        model: str,
   163→        config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   164→    ) -> Path | None:
   165→        """Attempt QLoRA training via Unsloth Studio.
   166→
   167→        Returns None when AMD training is not yet supported (current state).
   168→        Phase 2 will implement full QLoRA when Unsloth ships AMD ROCm support.
   169→
   170→        Parameters
   171→        ----------
   172→        dataset : Path
   173→            Path to the JSONL training dataset (e.g., from create_dataset_from_vault).
   174→        model : str
   175→            Base model identifier (e.g., "qwen3.5:9b").
   176→        config : dict, optional
   177→            Additional training configuration forwarded to the Unsloth API.
   178→
   179→        Returns
   180→        -------
   181→        Path | None
   182→            Path to the trained model checkpoint, or None if training is
   183→            unavailable on the current hardware.
   184→        """
   185→        if not self.is_training_available():
   186→            logger.info(
   187→                "UnslothBridge.train_qlora: AMD QLoRA not available yet (Phase 2). "
   188→                "Falling back to LocalFinetuner for %s.",
   189→                model,
   190→            )
   191→            return await self._llamafactory_fallback(dataset, model, config)
   192→
   193→        # Phase 2 placeholder — implement when AMD Unsloth support ships
   194→        raise NotImplementedError(
   195→            "Unsloth QLoRA training on AMD is not yet implemented. "
   196→            "Set is_training_available() → True once AMD support ships."
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→    # Private helpers
   201→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→    async def _call_data_recipes_api(
   204→        self, vault_query: str
   205→    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:
   206→        """Call Unsloth Studio Data Recipes endpoint.
   207→
   208→        Returns the parsed list of records on success, or None on any failure.
   209→        """
   210→        url = f"{self._studio_url}{_RECIPES_ENDPOINT}"
   211→        headers: dict[str, str] = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   212→        if self._api_key:
   213→            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self._api_key}"
   214→
   215→        payload = {"query": vault_query, "format": "jsonl"}
   216→
   217→        try:
   218→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   219→                async with session.post(
   220→                    url,
   221→                    json=payload,
   222→                    headers=headers,
   223→                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=30.0),
   224→                ) as resp:
   225→                    if resp.status == 200:
   226→                        data = await resp.json()
   227→                        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = data.get("records", [])
   228→                        return records
   229→                    logger.debug(
   230→                        "UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API returned HTTP %d — using fallback",
   231→                        resp.status,
   232→                    )
   233→                    return None
   234→        except aiohttp.ClientError as exc:
   235→            logger.debug("UnslothBridge: Data Recipes API unreachable: %s", exc)
   236→            return None
   237→
   238→    async def _llamafactory_fallback(
   239→        self,
   240→        dataset: Path,
   241→        model: str,
   242→        config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   243→    ) -> Path | None:
   244→        """Generate a llamafactory training config as a Phase 2 fallback."""
   245→        import asyncio
   246→
   247→        try:
   248→            from cohezion.flume.local_finetune_pipeline import LocalFinetuner
   249→
   250→            _MODEL_KEY_MAP = {
   251→                "qwen3.5:9b": "qwen3.5",
   252→                "qwen3.5": "qwen3.5",
   253→                "phi4": "phi4",
   254→                "phi4-mini-reasoning:latest": "phi4",
   255→                "phi3:mini": "qwen3-4b",
   256→                "qwen3-4b": "qwen3-4b",
   257→                "gemma3": "gemma3",
   258→            }
   259→            base_key = _MODEL_KEY_MAP.get(model, "qwen3.5")
   260→            epochs = int((config or {}).get("epochs", 3))
   261→
   262→            finetuner = LocalFinetuner(base_model=base_key, output_name="cohezion_unsloth_phase2")
   263→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   264→            config_path: Path = await loop.run_in_executor(
   265→                None,
   266→                lambda: finetuner.run_qlora_training(epochs=epochs),
   267→            )
   268→            logger.info(
   269→                "UnslothBridge: llamafactory fallback config written to %s", config_path
   270→            )
   271→            return config_path
   272→        except Exception as exc:
   273→            logger.error("UnslothBridge._llamafactory_fallback failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   274→            return None
   275→
   276→
   277→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→# Module-level helpers
   279→# ------------------------------------------------------------------
   280→
   281→
   282→def _read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   283→    """Read a JSONL file and return a list of parsed dicts. Skips invalid lines."""
   284→    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   285→    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   286→        for lineno, line in enumerate(fh, start=1):
   287→            line = line.strip()
   288→            if not line:
   289→                continue
   290→            try:
   291→                records.append(json.loads(line))
   292→            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   293→                logger.warning(
   294→                    "_read_jsonl: skipping invalid JSON at line %d in %s: %s",
   295→                    lineno,
   296→                    path,
   297→                    exc,
   298→                )
   299→    return records
   300→
