     1→"""CohezionWorkflow: wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet-compatible workflow.
     2→
     3→AgentJet calls run(task) which executes via CompoundExecutor and returns the
     4→output alongside a reward metadata dict containing phi_score, skill_name, and
     5→coherence. The phi_score is derived from JourneyTracker trajectory analysis.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import asyncio
    11→import logging
    12→from typing import Any
    13→
    14→from cohezion.compound.executor import CompoundExecutor
    15→from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→class CohezionWorkflow:
    22→    """Wraps CompoundExecutor as an AgentJet Workflow.
    23→
    24→    AgentJet calls ``run(task)`` which delegates to CompoundExecutor and
    25→    tracks the execution trajectory via JourneyTracker to produce a phi_score
    26→    reward signal.
    27→
    28→    Parameters
    29→    ----------
    30→    executor : CompoundExecutor
    31→        Configured compound executor to delegate task execution to.
    32→    tracker : JourneyTracker, optional
    33→        Journey tracker for phi_score computation. A default instance is
    34→        created if not provided.
    35→    default_operation_type : str
    36→        Default operation type used when task dict omits ``operation_type``.
    37→        Must be one of: generate, analyze, search, transform, persist.
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    def __init__(
    41→        self,
    42→        executor: CompoundExecutor,
    43→        tracker: JourneyTracker | None = None,
    44→        default_operation_type: str = "generate",
    45→    ) -> None:
    46→        self._executor = executor
    47→        self._tracker = tracker if tracker is not None else JourneyTracker()
    48→        self._default_operation_type = default_operation_type
    49→
    50→    async def run(self, task: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    51→        """Execute a task and return output with phi_score reward metadata.
    52→
    53→        Parameters
    54→        ----------
    55→        task : dict
    56→            Task specification. Expected keys:
    57→
    58→            * ``description`` (str, required) — human-readable task description
    59→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill to apply (default: ``"general"``)
    60→            * ``operation_type`` (str) — operation type (default: ``"generate"``)
    61→            * ``project`` (str) — vault project scope (default: ``"cohezion"``)
    62→            * Any additional keys are forwarded as task context.
    63→
    64→        Returns
    65→        -------
    66→        tuple[str, dict]
    67→            ``(output, metadata)`` where metadata contains:
    68→
    69→            * ``phi_score`` (float) — trajectory quality [0.0, 1.0]
    70→            * ``skill_name`` (str) — skill used for execution
    71→            * ``coherence`` (float) — execution coherence [0.0, 1.0]
    72→            * ``success`` (bool) — whether execution succeeded
    73→            * ``duration_seconds`` (float) — wall-clock execution time
    74→        """
    75→        description: str = task.get("description", "")
    76→        skill_name: str = task.get("skill_name", "general")
    77→        operation_type: str = task.get("operation_type", self._default_operation_type)
    78→        project: str = task.get("project", "cohezion")
    79→
    80→        if not description:
    81→            logger.warning("CohezionWorkflow.run() called with empty task description")
    82→
    83→        # Build a synchronous execute function that captures task context.
    84→        # CompoundExecutor.execute_task is synchronous; it calls execute_fn
    85→        # internally in a sync context.
    86→        def _execute_fn() -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    87→            output_text = description  # Minimal fallback — executor enriches this.
    88→            exec_metrics: dict[str, Any] = {"coherence": 0.5}
    89→            return output_text, exec_metrics
    90→
    91→        logger.info(
    92→            "CohezionWorkflow.run: skill=%s operation=%s description=%r",
    93→            skill_name,
    94→            operation_type,
    95→            description[:80],
    96→        )
    97→
    98→        # Execute via CompoundExecutor (synchronous call wrapped in async context)
    99→        try:
   100→            result = await asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
   101→                None,
   102→                lambda: self._executor.execute_task(
   103→                    task_description=description,
   104→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   105→                    operation_type=operation_type,
   106→                    execute_fn=_execute_fn,
   107→                    project=project,
   108→                ),
   109→            )
   110→        except Exception as exc:
   111→            logger.error("CohezionWorkflow execution failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   112→            return (
   113→                f"[error] {exc}",
   114→                {
   115→                    "phi_score": 0.0,
   116→                    "skill_name": skill_name,
   117→                    "coherence": 0.0,
   118→                    "success": False,
   119→                    "duration_seconds": 0.0,
   120→                },
   121→            )
   122→
   123→        # Track execution trajectory to compute phi_score
   124→        try:
   125→            trajectory_point = self._tracker.track_execution(
   126→                execution_result=result,
   127→                task_description=description,
   128→                operation_type=operation_type,
   129→            )
   130→            phi_score: float = float(trajectory_point.metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0))
   131→            coherence: float = float(trajectory_point.coherence)
   132→        except Exception as exc:
   133→            logger.warning(
   134→                "Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): %s", exc, exc_info=True
   135→            )
   136→            phi_score = float(result.metrics.get("coherence", 0.0))
   137→            coherence = phi_score
   138→
   139→        metadata: dict[str, Any] = {
   140→            "phi_score": phi_score,
   141→            "skill_name": skill_name,
   142→            "coherence": coherence,
   143→            "success": result.success,
   144→            "duration_seconds": result.duration_seconds,
   145→        }
   146→
   147→        logger.debug(
   148→            "CohezionWorkflow.run complete: phi=%.3f coherence=%.3f success=%s",
   149→            phi_score,
   150→            coherence,
   151→            result.success,
   152→        )
   153→
   154→        return result.output, metadata
   155→

     1→"""Gemini Embedding 2 integration with local FLUME VAE fallback.
     2→
     3→Models: GeminiEmbeddingModel (cloud + SurrealDB cache), FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel (local 256D).
     4→EmbeddingDistiller: knowledge distillation stub (Phase 2).
     5→EmbeddingOrchestrator: context-aware routing.
     6→"""
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import hashlib
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass
    14→from enum import Enum, auto
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Protocol, runtime_checkable
    17→
    18→import numpy as np
    19→
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Data types
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass
    30→class EmbeddingResult:
    31→    """Result of an embedding operation."""
    32→
    33→    vector: np.ndarray      # The embedding vector
    34→    model: str              # Which model produced it
    35→    cached: bool = False    # Whether this came from cache
    36→    dimension: int = 0      # Vector dimension (set from vector.shape[0])
    37→
    38→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    39→        self.dimension = len(self.vector)
    40→
    41→
    42→class EmbeddingContext(Enum):
    43→    """Routing context for embedding requests."""
    44→
    45→    VAULT_INDEXING = auto()   # Offline, one-time — use Gemini cloud
    46→    RUNTIME = auto()           # Online hot path — use local FLUME VAE
    47→    TRAINING_REWARD = auto()  # Offline for distillation — use Gemini cloud
    48→    FAST_ROUTING = auto()     # Hot path — use hash fallback
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# Protocol
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→
    56→@runtime_checkable
    57→class EmbeddingModel(Protocol):
    58→    """Protocol for embedding models — sync and async encode."""
    59→
    60→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult: ...
    61→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult: ...
    62→
    63→
    64→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→# FLUME VAE local model
    66→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67→
    68→
    69→class FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel:
    70→    """Local FLUME VAE embedding (256D). Used for runtime inference."""
    71→
    72→    DIMENSION = 256
    73→
    74→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    75→        self._encoder = None  # Lazy load
    76→
    77→    def _get_encoder(self):  # type: ignore[return]
    78→        if self._encoder is None:
    79→            from cohezion.flume.vae_encoder import get_encoder
    80→            self._encoder = get_encoder()
    81→        return self._encoder
    82→
    83→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
    84→        """Encode text using FLUME VAE (runs in thread to avoid blocking loop)."""
    85→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
    86→        vector = await loop.run_in_executor(None, self._encode_blocking, text)
    87→        return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="flume-vae-256d")
    88→
    89→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
    90→        """Synchronous encode using FLUME VAE."""
    91→        vector = self._encode_blocking(text)
    92→        return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="flume-vae-256d")
    93→
    94→    def _encode_blocking(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
    95→        try:
    96→            encoder = self._get_encoder()
    97→            return encoder.encode(text)
    98→        except Exception as exc:
    99→            logger.warning("FLUME VAE encode failed, using hash fallback: %s", exc)
   100→            return self._hash_encode(text)
   101→
   102→    @staticmethod
   103→    def _hash_encode(text: str) -> np.ndarray:
   104→        """Deterministic 256D hash fallback."""
   105→        h = hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).digest()
   106→        embedding = np.array([h[i % len(h)] / 255.0 for i in range(256)], dtype=np.float32)
   107→        norm = np.linalg.norm(embedding)
   108→        if norm > 0:
   109→            embedding /= norm
   110→        return embedding
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Gemini cloud model
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→
   118→class GeminiEmbeddingModel:
   119→    """Gemini Embedding 2 with content-hash SurrealDB cache and FLUME VAE fallback."""
   120→
   121→    GEMINI_API_URL = (
   122→        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models"
   123→        "/text-embedding-004:embedContent"
   124→    )
   125→    DIMENSION = 768  # Gemini Embedding 2 output dimension
   126→    _CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT = 3
   127→
   128→    def __init__(
   129→        self,
   130→        api_key: str | None = None,
   131→        fallback: EmbeddingModel | None = None,
   132→        surreal_url: str = "ws://localhost:8000",
   133→    ) -> None:
   134→        self._api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")
   135→        self._fallback = fallback or FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel()
   136→        self._surreal_url = surreal_url
   137→        self._fail_count = 0  # Simple circuit-breaker counter
   138→        self._surreal: object | None = None  # Lazy SurrealDB handle
   139→
   140→    # --- Public interface ---------------------------------------------------
   141→
   142→    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
   143→        """Encode with cache-first strategy."""
   144→        key = self._content_hash(text)
   145→
   146→        # 1. Cache lookup
   147→        cached_vector = await self._cache_lookup(key)
   148→        if cached_vector is not None:
   149→            return EmbeddingResult(vector=cached_vector, model="gemini-embedding-2", cached=True)
   150→
   151→        # 2. Circuit breaker: skip API if too many recent failures
   152→        if self._fail_count >= self._CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT:
   153→            logger.warning(
   154→                "Gemini circuit open (%d failures), routing to fallback", self._fail_count
   155→            )
   156→            return await self._fallback.encode(text)
   157→
   158→        # 3. Call Gemini API
   159→        try:
   160→            vector = await self._call_gemini_api(text)
   161→            self._fail_count = 0  # Reset on success
   162→            await self._cache_store(key, vector)
   163→            return EmbeddingResult(vector=vector, model="gemini-embedding-2")
   164→        except Exception as exc:
   165→            self._fail_count += 1
   166→            logger.warning(
   167→                "Gemini API error (%d/%d): %s — falling back to FLUME VAE",
   168→                self._fail_count,
   169→                self._CIRCUIT_FAIL_LIMIT,
   170→                exc,
   171→            )
   172→            return await self._fallback.encode(text)
   173→
   174→    def encode_sync(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:
   175→        """Synchronous wrapper for async encode."""
   176→        try:
   177→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   178→            if loop.is_running():
   179→                # Called from within an async context — run in thread
   180→                import concurrent.futures
   181→                with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
   182→                    future = pool.submit(asyncio.run, self.encode(text))
   183→                    return future.result()
   184→            return loop.run_until_complete(self.encode(text))
   185→        except RuntimeError:
   186→            return asyncio.run(self.encode(text))
   187→
   188→    # --- Internal helpers ---------------------------------------------------
   189→
   190→    @staticmethod
   191→    def _content_hash(text: str) -> str:
   192→        return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
   193→
   194→    async def _call_gemini_api(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
   195→        """Call Gemini Embedding 2 API. Raises on any error."""
   196→        try:
   197→            import aiohttp
   198→        except ImportError as exc:
   199→            raise RuntimeError("aiohttp required for Gemini API calls") from exc
   200→
   201→        if not self._api_key:
   202→            raise ValueError("GEMINI_API_KEY not set")
   203→
   204→        url = f"{self.GEMINI_API_URL}?key={self._api_key}"
   205→        payload = {"model": "models/text-embedding-004", "content": {"parts": [{"text": text}]}}
   206→
   207→        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   208→            async with session.post(url, json=payload, timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10)) as resp:
   209→                if resp.status != 200:
   210→                    body = await resp.text()
   211→                    raise RuntimeError(f"Gemini API {resp.status}: {body[:200]}")
   212→                data = await resp.json()
   213→
   214→        values = data["embedding"]["values"]
   215→        vector = np.array(values, dtype=np.float32)
   216→        norm = np.linalg.norm(vector)
   217→        if norm > 0:
   218→            vector /= norm
   219→        return vector
   220→
   221→    async def _cache_lookup(self, key: str) -> np.ndarray | None:
   222→        """Check SurrealDB for a cached embedding vector."""
   223→        try:
   224→            import aiohttp
   225→
   226→            url = f"http://localhost:8000/sql"
   227→            query = f"SELECT vector FROM embedding_cache WHERE content_hash = '{key}' LIMIT 1;"
   228→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   229→                async with session.post(
   230→                    url,
   231→                    data=query,
   232→                    headers={"Accept": "application/json", "NS": "cohezion", "DB": "embeddings"},
   233→                    auth=aiohttp.BasicAuth("root", "root"),
   234→                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=2),
   235→                ) as resp:
   236→                    if resp.status != 200:
   237→                        return None
   238→                    result = await resp.json()
   239→                    rows = result[0].get("result", []) if result else []
   240→                    if rows and rows[0].get("vector"):
   241→                        return np.array(rows[0]["vector"], dtype=np.float32)
   242→        except Exception as exc:
   243→            logger.debug("Cache lookup skipped: %s", exc)
   244→        return None
   245→
   246→    async def _cache_store(self, key: str, vector: np.ndarray) -> None:
   247→        """Store embedding vector in SurrealDB cache."""
   248→        try:
   249→            import aiohttp
   250→
   251→            vector_list = vector.tolist()
   252→            query = (
   253→                f"CREATE embedding_cache CONTENT {{"
   254→                f"content_hash: '{key}', vector: {vector_list}"
   255→                f"}};"
   256→            )
   257→            url = "http://localhost:8000/sql"
   258→            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
   259→                async with session.post(
   260→                    url,
   261→                    data=query,
   262→                    headers={"Accept": "application/json", "NS": "cohezion", "DB": "embeddings"},
   263→                    auth=aiohttp.BasicAuth("root", "root"),
   264→                    timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=2),
   265→                ) as resp:
   266→                    if resp.status != 200:
   267→                        logger.debug("Cache store returned %d", resp.status)
   268→        except Exception as exc:
   269→            logger.debug("Cache store skipped (non-critical): %s", exc)
   270→
   271→
   272→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   273→# Distiller
   274→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   275→
   276→
   277→class EmbeddingDistiller:
   278→    """Distills Gemini teacher embeddings into local FLUME VAE student (Phase 2 stub)."""
   279→
   280→    def __init__(
   281→        self,
   282→        teacher: GeminiEmbeddingModel,
   283→        student_vae_path: Path | None = None,
   284→    ) -> None:
   285→        self._teacher = teacher
   286→        self._student_vae_path = student_vae_path
   287→
   288→    async def distill(
   289→        self,
   290→        corpus: list[str],
   291→        epochs: int = 10,
   292→        output_path: Path | None = None,
   293→    ) -> Path:
   294→        """Fine-tune FLUME VAE to minimize distance to Gemini vectors (Phase 2 stub)."""
   295→        logger.warning(
   296→            "EmbeddingDistiller.distill() is a Phase 2 stub. "
   297→            "corpus=%d texts, epochs=%d. Full FLUME VAE training API not yet implemented.",
   298→            len(corpus),
   299→            epochs,
   300→        )
   301→        return output_path or Path("")
   302→
   303→
   304→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→# Orchestrator
   306→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   307→
   308→
   309→class EmbeddingOrchestrator:
   310→    """Routes embedding requests to the right model based on context.
   311→
   312→    VAULT_INDEXING / TRAINING_REWARD -> Gemini cloud.
   313→    RUNTIME / FAST_ROUTING           -> FLUME VAE local.
   314→    """
   315→
   316→    def __init__(
   317→        self,
   318→        gemini_model: GeminiEmbeddingModel | None = None,
   319→        flume_model: FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel | None = None,
   320→    ) -> None:
   321→        self._gemini = gemini_model or GeminiEmbeddingModel()
   322→        self._flume = flume_model or FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel()
   323→
   324→    def get_model(self, context: EmbeddingContext) -> EmbeddingModel:
   325→        """Return the appropriate model for the given context."""
   326→        if context in (EmbeddingContext.VAULT_INDEXING, EmbeddingContext.TRAINING_REWARD):
   327→            return self._gemini
   328→        # RUNTIME and FAST_ROUTING both use local FLUME VAE
   329→        return self._flume
   330→
   331→    async def encode(
   332→        self,
   333→        text: str,
   334→        context: EmbeddingContext = EmbeddingContext.RUNTIME,
   335→    ) -> EmbeddingResult:
   336→        """Encode text using the model appropriate for the given context."""
   337→        model = self.get_model(context)
   338→        return await model.encode(text)
   339→

Now I have a complete picture. The service file still has hardcoded paths (C3), trainer.py has 5 remaining fixes, and task_reader/unsloth_bridge/workflow/embeddings/api all need their fixes too. Plus the test still expects `pytest.raises` instead of the new `TrainingResult(success=False)` return.

Let me apply all remaining fixes in parallel.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `asyncio.get_event_loop()` inside a coroutine is deprecated in Python 3.10+ because it can silently return a **different** event loop than the one running the coroutine. `get_running_loop()` correctly raises `RuntimeError` if there's no running loop, making bugs visible instead of silent.
- The systemd `%h` specifier is resolved at unit **load time** from `/etc/passwd` — making it portable without hardcoding usernames into service files.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

61/61 passing. The fixes are all verified. Now let me commit all the code review fixes:

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Collecting all 11 bug fixes into a single commit makes the code review history clean — the reviewer can clearly see "review feedback → one cohesive fix commit," rather than 11 micro-commits that muddy the blame history.
- The `training_lock` local variable pattern (vs `self._training_lock`) is a subtle but important "temporal coupling" fix: state that only exists during `train()` shouldn't leak into the object's permanent state, which could cause silent bugs across async calls or test isolation issues.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Meanwhile\ncloud API costs accumulate on Claude Code / Gemini CLI every session.\n\nThe goal is a closed **Cohezion Autonomous Learning Loop (CALL)**: Cohezion's own runtime\nexperiences become the training signal, AgentJet provides the RL loop, Unsloth provides the\nfast fine-tuning backend, and improved local models route away from cloud APIs for familiar\ntasks. Ollama settings + context compression must be first-class.\n- AMD training gap: Unsloth Studio AMD training is \"coming soon\" — Phase 1 uses existing\n  llama.cpp/llamafactory path. function\n  PhiScoreJudger    — phi_score → scalar reward (phi×2-1 maps [0.5,1]→[0,1])\n  JourneyTaskReader — reads existing JSONL (no new data pipeline)\n  AgentJetTrainer   — orchestrates RL loop; manages model unload before training\n  ContextOptimizer  — per-model Ollama settings (num_ctx, flash_attn, rope_scaling)\n       │\n       ▼\nTRAINING BACKEND (phased)\n  Phase 1 (now):    llamafactory + llama.cpp via LocalFinetuner (already exists)\n  Phase 2 (soon):   Unsloth Studio QLoRA when AMD training ships\n  Both phases:      Unsloth Data Recipes (available NOW) for OllamaContextManager ensures models loaded with correct context settings\n```\n\n---\n\n## Model Registry (updated)\n\nPrimary fine-tune targets (in priority order for Phase 1):\n\n| Model | Size | QLoRA RAM | Domain | Priority |\n|-------|------|-----------|--------|----------|\n| qwen3.5:9b | 9B | ~8GB train | general, fast iteration | 1st |\n| nemotron-3-nano:30b | 30B | ~24GB train | agentic tasks | 2nd |\n| openai/gpt-oss-20b | 22B | ~16GB train | highest quality/param | 3rd |\n| qwen3-coder-next | 80B | ~64GB train | coding (when headroom allows) | 4th |\n\nMoE inference models (load via Ollama, NOT fine-tune targets initially):\n- nemotron-3-super:120b — 12B active params, multi-agent orchestration\n- glm-5 — 40B active params, reasoning + agentic\n- minimax-m2.7 — brand new \"\"\"Wraps CompoundExecutor as AgentJet Workflow.\n    AgentJet calls run(task) → returns output + phi_score as reward signal.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, executor: CompoundExecutor): ...\n Each session's\n`model_pool_manager.py` is a singleton that sees only its own allocations. A training job\ncan OOM the machine while a separate session is serving inference.\n\n**The solution**: A lightweight persistent resource daemon that acts as the platform's\nmemory arbiter — any session must check in before loading models or starting training.\n\n### Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│           RESOURCE DAEMON (persistent)              │\n│  Polls Ollama /api/ps every 30s                     │\n│  Tracks: loaded_models, reserved_gb, training_locks │\n│  Backend: SurrealDB (already running at ws://8000)  │\n│  API: HTTP :8765 (lightweight, always-on)           │\n└───────────┬────────────────────────┬────────────────┘\n            │ reads                  │ reads/writes\n  ┌─────────▼──────┐       ┌────────▼────────────┐\n  │ Claude Code    │       │ Training Job         │\n  │ Session A      │       │ (AgentJetTrainer)    │\n  │ ResourceClient │       │ ResourceClient       │\n  └────────────────┘       └─────────────────────┘\n            │\n  ┌─────────▼──────┐\n  │ Claude Code    │\n  │ Session B      │\n  │ ResourceClient │\n  └────────────────┘\n```\n\n### New Component: src/cohezion/platform/resource_manager.py\n\n```python\nclass ResourceDaemon:\n    \"\"\"Persistent platform resource arbiter.\n\n    Runs as background process between sessions.\n    Polls Ollama /api/ps for ground-truth model state.\n    Manages training locks to prevent concurrent training OOM.\n\n    Start: uv run python -m cohezion.platform.resource_manager\n    Or via systemd: cohezion-resources.service\n    \"\"\"\n    POLL_INTERVAL_S = 30         # Ollama /api/ps poll frequency\n    PORT = 8765                  # Local HTTP API port\n    TRAINING_LOCK_TTL_S = 7200   # Max training job reservation = 2 hours\n\n    async def poll_ollama_state(self) -> OllamaState:\n        \"\"\"Query /api/ps for actual loaded models + memory usage.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def get_available_memory_gb(self) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Total unified memory - Ollama usage - OS overhead - 10GB safety buffer.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def request_training_lock(\n        self, session_id: str, model: str, required_gb: float\n    ) -> TrainingLock | None:\n        \"\"\"Exclusive lock for training. Returns None if insufficient memory.\n        Locks are stored in SurrealDB with TTL for crash recovery.\n        \"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def release_training_lock(self, lock_id: str) -> None: ...\n\n    async def register_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Track active sessions for cleanup on death.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def cleanup_dead_sessions(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Release locks from sessions that died without cleanup.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n\nclass ResourceClient:\n    \"\"\"Lightweight client for any session to consult the ResourceDaemon.\n\n    Falls back gracefully if daemon not running (single-session mode).\n    All sessions MUST use this before loading large models or training.\n    \"\"\"\n    DAEMON_URL = \"http://localhost:8765\"\n\n    async def check_memory(self) -> PlatformMemoryState:\n        \"\"\"Get current memory state across all sessions.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def acquire_training_lock(\n        self, model: str, required_gb: float, timeout_s: float = 30.0\n    ) -> TrainingLock | None:\n        \"\"\"Request exclusive training access. Blocks until available or timeout.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    async def can_load_model(self, model_name: str, size_gb: float) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if loading a model is safe given all sessions' current usage.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    def is_daemon_running(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Silent check — False = fall back to local-only mode.\"\"\"\n        ...\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PlatformMemoryState:\n    total_gb: float              # 128 for Ryzen AI MAX+ 395\n    ollama_used_gb: float        # From /api/ps (all sessions combined)\n    system_reserved_gb: float    # OS + non-Ollama processes\n    training_reserved_gb: float  # Active training lock reservations\n    available_gb: float          # total - ollama_used - system_reserved - training_reserved\n    safety_buffer_gb: float = 10.0  # Never let available drop below this\n    loaded_models: list[str]     # All models currently loaded across ALL sessions\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TrainingLock:\n    lock_id: str\n    session_id: str\n    model: str\n    reserved_gb: float\n    acquired_at: float\n    expires_at: float            # TTL for crash recovery\n```\n\n### Session Usage Pattern\n\nEvery new Claude Code / Gemini CLI session that uses Cohezion:\n\n```python\n# On session start (in CompoundExecutor or via hook)\nclient = ResourceClient()\nawait client.register_session(session_id=os.environ[\"COHEZION_SESSION_ID\"])\n\n# Before any model load\nif not await client.can_load_model(\"qwen3-coder-next\", size_gb=45.0):\n    logger.warning(\"Insufficient platform memory, falling back to nemotron-3-nano:30b\")\n\n# Before training (in AgentJetTrainer)\nlock = await client.acquire_training_lock(\"qwen3.5:9b\", required_gb=27.0)\nif lock is None:\n    raise ResourceUnavailableError(\"Training blocked: another session holds training lock\")\ntry:\n    await self._run_training(...)\nfinally:\n    await client.release_training_lock(lock.lock_id)\n```\n\n### Usage Pattern Learning\n\nThe daemon tracks which models each session uses and how long, building a usage histogram\nstored in SurrealDB. This feeds back into `model_pool_config.py` tier assignments:\n\n```python\n# Weekly: daemon promotes/demotes models based on actual cross-session usage\n# \"nemotron-3-nano is used by 80% of sessions for >30min → promote to WARM\"\n# \"qwen3-coder-next last used 14 days ago → demote to COLD\"\nclass TierOptimizer:\n    def recommend_tier_changes(self, usage_history: list[SessionRecord]) -> list[TierChange]:\n        \"\"\"Data-driven tier assignment based on actual cross-session usage.\"\"\"\n        ...\n```\n\n### Files to Add\n\n```\nsrc/cohezion/platform/\n├── __init__.py\n├── resource_manager.py    (ResourceDaemon, ResourceClient, PlatformMemoryState, TrainingLock)\n├── tier_optimizer.py      (TierOptimizer — usage-driven tier assignments)\n└── session_tracker.py     (SessionRecord, cross-session usage histogram)\n\nscripts/\n├── start_resource_daemon.sh   (launch daemon, check if already running)\n└── cohezion-resources.service (systemd unit for persistent daemon)\n\ntests/platform/\n├── __init__.py\n├── test_resource_manager.py   (mock Ollama /api/ps, mock SurrealDB)\n└── test_tier_optimizer.py\n```\n\n### Integration into AgentJetTrainer\n\n`AgentJetTrainer._safety_check()` becomes:\n\n```python\nasync def _safety_check(self, model: str, required_gb: float) -> None:\n    client = ResourceClient()\n    if client.is_daemon_running():\n        lock = await client.acquire_training_lock(model, required_gb, timeout_s=30.0)\n        if lock is None:\n            raise ResourceUnavailableError(\n                f\"Cannot train {model}: platform has insufficient memory \"\n                f\"or another session holds the training lock\"\n            )\n        self._training_lock = lock  # Released in finally block of train()\n    else:\n        # Fallback: local-only check (single-session safety)\n        state = await OllamaContextManager().get_available_memory_gb()\n        if state < required_gb * 1.2:\n            raise OOMRiskError(f\"OOM risk: need {required_gb}GB, have {state}GB\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## Gemini Embedding 2 — Orchestration Anchor\n\nGemini Embedding 2 (March 10, 2026) is Google's first natively multimodal embedding model:\nmaps text, images, video, audio, and documents into ONE unified embedding space.\n\nThis upgrades Cohezion's orchestration from keyword matching → semantic concept matching.\n\n### Why It Matters\n\n`SemanticCache` already has a plug-and-play `EmbeddingModel` interface. The current\n`FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel` uses a 256D SHA-256 hash projection — not truly semantic. Gemini\nEmbedding 2 slots directly into the same interface as a higher-quality tier.\n\n### Three Uses (in priority order)\n\n**1. Vault Indexing (offline, one-time cost)**\nEmbed every vault note, decision, pattern, and PRIME skill using Gemini Embedding 2.\nStore vectors locally in SurrealDB. Never pay again for the same content.\nThis makes `vault_find_relevant_context()` orders of magnitude more accurate.\n\n**2. FLUME VAE Distillation (calibration, not runtime)**\nUse Gemini Embedding 2 vectors as teacher signal to train the FLUME VAE.\nThe VAE learns to approximate Gemini's embedding quality locally.\nAfter calibration: near-cloud quality at zero API cost per runtime query.\nThis is knowledge distillation for embeddings — same principle as DistilBERT vs BERT.\n\n**3. Cross-Modal Orchestration (when cloud available)**\nTask descriptions, code snippets, trajectory data, and skill definitions all embedded\ninto the same space. SmartRouter semantic matching across modalities, not just text.\n\n### New Component: src/cohezion/agentjet/embeddings.py\n\n```python\nclass GeminiEmbeddingModel(EmbeddingModel):\n    \"\"\"Cloud Gemini Embedding 2 — uses Gemini API, caches locally.\n\n    Never embeds the same content twice. Stores in SurrealDB with content hash key.\n    Falls back to FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel when cloud unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, cache_backend: SurrealClient, fallback: EmbeddingModel): ...\n    async def encode(self, text: str) -> EmbeddingResult:\n        key = sha256(text)\n        if cached := await self.cache_backend.get(key): return cached\n        result = await self._call_gemini_api(text)\n        await self.cache_backend.store(key, result)\n        return result\n\nclass EmbeddingDistiller:\n    \"\"\"Distills Gemini Embedding 2 quality into local FLUME VAE.\n\n    Generates Gemini embeddings for vault corpus, then fine-tunes FLUME VAE\n    to minimize distance to Gemini vectors. Result: local embeddings that\n    approximate cloud quality without runtime API calls.\n    \"\"\"\n    def distill(self, vault_corpus: list[str], epochs: int = 10) -> Path: ...\n    # Outputs calibrated FLUME VAE checkpoint\n\nclass EmbeddingOrchestrator:\n    \"\"\"Selects embedding model based on context and availability.\n\n    Routing table:\n    - Vault indexing (offline):    GeminiEmbeddingModel (cloud, cached)\n    - Runtime inference (online):  FlumeVAEEmbeddingModel (local, calibrated)\n    - Training reward signals:     GeminiEmbeddingModel (offline, for distillation)\n    - Fast routing (hot path):     DistilledEmbeddingModel (hash fallback, <1ms)\n    \"\"\"\n    def get_model(self, context: str) -> EmbeddingModel: ...\n```\n\n### Integration Points\n\n- `SemanticCache.__init__`: Accept `EmbeddingOrchestrator` as `embedding_model` param\n  (already supports custom `embedding_model` injection — zero interface change)\n- `TrajectorySearchEngine.find_similar_trajectories()`: Replace hash embeddings with\n  calibrated FLUME VAE (post-distillation) for better trajectory matching\n- `vault_find_relevant_context()`: Use GeminiEmbeddingModel for vault indexing pass\n- `SkillSelector.select_skills()`: Use calibrated embeddings for semantic skill matching\n\n### Cost Control\n\nGemini Embedding 2 is API-based (cloud). Usage rules:\n- ONLY call for novel content (not seen before, per SurrealDB content-hash cache)\n- NEVER call during runtime execution (FLUME VAE handles runtime)\n- Batch vault indexing runs offline (configurable schedule, not per-request)\n- Circuit breaker: if Gemini API unavailable, fall back to FLUME VAE silently\n\n---\n\n## Key Reuse Points (existing code)\n\n| Existing | Used By | How |\n|----------|---------|-----|\n| `flume/journey_finetune_pipeline.py:JourneyFinetuneConverter` | `JourneyTaskReader` | Reads its JSONL output |\n| `flume/local_finetune_pipeline.py:LocalFinetuner` | `AgentJetTrainer` | Phase 1 training backend |\n| `compound/journey_tracker.py:phi_score` | `PhiScoreJudger` | Already computed, just read |\n| `compound/executor.py:CompoundExecutor` | `CohezionWorkflow` | Wrapped as AgentJet rollout |\n| `reliability/context_harness.py:ContextHarness` | `OllamaContextManager` | Extended for per-model profiles |\n| `swarm/model_pool_config.py:TierConfig` | `OllamaContextManager` | `memory_pressure_threshold` |\n| `swarm/dynamic_model_router.py` | `AgentJetTrainer` | Concurrency limit enforcement |\n| `compound/degradation_detector.py` | `AgentJetTrainer._safety_check` | Memory pressure alerts |\n| `persistence/surreal_client.py` | `ResourceDaemon`, `ResourceClient` | Training lock storage + TTL |\n| `swarm/model_pool_manager.py` | `ResourceDaemon` | Per-session model state (feeds daemon) |\n| `swarm/model_pool_config.py:TierConfig` | `TierOptimizer` | Reads current tiers, writes recommendations |\n",
